Apotheosis – Ascension to Godhood
Vol.23

Chapter 2201
After luring Little Yun and Fu Chu into the Nuwa Clan, Luo Zheng's mission came to an end.
Whether he could leave this world and return to the Divine Dominion would be decided after the Light of Judgment. Currently, the Nuwa Clan was responsible for improving Fu Chu's cultivation base.
During this time, Luo Zheng had also returned to the Chi Spirit Bone Tower to cultivate in seclusion.
It was conceivable that leaving this world would not be smooth sailing. Facing the warden's clan, a fierce battle was unavoidable.
Moreover, after returning to the Divine Dominion, Luo Zheng would also have to face those Saints. His motivation to improve his cultivation base was also very urgent!
Ever since the Chiyou Clan and the Nuwa Clan joined forces, the atmosphere in the clan had become more positive.
For the Aragami who were born and raised in this world, although they were not that eager to leave this world, they were also yearning to return to their home world which was further away and much larger. Under the leadership of the exiles, almost all of the Aragami's people were training hard.
…
…
The waves on the Sea of Time rolled slightly. Colorful colors rolled up, and countless fragments tumbled within.
White clouds fluttered past, and at a very special angle, the light that reflected on the Sea of Time could dye the clouds into various colors.
In the depths of the Dongfang Clan's floating island.
A pale-golden palace stood there.
The palace had just been built. Although it did not occupy a large area, it was exceptionally exquisite.
If those craftsmen who were good at refining saw this palace, they would probably be speechless.
The entire palace was forged from Ru Yun Gold. This material was normally used in the highest level of magic treasures. And on the surface of the palace, there was a layer of emerald green 'Ru Yun Jade'. This sort of jade was innately able to attract heaven and earth origin energy. Some True Divinities liked to forge Ru Yun Jade into beads and place them in their houses. Like this, they could constantly attract the origin energy from a hundred miles around.
However, this palace was completely covered in Ling Jade. It was obvious how luxurious it was.
Even the wealthy families of the floating island would not be so wasteful.
In the center of the palace, there was a box made of metal. At the top of the box, there was a brown liquid boiling, but no heat could be felt.
A burly figure appeared at the front door of the palace. It was Dongfang Chunjun.
In the Dongfang Clan, and even in front of all the Saints of the wealthy families, he was like a ruler.
Just one look was enough to make countless people tremble in fear. Of course, just one look was enough to kill countless people …
But every time he stepped into this palace, his handsome face would be dyed with a trace of malice.
Dongfang Chunjun slowly walked in front of the glass box and reached out to gently stroke it. As he continued to stroke it, the patterns on the surface of the box began to shine faintly, and the brown liquid began to boil more and more violently.
Soon, a ball of silk-like hair floated out. The hair seemed to have its own consciousness as it slowly curled and twined in all directions.
After a short while, a head slowly floated up from the box.
This head had a clear and unparalleled face. At this moment, its eyes were tightly shut and it was motionless …
Seeing this face, the malice on Dongfang Chunjun's face disappeared without a trace, and his eyes were full of love.
If anyone saw Dongfang Chunjun's expression, they would definitely sigh in their hearts. After all, such an ambitious and ruthless person had never shown his romantic side in front of others.
"Luo River …"
Dongfang Chunjun called out softly.
The eyes on the head did not open. They were still tightly shut, as if she did not hear Dongfang Chunjun's call.
"If you don't like the body I built for you, I can find a way to find your former body," Dongfang Chunjun said again.
During this period of time, Dongfang Chunjun had used all kinds of methods to help Li Luoshui find all kinds of perfect bodies …
With Dongfang Chunjun's methods, it was enough to hunt for rare things in the world.
Someone in the North Sea offered a "Pure Yin Body", someone in the Realm of Enlightenment offered a "Phoenix Body", and a flesh and blood puppet body …
Even if these bodies were not used for themselves, they would be extremely perfect for creating clones.
But Li Luoshui did not even look at them.
Dongfang Chunjun only knew that Li Luoshui despised these bodies. After all, how could mere objects from the Realm of the Gods catch her eye?
Thinking about it, the only thing that could truly satisfy her was her original body.
Hearing this, the eyes of the head finally opened slowly. A trace of surprise appeared in those extremely beautiful eyes.
"My body has been destroyed. Where did you find it?" Li Luoshui asked.
When Dongfang Chunjun saw Li Luoshui's expression, a faint smile bloomed on his face. His gaze also became deep. "Your body has been destroyed, but it was not destroyed in the Sea of Time."
"You want to enter the Sea of Time?" Li Luoshui's thin eyebrows raised slightly.
Dongfang Chunjun nodded slightly.
Li Luoshui sneered. "There are endless space-time fragments in the Sea of Time. Those fragments are all fragmented space-time. If you want to find the former me, it's tantamount to finding a needle in a haystack."
"If the pursuit is not difficult, the harvest is meaningless," Dongfang Chunjun said indifferently.
Wanting to find a specific space-time was like finding a needle in a haystack. It was still too simple.
Since the beginning of the Realm of the Gods, the Sea of Time had slowly expanded.
A period of time in every space would turn into colorful fragments and fall into this Sea of Time.
If one wanted to find the source, they would definitely be able to find it in the Sea of Time. Unfortunately, these fragments were so messy that it was impossible to sort them out …
Li Luoshui stared at Dongfang Chunjun with a slightly complicated gaze.
It was normal for such ambitious words to come from the mouth of a young man. However, it still felt strange for the First Sage of the Realm of the Gods, someone who had lived for countless Divine Epochs, to say such words.
However, in the end, she just sneered and closed her eyes.
Seeing Li Luoshui like this, Dongfang Chunjun was not angry. He allowed her head to slowly immerse in the brown liquid.
"Gurgle …"
Soon, the brown liquid began to boil again.
Then, Dongfang Chunjun laughed bitterly and immediately left the palace.
Dongfang Chunjun had been very busy during this period. On one hand, it was for Li Luoshui. On the other hand, he had to deal with all kinds of matters.
Not long after, Dongfang Chunjun came to the Green Hidden Hall of the Dongfang family's floating island.
As the patriarch of the Dongfang family, today he had to examine the new batch of outstanding disciples of the Dongfang family. These disciples were all new. Some of them had good talent and were recommended by the main family of the Fusang Divine Tree. Some of them performed extremely well in the arena.

Chapter 2202
Hundreds of True Gods stood in the Green Hidden Hall.
Almost half of them came from the floating island and were disciples of the floating island.
The other half came from the Fusang Divine Tree, which was the main branch of the Dongfang family!
Compared to other wealthy families, the Dongfang family had a much deeper foundation. The disciples recommended by the main branch of the Dongfang family could not be ignored. In other words, even if the Dongfang family did not rely on the floating island, they could still cultivate young talents comparable to the disciples of the floating island!
When Dongfang Chunjun appeared, all the disciples of the Dongfang family looked at him with reverence.
These young talents knew that the burly middle-aged man in front of them was the patriarch of the Dongfang family, and also the first Sage of the Divine Realm!
The title of the first Sage might not be so accurate.
After all, Sages would not easily fight with each other. Some Sages might even be more powerful than Dongfang Chunjun.
However, on the surface, most of the True Gods thought that Dongfang Chunjun was the strongest among the Sages!
"Greetings, Great Sage Dongfang!"
The youths in the Green Hidden Hall bowed to Dongfang Chunjun.
Dongfang Chunjun looked down at the crowd and nodded slightly. Then, he said, "The Dongfang family is able to stand at the top of the floating island with the power of the family. It is not because of me alone, but because of all the elites here. You are the future of the Dongfang family.
"According to the rules of the Dongfang family, the most outstanding disciples will be sent here every once in a while. This time, I will personally give out the rewards," Dongfang Chunjun continued.
The youths below immediately looked very interested.
Those who could stand here were those who had the most outstanding performance recently. They had either gained a lot in the forbidden area and contributed a lot to the family, or performed amazingly in the arena and had great potential …
Naturally, they were looking forward to the rewards personally given by the patriarch.
Then, on one side of the Green Hidden Hall, a Secondary Sage of the Dongfang family quietly walked to Dongfang Chunjun's side and whispered to him.
Just as he whispered, Dongfang Chunjun's gaze fell on a corner of the Green Hidden Hall.
The youths in the hall also noticed Dongfang Chunjun's gaze and looked over quietly.
In the corner stood a young man in white clothes.
Soon, Dongfang Chunjun said with a smile, "The land of the Fusang Divine Tree is full of outstanding people. It's the Dongfang family's fortune to have such an elite among the outsiders. Your name is Sky Sword, right?"
After hearing Dongfang Chunjun's words, there was a buzz in the Green Hidden Palace.
"Sky Sword? He is Sky Sword? "
"I didn't expect Sky Sword to be a disciple of my Dongfang Clan's floating island, and an outsider at that …"
"I was wondering which family it belonged to!"
They did not expect that the person in the corner was the number one among the upper true gods in the Arena of Gods. It was said that this guy was planning to challenge a Great Perfection deity above his level!
However, no one knew which wealthy family this guy belonged to.
Some people who were not in the know were even guessing if Sky Sword was that anonymous person!
Now that he had suddenly appeared on the Dongfang family's floating island in the Green Hidden Palace, the Dongfang family's disciples were naturally shocked.
These youths looked at Tian Jian with envy, jealousy, and even hatred. After all, in order to climb to the top, Tian Jian would naturally defeat some of the disciples of the Dongfang family. Some of the disciples of the Green Hidden Palace had been defeated by Tian Jian.
Sky Sword cupped his hands at Dongfang Chunjun and answered loudly, "Yes!"
"Not bad." Dongfang Chunjun showed a hint of approval. "To be able to win all the way up without a single defeat, you are considered one of the most outstanding people in the history of the Divine Era …"
Speaking of this, an undetectable haze appeared in Dongfang Chunjun's eyes.
Sky Sword had won 29 matches in a row. Even the Sages could not achieve such a record back then.
However, when it came to winning streaks, Dongfang Chunjun could not help but think of the anonymous person, Luo Zheng!
The last time in the underground world, Li Luoshui sent Luo Zheng away with great magical power.
With Dongfang Chunjun's character, he would definitely not let it go. However, he had searched almost the entire divine territory and could not find any trace of that guy. This made Dongfang Chunjun very unhappy.
His hatred for Luo Zheng was not only to eliminate the root of the problem, but also because of Luo Zheng's identity. He was Luo Xiao's son, Li Luoshui's son. How could he, Dongfang Chunjun, tolerate the existence of such an unfilial son?
As a man, no matter how magnanimous he was, he was destined to be narrow-minded in the face of such things!
"I hope you can continue to work hard and create the first winning streak record," Dongfang Chunjun said again.
Then, he reached out and gently pinched. Two rays of light, one green and one yellow, slowly spiraled from his hand and turned into two swords in a short while.
After seeing these two swords, the people below immediately began to discuss in low voices again.
"It's a pair of green and yellow swords."
"The green sword is Five Spirits, and the yellow sword is Missing Moon. The master actually gave this pair of swords to Sky Sword, an outsider!"
"That's not right. Sky Sword doesn't use dual swords. Why did he give this pair of swords?"
The green and yellow swords slowly floated in Dongfang Chunjun's hand. His gaze then fell on a woman beside Sky Sword.
This woman was wearing a tri-colored dress. Her appearance was pure and beautiful, and she exuded a different kind of vitality just by standing there.
"Dongfang Yingqing, you belong to the Heng family, right? Send my greetings to your father, Hengmin, for me. Your branch has produced a smart and beautiful girl, not bad! I'll give this pair of green and yellow swords to the two of you, "Dongfang Chunjun said with a faint smile.
This woman was Dongfang Yingqing, whom Hua Tianming had saved.
Ever since Hua Tianming saved her, Dongfang Yingqing had been following him.
Later, she actually became dependent on him. Even when she had the chance to return to the Dongfang family, she couldn't leave him.
In Dongfang Yingqing's eyes, Sky Sword was always so mysterious.
Until now, Dongfang Yingqing still couldn't see through him. Perhaps it was this mysterious aura that exuded an attraction that made her ignore everything else.
Although Dongfang Yingqing had brought a person of unknown origin into the Dongfang family, which was quite a hidden danger, and Sky Sword had to have ulterior motives for suddenly asking to join the Dongfang family. However, she still helped Sky Sword hide everything, such as the shocking fact that he could eat stone slabs. She did all this willingly.
In the eyes of outsiders, Sky Sword was able to enter the floating island because of Dongfang Yingqing. If they became divine companions in the future, Sky Sword would naturally belong to the Dongfang family.
However, Dongfang Yingqing knew that she and Sky Sword were only in a subordinate relationship.
"Children, what are you waiting for? Hurry up and thank the patriarch!" the quasi-sage urged from above.
Hua Tianming had a faint smile on his face as he cupped his hands toward Dongfang Chunjun from afar. Dongfang Yingqing also clasped her hands and bowed.
"Thank you, Great Sage Dongfang!"
"Thank you, patriarch!"

Chapter 2203
Dongfang Chunjun indeed cherished Sky Sword's talent.
He had also heard that Sky Sword had joined the Dongfang family because of Dongfang Yingqing. He had bestowed this pair of green and yellow swords to Sky Sword.
After bestowing these two swords, Dongfang Chunjun also bestowed rewards to the other outstanding disciples.
Hua Tianming put the swords into his spatial ring and stood quietly in a corner, quietly sizing up Dongfang Chunjun in front of him.
This was the first time Hua Tianming had seen his target.
If it was just based on first impressions, Hua Tianming would find it hard to dislike this handsome middle-aged man.
However, this was Hua Tianming's fate. This fate was engraved in his bones, and no matter how many twists and turns he went through, it could not be changed.
This was also the reason why Luo Xiao had created him …
Using Dongfang Yingqing to infiltrate the Dongfang family was Hua Tianming's first step.
Here, he could obtain more resources for his own cultivation. More importantly, he could gain the trust of the Dongfang family and even Dongfang Chunjun.
After Dongfang Chunjun bestowed rewards, he left with the quasi-sages of the family.
With the elders gone, the young elites in the Hidden Green Hall immediately became noisy.
These True Gods were either fighting in the Arena of Gods, cultivating in seclusion, or shuttling through forbidden areas all day long. They rarely had the chance to gather together.
They were all talented and proud people, so it was inevitable that they would have thoughts of comparison when gathered together.
Most of them had their eyes on Hua Tianming and Dongfang Yingqing. There was a hint of hostility in those eyes.
After all, this was the Dongfang family. How could the disciples of the family tolerate an outsider receiving the best reward?
Hua Tianming realized something and smiled faintly. He said to Dongfang Yingqing, "Let's go."
Dongfang Yingqing nodded and was about to leave with Hua Tianming.
This was also her first time stepping onto the Dongfang family's floating island. She didn't have much of a sense of belonging to this place. If it weren't for the Sky Sword, she might not have set foot here in her entire life, even though she was qualified to enter the floating island.
But at this moment, a voice with a slight smile sounded, "I didn't expect the Heng branch of the Dongfang family to be in such dire straits. There's no one in our family, but we actually betrayed our own woman to rope in an outsider. This is too shameful. I'm really sorry for the surname 'Dongfang', hehe …"
Hearing this, Dongfang Yingqing frowned.
The Dongfang family's roots were huge, and the internal competition was also very fierce.
Although the Heng Lineage was only a branch lineage, it also had a Secondary Saint overseeing it. It was not as embarrassing as that person said.
It was just that the person who spoke was deliberately looking for trouble, so his words were naturally very unpleasant.
Dongfang Yingqing also knew that these people weren't targeting her, but Sky Sword. However, she didn't want Sky Sword to get involved in this conflict.
"Let's go!" Dongfang Yingqing pulled Hua Tianming and was about to leave.
However, Hua Tianming stood in place and didn't move.
Instead, he turned around and smiled faintly. In a clear voice, he said, "If you want to target me, just come directly. Why bother mocking your own clansmen like this? You're making a fool of yourself by playing such tricks! "
Clap, clap, clap …
A person slowly walked out from the crowd and clapped his hands lightly. This person wore a golden crown and had a vermillion jade pendant on his body. He exuded a sense of luxury.
When everyone saw this person, they thought that there would be a good show to watch today.
This person was called Dongfang Xunfeng. He was a native of the floating island, and his father was another Sage of the Dongfang family.
Although he was extremely talented, his name had never appeared on the Pantheon Jade Wall. It was said that his father didn't allow him to enter the Arena of Gods.
"I heard that you had an extraordinary performance in the Arena of Gods. Now, it seems that you do have some guts. In that case, I, Dongfang Xunfeng, will get straight to the point. Please enlighten me." Dongfang Xunfeng's eyes flashed with a strange light.
The corners of Hua Tianming's mouth curled up slightly. He had never rejected such challenges. In other words, he wouldn't miss any opportunity to train himself.
…
…
God Realm, Sky Dragon Region.
Black Thunder Mountain Range.
Bolts of reddish-gold lightning circled the peak of the mountain range all year round.
Even if a True God walked in the Black Thunder Mountain Range, they had to be careful. If they were accidentally struck by the reddish-gold lightning in the sky, it wouldn't be fun. If they weren't careful, they might be completely destroyed.
Generally speaking, if an upper-level True God had extraordinary strength, they could walk alone.
If it was an intermediate or lower-level True God, they would have to form a group and use all kinds of methods to attract lightning, thunderbolts, and intercept lightning to carefully pass through.
Today, a lower-level True God had appeared in the Black Thunder Mountain Range!
And this lower-level True God only had a lousy sword.
"Crackle, crackle …"
A golden light flashed, and the sound of a bucket-sized reddish-gold lightning exploding was deafening.
The lower-level True God raised his head to take a look, and there was a faint look of fear on his face.
"Hey, hey, hey. Are you sure I want to enter the mountain alone? Why don't you wait? If I can join a True God's group, it'll be easier, right? "The lower-level True God had a helpless look on his face.
"Join someone else's group? Hehe, with your cultivation, they won't take a liking to you. Who would want you to join them? Moreover, we're going to look for the Thunder Mountain's secret treasure. Do you plan on sharing it with others? Among these Daoists, you're the weakest, have the worst talent, and are the most cowardly! If you don't work hard, you won't even be qualified to watch in the future, "the lousy sword ridiculed.
This lower-level True God was Dugu Jian Xiaoxiao. After he had achieved godhood, he had snuck in not long ago.
The first thing he did after entering the God Realm was to take the Thunder Mountain's secret treasure. This was the treasure left behind by the previous owner of the lousy sword.
Although this lousy sword was telling the truth, Dugu Jian Xiaoxiao was still faintly unconvinced.
Speaking of which, he was able to stand out in the Great Evolution Universe because of this lousy sword in his hand.
Now that he had entered the God Realm, he had relied on this lousy sword to guide him all the way.
It could be said that everything he had was bestowed upon him by this lousy sword.
"You're the coward," he retorted. With a stomp of his foot, he turned into a streak of light and dashed along the undulating mountain range.
"Crackle, crackle …"
After he entered the Black Thunder Mountain Range, the reddish-gold lightning bolts in the sky seemed to have found a target and continuously smashed down on him.
However, Dugu Jian Xiaoxiao used a strange movement technique. Like a loach in water, he nimbly twisted and turned, and those reddish-gold lightning bolts all missed.
Some of the True Gods in the Black Thunder Mountain Range revealed strange expressions when they saw Dugu Jian Xiaoxiao.
"Look, a lower-level True God actually came in!"
"He's really courting death!"

Chapter 2204
Fortunately, that lousy sword by Dugu Jian Xiaoxiao's side was very familiar with the Mystic Thunder Mountain Range.
Dugu Jian Xiaoxiao relied entirely on his own strength to force his way through the outskirts of the mountain range.
When Dugu Jian Xiaoxiao entered the inner area of the Mystic Thunder Mountain Range, those Crimson Gold Mystic Bombs became even more powerful and faster. With Dugu Jian Xiaoxiao's current strength, he was indeed unable to force his way through.
However, that lousy sword was already prepared.
The origin of this Mystic Thunder Mountain Range was quite deep.
It was once the ancestral land of a large clan in the Divine Domain. This clan had produced Saints, and dozens of Divine Epochs ago, they had also entered the Sea of Time and became a powerful clan. For a time, they were incomparably famous in the Divine Domain.
Unfortunately, this glory didn't last for long.
Because they were ostracized by the League of Wealthy Clans, and because this clan's foundation was too deep, their floating islands were quickly squeezed into the Sea of Time by the League of Wealthy Clans. At the same time, they implicated the clan's ancestral land and were tragically exterminated.
Now, that lousy sword was guiding Dugu Jian Xiaoxiao to take away all of this clan's inheritance.
The lousy sword itself was also burdened with hatred.
However, this hatred could not be repaid with just a single sword.
Later on, this lousy sword was obtained by Luo Xiao, who let it choose a sword wielder in the Universe of Great Expansion. In the end, it placed its hopes on Dugu Jian Xiaoxiao.
Dugu Jian Xiaoxiao was ultimately unable to exact revenge by relying on a single Dao disciple. However, its goal was the same as Luo Xiao's. Now, it was assisting Dugu Jian Xiaoxiao for its own sake!
After entering the inner area, that lousy sword's blade flashed with a strange light blue rune. When this rune entered Dugu Jian Xiaoxiao's mind, a blue path appeared in front of Dugu Jian Xiaoxiao's eyes.
This blue path meandered through the mountain range, extending into the distance.
When Dugu Jian Xiaoxiao advanced along this path, he discovered that all of the Crimson Gold Mystic Bombs seemed to have grown eyes and actually avoided him completely …
No matter how dense the lightning on both sides of the path was, in the end, not a single Mystic Bomb landed on him!
He smoothly entered the inner area of the Mysterious Lightning Mountain Range and walked to the end of the path. It was an inconspicuous precipice.
Many True Gods had passed by this place before, but they did not notice anything strange.
The history of the Mystic Thunder Mountain Range was widely spread in the Divine Dominion. Many True Gods hoped to excavate some ruins and obtain some benefits from this ancestral land of the rich and powerful. They might even obtain some lost inheritances.
However, after so many years, he still hadn't found anything.
The reason was that they did not possess this broken sword …
When the Dugu Jian Xiaoxiao brandished this broken sword, she finally managed to knock open the door to the ancestral land of this great clan.
Soon, his figure disappeared in front of the cliff.
…
…
To the Divine Realm, these years were eventful times.
On the surface, the entire Divine Realm seemed as stable as the surface of a calm lake. But, in reality, it was filled with dangers.
During this period of time, the Governing Slate had trembled a few times, and the 833rd rule recorded on the Governing Slate had been triggered multiple times.
If it had been in the past, the wealthy Jiang family would have reacted strongly to such things.
After all, the Jiang family was in charge of receiving people, and they were in charge of the entrances between the various universes and the Divine Realm …
However, after Yu Taibai's warning, the Jiang family's reaction was very calm. It was almost to the point of indifference.
They knew that the problem originated from the Universe of Dayan, but in such a delicate situation, the Jiang family chose to remain silent.
During this period of time, three Saints of the Wealthy Alliance had fallen, and the universe that the Liu family's Saint had constructed had completely collapsed.
In other words, of the three Saints who had fallen, two could be resurrected, and the Liu family's Saint would forever be dead.
The myth of Saints being immortal was shattered for the second time, and this time, it was even more shocking.
After all, the prerequisite to killing a Saint was to shatter the universe …
However, someone had managed to kill a Saint and sever the connection between the Saint and the universe. Such a method was almost unheard of.
Later, news slowly spread among the wealthy families. It was said that Dongfang Chunjun had led a team to the Han family's ancestral land and found a woman. This woman had drawn out the Black Fire Torrent underground, which had swept away the lives of the three Saints. It was this woman who'd used a remarkable ability to cause the death of one of the Saints. At the same time, she'd severed the connection between the Saint and the universe, causing the universe to shatter.
On this day, the Governance Slate vibrated again.
In the Azure Demon Region, two quadrillion kilometers away from the Sea of Time, a woman in a light blue cotton dress fell out of a transportation tunnel and landed heavily on the grass.
"Ugh …"
Dizzy from the fall, she crawled up from the ground and looked at the desolate scenery around her with an aggrieved expression.
"Where is this …"
After looking around, her aggrieved expression turned into confusion.
Before she'd sneaked in, Lady Startail had told her that she could quickly find that immortal residence.
And Luo Zheng was very likely waiting for her there …
But where was the immortal residence in front of her?
Whoosh whoosh whoosh …
Just as she was at a loss, a row of light appeared in the distance.
Seven or eight tall and sturdy men flew over.
Most of these men were Proof of Divinity martial artists. Of the two men in the lead, one was a lower true god, and the other was a middle true god!
In such a remote place, it was indeed rare for a middle true god to appear.
"Why would a woman appear here?"
"Haha, the heavens must have prepared her for Boss!"
"Bring her back and present her to Boss!"
When these burly men saw the woman, excitement appeared on their faces and they all gathered around her.
The woman didn't panic and just stared blankly at these men. Instead, she asked, "Where … is this?"
"This is the Azure Demon Region," the middle true god in the lead replied.
"Azure Demon Region?"
The woman's expression became even more confused.
She vaguely remembered that the immortal residence was located in the Waterspout Forest. If she entered the divine region, she had to enter the forest to enter the immortal residence …
"Where is the Waterspout Forest?" the woman finally asked.
"The Waterspout Forest? Hehe, it's not far from here, but it's not a place you can enter. "The middle true god laughed maliciously, his gaze sweeping over her body.
The Waterspout Forest was the same as the other forbidden areas, constantly expanding.
Now, it had already occupied three quarters of the Azure Demon Region. The large and small families that had once lived in the Azure Demon Region had long since moved away. Those who gathered in the Azure Demon Region were all notorious villains of the divine region.
"Why can't I enter?" the woman asked curiously.
Seeing the woman's confused expression, the middle true god laughed even more wantonly. "If you want to know why, then follow us and you'll know!"
If it were any other woman, facing such a situation, they would be very vigilant. How could they so easily follow others?

Chapter 2205
When the group of strong men heard this woman's reply, they immediately burst into laughter.
They had wanted to capture this woman, but they hadn't expected that this woman of unknown origin would have such a strange temperament. She didn't have the slightest bit of vigilance …
"Then come with me." The middle True Divinity led the way.
The other Proving Divinities and lower True Divinities followed behind. Like this, they brought the woman away.
Looking at this scene, it was as if they were holding her hostage, but the woman still didn't notice anything strange.
The Azure Demon Region had very few people. After the city had been abandoned, it had been reduced to ruins. The plants of the entire Azure Demon Region had also been affected by the forbidden area of Takeshi Xuan Forest, growing abnormally tall.
Weeds that were originally knee-high could now grow to dozens of feet high. As for those towering trees, they grew even stronger, nearly blocking out the sun.
Occasionally, strange cries could be heard from the depths of the forest, causing one's hair to stand on end.
The woman finally became alert and asked, "You … where do you plan on taking me?"
"We're taking you to see our boss!" The middle True Divinity chuckled.
Hearing this, she immediately became unhappy. "I want to find Takeshi Xuan Forest, not your boss!"
"Our boss knows where Takeshi Xuan Forest is," the middle True Divinity explained.
The middle True Divinity was also thinking that it was better to avoid unnecessary trouble. If this woman obediently followed him, he wouldn't have to do anything.
If she didn't listen, then he couldn't be blamed for taking action.
"Oh …"
The woman's face was a bit reluctant. Even if she was stupid, she could tell that something was wrong.
However, she was still too inexperienced.
In the Realm of the Gods, her master had protected her. Even in the Illusory Battlefield, she could rely on her strength to crush countless powerhouses.
Now that she was in the Realm of the Gods, many things were no longer under her control.
Even before she had snuck into the Realm of the Gods, they had warned her and taught her. But now, she was still confused, completely unable to tell north from south.
Not long after, a thousand foot high wall appeared in front of them. This used to be a large city, but now it had become this group's lair.

Three Paragons and one quasi-sage. These people could easily kill him with a flick of their fingers, especially the Gu brothers.

Her words were definitely not a threat.
For Xi Youqin to be able to find the immortal mansion in such a short time was already a great stroke of luck.

As for the big-mouthed monster, Wu Yu, he hurriedly took his leave, afraid that the Gu brothers would bring him back to the dungeon …
Overall, the divine realm was in a temporary state of stability.
Dongfang Chunjun, who had originally planned to conquer the Abyssal Demon Realm, had temporarily given up on the idea.

After Dugu Jian Xiaoxiao opened that door, she also stepped onto a new path of destiny.

Chapter 2210

Not a human soul …

Chapter 2213

The time of the Judgment Light's descent was getting closer and closer.

Meanwhile, some of the Aragami who knew the truth were trying their best to squeeze into the bone tower. Under normal circumstances, these Aragami would not leak any information about the Judgement Light. A small piece of news suddenly began to spread like wildfire. At first, this news only spread in the more peripheral cities. Because the content of the news was too shocking, the speed of its spread was also extremely fast.

However, there will be a small number of people who can make it into the bone towers. Only a small number of people can survive! Are you willing to take the risk? "
"We are going to die anyway. Why not?"
"Since staying here is also waiting for our deaths, if we are lucky enough to make it into the bone towers, we might still be able to survive!"

Tan Xuan was ordered to spread the news about the Judgment Light in the Levitating City.
With his identity, it was not appropriate for him to show his face. Therefore, he found this middle-aged man who was quite prestigious in the Levitating City.
Tan Xuan promised this middle-aged man that as long as he encouraged the Aragami to attack the bone towers, this middle-aged man would be able to join the Xuanyuan Clan and take refuge in the bone towers of the Xuanyuan Clan.
The result was very successful …
"I'm sorry. The bone towers of the Xuanyuan Clan can't hold that many people." Tan Xuan grinned.
When the middle-aged man heard what Tan Xuan said, his face instantly turned pale. He stared at Tan Xuan and said, "You promised me!"
"So what?" Tan Xuan said calmly, "You can also attack the bone towers with them. Maybe you will have a chance to survive!"
"You, you … I'm going to kill you!"
The middle-aged man was furious. He began to activate the Aragami's power in his body.
However, just when his body began to expand, Tan Xuan had already turned into a stream of light and swept past the middle-aged man.
The middle-aged man's eyes suddenly widened. His body stiffened and did not move at all.
Meanwhile, there was blood flowing out from Tan Xuan's fingertips …
Plop!
The middle-aged man fell to the ground. Tan Xuan gently brushed his right hand and shook off the blood on it. He looked at the middle-aged man's body and said, "Why do you have to force me to do this? As a lowly person in the wilderness, you should obediently be purified by the Judgment Light … "
After saying that, Tan Xuan disappeared into the darkness again.
Things like this were happening in all the major cities.

Chapter 2214
Such things were not only happening within the territory of the Nuwa Clan.
In the Chiyou Clan, the Star Deer Clan, and other major clans, there were also countless Aragami being incited …
However, for the Xuanyuan Clan, the Nuwa Clan was their real target!
Below the Barbaric Water Bone Tower, millions of people were inciting the power of the Aragami. They turned into hundreds of thousands of feet tall Aragami and stacked together, forming a wall that completely surrounded the entrance of the bone tower.
Meanwhile, at the entrance of the Barbaric Water Bone Tower, six elders of the Nuwa Clan were guarding the place.
"How could this be?! Who spread the news about the Light of Judgment?! "An old woman with a hunched body said angrily.
Previously, before the Light of Judgment arrived, a small number of Aragami would attack the bone tower. However, there would only be dozens or even hundreds of Aragami. The Nuwa Clan would selectively take in these Aragami and kill them when they had no other choice!
However, this time, all the Aragami within the territory of the Nuwa Clan were coming!
The situation had become complicated.
Even though the Barbaric Water Bone Tower had opened up quite a lot of space, it would not be able to accommodate so many Aragami!
"Someone is spreading the news on purpose." A female elder with a voluptuous body made her judgment.
"What's their goal?" Another woman with a cold expression asked.
The old woman snorted coldly. "Do you even need to ask? Their goal must be the brother and sister of the head warden! "
The Nuwa Clan had taken in the brother and sister. They also knew the consequences. It was equivalent to declaring war on the head warden.
However, they thought that the real battle would only happen after the Light of Judgment arrived. They did not expect that they would do such a thing before the Light of Judgment arrived!
"That's not right. There aren't many people from the head warden's clan, right? For them to incite so many people in such a short period of time? Is that even possible? "The female elder with a voluptuous body felt strange.
Moreover, the head warden's clan had never shown their true faces to others. They always looked strange. It would be difficult for them to convince so many people in such a short period of time.
Just when the elders were talking, a rainbow-like halo appeared behind them. Gatekeeper Ming Wei's voice was heard. "This is not the head warden's clan."
"Gatekeeper!"
"Gatekeeper!"
The few female elders bowed to Ming Wei.
Ming Wei nodded slightly as she looked at the group of Aragami with a complicated expression.
"Sect Master, you said that these people were not instigated by the warden's family. Then who are they?" the old woman asked.
Ming Wei's eyes flickered as a cold smile appeared on her face. "Not all clans have been invited. For example, the Xuanyuan Clan."
"Sect Master, are you talking about the Xuan Yuan Clan?" The old woman was shocked.
The other elders' expressions were also very ugly. The cold-looking woman said coldly, "Does the Xuan Yuan Clan want to die?"
The Nuwa Clan was the most powerful clan, and no other clan dared to offend them. Even the Xuanyuan Clan was not as powerful as the Nuwa Clan. What the Xuanyuan Clan did was tantamount to courting death. One could imagine the consequences of angering the Nuwa Clan.
"The Chiyou Clan did not allow the Xuanyuan Clan to join our escape plan. Under such circumstances, if the Warden Clan extended an olive branch to them, what would you choose?" Ming Wei asked.
When the elders heard this, they immediately understood.
This was a counterattack from the Warden Clan. It was just that the Xuanyuan Clan had taken their place.
"It's not just the Xuanyuan Clan. Those smaller clans that did not join us might also join us. Their ultimate goal is still the siblings …"
At this point, Ming Wei smiled as the light in her eyes flickered. "Luo Zheng is right. I've been looking forward to the Warden Clan's counterattack!"
When they heard this, the elders were surprised.
The old woman even asked, "Why do you say that, Sect Master?"
Ming Wei asked calmly, "If we got Fu Chu and Xiao Yun, and the Warden Clan did not do anything, what would you say?"
The old woman thought for a moment, and her dim eyes suddenly brightened. "This means that Fu Chu and Xiao Yun are not important at all!"
"Yes, this means that Fu Chu and Xiao Yun cannot bring us out of this world. Therefore, the Warden Clan does not care at all. This is the most terrifying problem …"
As Ming Wei spoke, she had already started walking. At the same time, she said, "Now that the Warden Clan has joined forces with the Xuanyuan Clan, and even those smaller clans, this means that they are beginning to fear us. At the same time, it also means that Fu Chu and Xiao Yun have the ability to bring us out of this world!"
"Then what should we do with these Aragami?" The old woman asked again.
Ming Wei shook her head slightly. "The Xuanyuan Clan might have sent someone to hide among these Aragami. Their ultimate goal is to sneak into the bone tower. These people cannot be allowed to enter."
"Buzz …"
"Buzz …"
"Buzz …"
With every step that Ming Wei took, a rainbow-like ripple spread out from the tip of her feet. The ripple let out a faint buzzing sound.
At the same time when the ripple spread out, her body size doubled.
After she took dozens of steps, her entire body had transformed into an Aragami that was almost five hundred thousand feet tall. She looked down at the densely packed Aragami. At the same time, she opened her red lips slightly and spoke with a cold and clear voice, "Everyone, leave my Barbarian Water Bone Tower immediately. Otherwise, you will be killed without mercy!"
When the old woman and a few other female elders heard this, they looked at each other and took a deep breath at the same time. It seemed that Ming Wei was going to go on a killing spree today.
They knew that Ming Wei had such strength, but they did not expect her to really do it. There were tens of millions of people here!
After all, these few elders were born in this world, and they grew up in this world.
Although they saw the Aragami as the 'people of the wilderness', it was still difficult for them to slaughter tens of millions of living beings …
Meanwhile, Ming Wei, Jin, and the other powerful beings among the exiles had long regarded ordinary living beings as weeds. In order to leave this place, let alone killing tens of millions of people, even if there were ten times more people, they would still not blink their eyes.
The Aragami that gathered around still had a glimmer of hope in their hearts. They were hoping that the Nuwa Clan would be kind enough to let them stay in the Barbarian Water Bone Tower. That was why they did not attack the Barbarian Water Bone Tower immediately.
However, when they heard Ming Wei's words, everyone's heart instantly turned cold.
"After all, we are the people of the Nuwa Clan's territory!"
"Do you Nuwa Clan have the heart to watch us being purified …"
"I'd rather be killed by you than be purified by the Judgment Light …"
These people knew their fate. However, who would be willing to accept their fate?
Begging, cursing, and roaring sounds were heard one after another. The sound was so loud that it shook the sky. The scene was extremely chaotic.
Disappointment, resentment, anger, and resignation. Everyone had different expressions on their faces.
Nobody knew who was the first to charge towards the entrance of the Barbarian Water Bone Tower. Following that, more Aragami followed suit.
These Aragami worked together and rushed towards the Barbarian Water Bone Tower behind Ming Wei like a torrent.

Chapter 2215
People in high positions would usually be more cold-blooded.
However, it did not mean that they were cruel and bloodthirsty. It was just that they had no choice but to make some choices.
Ming Wei was not a person who liked to kill. However, at this moment, she did not hesitate at all!
Vroom …
Ming Wei suddenly pointed her finger at the crowd.
Not far away from her, a beam of colorful light appeared out of nowhere. This colorful light was like a little colorful snake. It was constantly crawling in the air. The head of the snake was chasing after the tail of the snake. The head and tail of the snake were connected together, forming a circle.
The speed of the light was extremely fast. Under the high-speed rotation, it formed a light circle that was millions of meters wide. At the same time, an extremely violent aura was being emitted from the light circle!
Some of the Aragami felt the horrifying aura and stopped in their tracks. Their faces were filled with fear.
"What kind of skill is this? I think it is even more powerful than the Judgment Light?"
"If we are caught by this light circle, we will definitely die!"
However, these voices of fear were soon drowned out.
"We have more people on our side. She might not dare to kill us!"
"That's right. She can't possibly kill all of us!"
"Don't be afraid. Let's go!"
These Aragami's eyes had turned red. They knew that only by entering the bone tower would they have a chance to survive. Other than that, they had no other choice. The desire to survive had completely suppressed the fear in their hearts.
Whoosh whoosh whoosh whoosh whoosh …
Not only did the Aragami not stop, but they also increased their speed.
"I don't dare to kill?" Ming Wei revealed a faint smile on her face. She grabbed the light circle with her right hand and gently twisted it.
Whoosh whoosh whoosh …
In an instant, a gravitational force was emitted from the light circle!
The gravitational force was so strong that it was impossible to resist.
Whether it was the Aragami that was hundreds of meters tall, or thousands of meters tall, or even thousands of meters tall, they could only feel a horrifying gravitational force pulling them. Even if they were to unleash their strongest power, they could not break free from it. They were involuntarily sucked into the gravitational force!
Crack crack crack …
After these Aragami that were as tall as mountains were sucked into the light circle, their originally intact bodies were shattered into pieces in the blink of an eye. They did not even have the time to scream. Or rather, they screamed before they died. However, even their voices could not escape from the light circle.
"So miserable!"
"Let's go …"
"What a vicious move! She really wants to kill us all! "
"I can't accept this, I …"
When the Aragami in front of them saw what happened, their faces turned pale. Their faces were filled with regret. However, it was too late for regrets.
The Aragami at the back still had a glimmer of hope in their hearts as they charged forward. They did not know that this was a path that would lead to death.
Looking from afar, the gigantic light circle was like a grim reaper, continuously harvesting the lives of the Aragami. Meanwhile, all of the Aragami were like moths that were flying towards the flame. From the moment they decided to charge into the barbarian water bone tower, their fates had already been decided.
The elders of the Nuwa Clan stood where they were, their faces stiff.
Although it was their own fault for attacking the barbarian water bone tower, these Aragami were living under the rule of the Nuwa Clan after all. They had quite a lot of connections with the sects under the Nuwa Clan.
Now that Grandmaster Ming Wei was about to slaughter millions or even tens of millions of Aragami, their hearts were heavy.
However, they also knew that their Grandmaster had no other choice. At this critical moment, if she showed any mercy to these Aragami, it would be cruel to herself and to the Nuwa Clan!
Tens of thousands of miles away from the barbarian water bone tower, Tan Xuan was hiding in the sand. He was covered with a light brown cloth. This Shadowless Cloth could completely conceal his presence.
The senses of those exiles were too powerful. Even though he was far away, he still had to be cautious.
"She's really going to kill them all. What a bold move …" Tan Xuan looked at the gigantic light circle. There was a hint of fear on his face. It seemed that the strategy of the Xuanyuan Clan was about to fail. He had no idea what those exiles were thinking about.
On the evil mountain in the Xuanyuan Clan's Evil Mountain bone tower, a few exiles were standing in the air. Meanwhile, Yan Lai who was dressed in black was sitting on the top of the mountain.
"Ming Wei is really ruthless. Is she really going to kill everyone?" One of the exiles from the Xuanyuan Clan said with a gloomy face.
At first, these exiles thought that their strategy was working. At least, they had successfully incited so many Aragami to attack the barbarian water bone tower. However, it seemed that it was like throwing eggs at a rock. There was no effect at all.
Yan Lai smiled and said, "Don't worry. She won't kill everyone."
"Brother Yan Lai, why do you say that?" One of the exiles asked.
Yan Lai was holding two small black pearls in his hand. The surface of the pearls was smooth and shiny. If one looked closely, they looked like the pupils of some kind of living creature. As the two pearls were moving back and forth in his hand, he continued to say, "I'm just thinking about it from another perspective. As the leader of the Nuwa Clan, slaughtering tens of millions of people is too cruel. In order to maintain her image among her people, what would she do?"
One of the exiles thought for a while and said, "She would spare some of the Aragami to save their lives."
Yan Lai said calmly, "That's right. She won't kill all of the Aragami. She would definitely spare some of them to show her kindness. This is the trick of a superior."
The other exiles were also enlightened.
"In other words, our assassins will be among the last batch of Aragami?" Another exiled asked.
Yan Lai nodded. "That's right."
"But even if the assassins successfully sneak into the barbarian water bone tower, they won't be able to get the chance to attack, right?" The exiled frowned and said.
Even if the Nuwa Clan did take in some of the Aragami, they would be watching over these Aragami strictly. It would be almost impossible for the assassins to get away. It would be even more impossible for them to assassinate the siblings. They might not even have the chance to find their targets.
"Of course not," Yan Lai said calmly.
"Then what's the real purpose of Brother Yan Lai?"
The exiles were instantly confused. In their eyes, what Yan Lai was doing was useless. However, a smart person like Yan Lai would never waste his time doing something like that.
"You'll know when you see it." Yan Lai's handsome face showed a confident expression.
Below the barbarian water bone tower …
The light circle that Ming Wei summoned had taken the lives of more than nine million Aragami.
These Aragami did not even leave behind their corpses. They were directly crushed into dust and disappeared from this world.

Chapter 2216
The rest of the Aragami thought that they were going to die. However, they suddenly noticed that the gravitational force had disappeared. All of them quickly retreated as if they had just been pardoned!
These Aragami had the same thought in their minds as they were saved from death.
At that moment, Ming Wei's voice was heard, "The Nuwa Clan has the Barbaric Water Bone Pagoda. We will not leave you to die. However, the space in the Barbaric Water Bone Pagoda is limited. It is impossible for us to save so many lives. All of you … can temporarily hide in the Barbaric Water Bone Pagoda!"
The tens of thousands of Aragami were already scared out of their wits. All they could do was to run for their lives.
Although they would still die outside the bone pagoda, they would still be able to live for a few more days. That was the instinct of life.
However, just as they were about to flee, they suddenly heard Ming Wei's words. The fleeing figures came to an abrupt halt, their faces filled with disbelief.
Many people showed hesitation on their faces.
"Could it be that she's lying to us?"
"Don't be fooled. She's lying to us. She must be trying to kill us!"
"Hmph. She has killed almost ten million people in such a short period of time. If she really wants to kill us, how much effort would she have to put in? You guys are not qualified to be fooled by her … "
As soon as she said that, many of them felt that it made sense.
If the Nuwa Exiled did not stop, the remaining twenty to thirty thousand people would have died. There was no need for such a powerful being to lie to them.
After understanding this point, many people's faces immediately revealed ecstasy.
They did not expect that they would be invited by the Nuwa Clan to temporarily hide in the Barbaric Water Bone Pagoda …
Twenty to thirty thousand people out of ten million had entered the Barbaric Water Bone Pagoda. They were one of the lucky few.
Among the crowd, two of them looked at each other quietly.
These two were the assassins that Yan Lai had arranged.
They were ordered by Yan Lai to hide at the back of the crowd.
At the last moment, the two of them were in despair. They could not break free from the light circle. Their final fate was the same as the others. They would be killed into pieces.
The two of them dared to sneak into the Barbaric Water Bone Pagoda to assassinate the siblings. Naturally, they were prepared to sacrifice themselves. Yan Lai would treat their families well.
However, they could not even enter the Barbaric Water Bone Pagoda. Their sacrifice was not worth it.
They did not expect Yan Lai to be so accurate. Ming Wei had really stopped!
"Sect Leader Ming Wei, do you really intend to let these people in?" The old woman looked worried.
"Sect Master, you're a kind-hearted person. There's really no need to be considerate of these lowly commoners. A long night may bring trouble …" Another Elder advised.
Ming Wei smiled faintly. "Alright, there's no need to kill everyone. Even if you let these Aragami into the barbarian water bone tower, it won't be a big problem. However, you guys need to be more alert. Do not let these people walk around in the bone tower."
She had just killed tens of millions of people, but now she was being called "benevolent". This was the reason why she had left these twenty to thirty thousand people alive.
As the leader of the Nuwa Clan, it was inappropriate for her to be too cruel and bloodthirsty.
Under the surveillance of the people of Nuwa Clan, the 20,000 Aragami entered the bone tower in an orderly manner.
Of course, there were also the two assassins sent by Yan Lai.
The Nuwa Clan had divided the interior of the bone tower into a separate area. The Aragami were arranged to stay in that area.
The two assassins mixed in with the crowd and carefully observed the interior of the Barbaric Water Bone Tower.
Dozens of Nuwa Clan members were standing on the high ground, responsible for monitoring the Aragami.
After that, an elder of the Nuwa Clan floated above the heads of the Aragami and spoke with a cold voice, "I believe that all of you understand the situation that you're in right now. It's a great blessing that we, the Barbaric Water Bone Tower, are willing to take you in. I hope that all of you can spend this period of time peacefully. When the Judgment Light is over, if any of you dare to do anything strange, you will be killed on the spot!"
The more than 20,000 Aragami remained silent. Silence filled the air.
They were already satisfied to have a place to stay and live. Why would they dare to do anything else? Everyone was sitting on the ground obediently.
After the elder of Nuwa Clan left, the two assassins exchanged glances with each other...
When Yan Lai handed the mission to them, the two of them were puzzled.
Even if they managed to sneak into the bone tower, it was impossible for them to assassinate the siblings. Killing someone under the eyes of the exiles was more difficult than ascending to the sky.
However, Yan Lai told them that as long as they could find the right opportunity, they would have to act immediately. The two assassins could only accept their fate!
After waiting for about an hour, one of the assassins suddenly made a hand gesture. The other assassin nodded as well, showing an expression as if he was ready to risk everything.
"Let's go!"
The first assassin suddenly roared and activated his power of the Aragami. He grew to the size of 20,000 feet as he rushed into the depths of the cave!
"Die!"
The faces of the few Nuwa Clan members darkened. They also quickly activated their power of the Aragami and chased after the first assassin.
The first assassin had attracted the attention of most of the people. The second assassin followed closely behind him and soared into the sky.
The assassin suddenly burst out with an astonishing speed as he rushed towards another direction. In front of the assassin's forehead, there was a cluster of black smoke that could not be dispersed.
The cluster of black smoke was given to him by the warden. Through the black smoke, he could sense the location of Fu Chu and Xiao Yun.
The siblings were not far away from here. They were only dozens of miles away. As long as he could kill the siblings, everything would be over!
In the blink of an eye, the second assassin had already rushed more than ten miles away …
Dozens of miles away, Fu Chu sat on top of a green stone platform as he focused on his cultivation.
In a short period of time, Fu Chu had already cultivated to the level of an intermediate true god. From the looks of it, his progress was not bad.
All of this was due to Ming Wei's ability.
"Whoosh … Whoosh …"
At this moment, Fu Chu suddenly heard a piercing sound of wind. He then saw an Aragami approaching from afar, heading straight for him!
"Bloodline Seal?" Fu Chu's eyebrows twitched.
The assassin could sense Fu Chu's existence by relying on the black smoke on his forehead, which was the Bloodline Seal that Fu Chu mentioned. Similarly, Fu Chu could also sense the existence of the cluster of black smoke.
Therefore, Fu Chu instantly understood that the Aragami was coming for him!
The assassin's speed was extremely fast. Even if the other Nuwa Clan members reacted, they would not be able to stop him in time …
However, when the assassin was about a few miles away from Fu Chu, a rainbow-like light flashed and slashed at the assassin.
The assassin maintained his high-speed trajectory. However, his huge body could not dodge in time and was instantly slashed in half.
After that, Ming Wei's figure silently appeared in front of Fu Chu. She frowned slightly as she looked at the assassin.
"Child's play …"

Chapter 2217
Fu Chu slowly walked over to Ming Wei. He looked at the corpse that had been cut in half by Ming Wei and said, "This person has a bloodline mark. As long as he is within a certain range, he will be able to find me. It was the warden's family."
The Bloodline Seal's range was not large. Otherwise, the two sisters would not have been able to escape the Warden Clan's pursuit.
Ming Wei looked at Fu Chu and said, "It's not just the Warden Clan that did this."
"There are other clans that joined them?" Fu Chu chuckled. He was very optimistic.
"Yes, don't worry … I'll ensure your safety." Ming Wei nodded.
At this moment, a few elders from the Nuwa Clan and a few exiles gathered. The last to arrive was Fu Chu's sister, Xiao Yun.
They knew Fu Chu's importance. When Fu Chu was attacked, it was natural for them to be nervous.
However, Ming Wei was the one who let these hidden dangers in.
"Sect Master Ming Wei, should we bring Fu Chu to a more secluded place?" An elder from the Nuwa Clan asked.
Xiao Yun shook her head. "I sensed the Bloodline Seal just now. As long as we're in this bone tower, it doesn't matter where we hide …"
Ming Wei smiled when she saw the worried looks on everyone's faces. "Don't worry. Whether it's the Xuanyuan Clan or the Warden Clan, they only dare to act in the dark. I, Ming Wei, am personally overseeing the situation.
In terms of strength, Ming Wei was the strongest exiled in this world. Yan Lai from the Xuanyuan Clan and Elder Jin from the Chiyou Clan could only be second to her. She was quite confident in her own strength.
Everyone nodded. If even Ming Wei could not protect Fu Chu, then it would be useless to hide anywhere.
However, at this moment, a spatial vortex appeared behind them. Elder Jin walked out of the spatial vortex.
Usually, exiles were not allowed to enter the bone tower of the Nuwa Clan. However, it was a special time now. The clans that were ranked at the top had agreed to take care of each other. Naturally, they were not so particular.
The moment Elder Jin appeared, his gaze fell on the corpse of the assassin. His eyes flashed and his face darkened. "Ming Wei, is everything alright here?"
Chi Ling Bone Pagoda was also under siege by the Aragami. After Old Jin had dealt with those Aragami, he was naturally worried.
Ming Wei smiled. "They're just some ants. I've already dealt with them."
Elder Jin nodded as a sneer appeared on his face. "That fellow Yan Lai actually used such a childish method. He's really stupid!"
At this moment, spatial fluctuations appeared around them one after another.
Following that, the exiles from the Star Deer Race, Fire Melt Race, Southern Dipper Race, and other large races appeared one after another. Like Elder Jin, they were very concerned about Fu Chu and Xiao Yun.
In the eyes of some exiles, they could even give up their bone pagodas and their clansmen, but the siblings could not die!
This showed how important this pair of siblings was to them.
However, when they understood the whole story, their expressions relaxed.
"Hahaha, that fellow Yan Lai has always been crafty, but this time he actually came up with such a rotten idea …"
"The warden's family is really worried about this pair of siblings. Looks like we might succeed this time!"
"Once the Judgement Light passes, we'll set off!"
In this relaxed atmosphere, the Star Deer Race's exiled Zhuang Xun casually took two steps toward Fu Chu and said, "However, since they've already made their move once, there will be a second time. We still have to be careful. Why don't I use my Heaven's End ability to create an area to protect Fu Chu?"
Hearing this, Ming Wei and Elder Jin frowned slightly as they looked at each other.
Ming Wei shook her head and said, "There's no need for that."
"Is that so?"
As soon as Zhuang Xun said that, a black light flashed in the depths of his eyes.
Whoosh!
The Star Deer Race's exiled Zhuang Xun extended his arm toward Fu Chu.
"What are you doing!?"
"Zhuang Xun! How dare you! "
"…"
The exiles from the other large races were shocked.
As an exiled, Zhuang Xun's speed was extremely fast.
The elders of the Nuwa Race could not react at all. As for Fu Chu, he did not resist at all.
"Crack!"
Zhuang Xun's hand was faster than lightning. In the blink of an eye, he had already grabbed Fu Chu's head. With a gentle tug, Fu Chu's head was separated from his body. At the same time, Zhuang Xun squeezed hard, and Fu Chu's head turned into a pool of blood. He was deader than dead …
"Hahaha …"
Zhuang Xun's face was full of pride as he kept drifting and distancing himself from Ming Wei, Elder Jin, and the rest of the exiles.
Ming Wei and Elder Jin, on the other hand, stood where they were and looked at Zhuang Xun with indifferent expressions.
"I'm sorry, Ming Wei, Elder Jin." Zhuang Xun held the bloody head with one hand and said with a fake smile, "The quota to leave this world is limited. I want to fight for one!"
Ming Wei stared coldly at Zhuang Xun and said, "If we succeed, you can also leave. Why do you still want to fight for the warden's quota?"
Zhuang Xun waved Fu Chu's head in his hand. "Because you are destined to fail. I don't think highly of you! The result proves that I'm right. You have failed. Why not use your head in exchange for a quota to leave? "
Yan Lai did not expect the Aragami assassins to complete their mission. He had spent so much effort for only one purpose, and that was to create a reasonable opportunity for the other exiles to visit the barbarian water bone tower.
Before that, Yan Lai had already promised Zhuang Xun of the Star Deer Clan.
As long as the young man was killed, the warden's clan would let Zhuang Xun leave. After some hesitation, Zhuang Xun made a choice. He chose to betray …
The two assassins in front used the warden's clan's "Bloodline Seal" to determine Fu Chu's location. Then, Zhuang Xun took the opportunity to visit the Nuwa Clan and killed Fu Chu when they were unprepared. It was a very direct method, but it was very effective.
Ming Wei sneered and sighed softly. She shook her head and said, "It's a pity that your judgment is wrong. You had the chance to leave with us …"
Hearing this, Zhuang Xun's face showed a strange expression. He felt a little uneasy. He grabbed the head in his hand and said, "Fu Chu is dead. What chance do you have?"
Elder Jin grinned and said, "You should be the one to die."

Chapter 2218
An exiled person like Zhuang Xun could teleport millions of miles away in an instant.
However, after the black snake coiled around Zhuang Xun, it actually fixed him on the spot …
"This, this is the Spirit Binding Snake Divine Venomous Worm! Ming Wei, you … "
Zhuang Xun stared at Ming Wei in panic. At the same time, his eyes were filled with confusion.
The Spirit Binding Snake Divine Venomous Worm was an extremely powerful venomous worm technique of the Nuwa Clan. When it was used by Ming Wei, it was even more powerful. Even a Nirvana Realm expert wouldn't be able to escape from it!
The problem was that Ming Wei used the Spirit Binding Snake Divine Venomous Worm on Fu Chu. Fu Chu would definitely die. Did this mean that Ming Wei had the intention to sacrifice Fu Chu?
Why would she choose to sacrifice Fu Chu? Zhuang Xun couldn't understand.
"Is it strange?" Ming Wei's pretty face finally revealed a smile.
"You are willing to kill Fu Chu?" Zhuang Xun asked as he struggled.
"Why not?" Ming Wei smiled and clapped her hands.
Not far away, a figure slowly floated over. It was the youthful Fu Chu. Fu Chu had a faint smile on his face.
Zhuang Xun frowned and shook his head. "Impossible. They used the bloodline imprint to confirm it. The one in my hand can't be fake!"
"Who said it's fake?" Ming Wei clapped her hands again.
A few more young men slowly walked over from the other side. These young men were all Fu Chu. No matter if it was their demeanor, appearance, or cultivation, they were all exactly the same.
At the same time, Ming Wei continued, "The moment I brought him into the bone pagoda, I knew that there might be a traitor. However, I couldn't guard against everyone, so I decided to let you all show yourselves … You did kill Fu Chu, but I have more than ten of them here!"
After hearing Ming Wei's words, Zhuang Xun's eyebrows twitched.
"It shouldn't be difficult for the Nuwa Clan to create someone with the same bloodline, appearance, and memory, right?" Ming Wei continued with a smile.
Zhuang Xun was silent for a while. No matter what, he had failed.
Realizing that he would not be able to escape death, his eyes suddenly lit up with two flames.
Hualala …
Red-gold flames rose on the surface of his body.
The scorching heat spread out and instantly caused the temperature in the entire cave to rise rapidly!
After realizing that he could not escape, Zhuang Xun decided to risk his life!
The other elders of the Nuwa Clan all stepped back. Ming Wei, on the other hand, sneered, "Is there any point in resisting now?"
A dazzling multicolored light spread out from her fingertips, and this dazzling multicolored light quickly enveloped Zhuang Xun.
Even though Zhuang Xun wasn't considered weak among the exiles, he didn't have the slightest ability to resist.
When Elder Jin saw this scene, he was also secretly shocked.
Although Elder Jin was confident that he could kill Zhuang Xun, he knew that he couldn't do it so easily. Elder Jin knew that Ming Wei's strength was above his own, but he didn't expect that after all these years, Ming Wei had already reached an unfathomable level …
"Huff huff …"
The light that enveloped Zhuang Xun suddenly expanded, and then began to contract violently.
When the light shrank to its limit, a scream rang out, and Zhuang Xun disappeared along with it.
Killing a Nirvana Realm powerhouse with a wave of her hand made the exiles of the other large clans' pupils dilate slightly, and there was a hint of fear in their eyes.
"Even now, I still can't completely trust all of you." Ming Wei's red lips parted slightly as she looked at the exiles and said, "I hope that all of you can properly grasp this opportunity. If you choose to side with the other side, that's also your freedom. However, I believe that such a choice is destined to be regretted!"
After saying that, Ming Wei took two light steps forward. Circles of rainbows appeared in front of her, and she entered them.
"Hmph, that Zhuang Xun betrayed us at the last moment. He deserved to die!"
"Just now, I thought that child Fu Chu was really dead. That scared me …"
"…"
In the face of Zhuang Xun's death, the exiles present shook their heads and sighed.
Elder Jin also said to everyone, "Everyone should obediently guard your own bone pagoda. Everything will wait until the Judgment Light descends, so as to avoid arousing suspicion …"
Even Elder Jin sighed. It was a wise choice to seek out the Nuwa Clan back then.
With just the Chiyou Clan alone, it would be difficult to protect Fu Chu.
Then, the exiles left the bone pagoda and prepared to welcome the Judgment Light.
…
…
Inside the bone pagoda on Evil Mountain.
Yan Lai was still sitting at the top of the mountain, his calm expression unreadable.
The other exiles of the Xuan Yuan Clan had ugly expressions on their faces.
"I thought that Zhuang Xun had attacked at such a close distance. Who knew that Ming Wei was even more ruthless!"
"That's because she's so powerful! Now that we've completely offended the Nuwa Clan, we'll be in big trouble if they come looking for trouble with us after the Judgment Light descends! "
"Big Brother, what do you think we should do?"
Although these exiles were determined to follow Yan Lai, they were still wary of the Nuwa Clan and Ming Wei.
Yan Lai raised an eyebrow. "Look for trouble with us? Hehe, they're busy escaping from this world. Where would they find the time to look for trouble with us? "
Despite Yan Lai's words, the exiles still didn't look too happy.
However, at this moment, the black-robed man from the warden's clan appeared at the top of the mountain again.
He looked down at Yan Lai and said, "You've failed."
Yan Lai blinked. "That's normal. With Ming Wei's vigilance, it'd be strange if I could succeed so easily."
"What do you plan to do next?" the black-robed man asked.
The warden's clan hadn't been very helpful in the assassination of Fu Chu. They had only planted a bloodline imprint in the assassin's mind. However, they hadn't expected Ming Wei to create more than ten Fu Chus.
"We'll talk about it after the Judgment Light." Yan Lai narrowed his eyes, a dangerous glint faintly visible in them. "The prerequisite is that you give me more authority and help. Otherwise, it'll be very difficult to deal with that crazy woman."
Ming Wei's strength was too terrifying. Even Yan Lai was wary of such a woman.
"Sure," the black-robed man replied in an ethereal voice. "I hope you won't disappoint me."
With that, the black-robed man's figure slowly disappeared.
Three days later, a milky white light slowly rose from the easternmost part of this world and slowly rolled toward the west.
This light contained an almost limitless purifying power, and this purifying power would purify all living creatures, except for those without intelligence.
Whether they flew to the top of this world or hid deep underground, they couldn't escape the fate of being purified.

Chapter 2219
The cities that were bustling with noise were now silent.
The living beings outside the bone towers had returned to their starting point after being active in these cities for a thousand years.
This world had gone through countless reincarnations.
The major clans living in the bone towers had already gotten used to such a scene and result.
If things were to go according to the previous path, the Yin people would slowly gain intelligence. Then, under the guidance of the major clans, they would step into the empty cities and give birth to new Aragami. Meanwhile, a few of them would be able to join the bone towers …
However, the situation this time was different.
The exiles in the bone towers were ready to make a move.
The major clans led by the Nuwa Clan and the Chiyou Clan were aiming to defeat the warden's clan and escape from this world.
The major clans led by the Xuanyuan Clan were also aiming to escape from this world. However, their goal was to stop the Nuwa Clan and the Chiyou Clan …
There was an irreconcilable conflict between these two forces.
Both sides knew very well that this thousand-year reincarnation cycle was destined to be different from the past.
In the Chiyou Bone Tower …
All the Chiyou clansmen had gathered in the square.
Elder Jin stood in front of them. He could not hold back the excitement in his heart. However, his face was still calm.
"As the clansmen of the Chiyou Clan, I believe that you know the origin of our clan very well." Elder Jin's voice spread out and entered their ears clearly. "I, Fu Er, Chi Yi, and … we came from a broader mother world. Our roots are in the mother world! Since the moment I entered this world, I have always wanted to leave this world. However, I never had the chance. However, now, the chance has appeared. "
The Chiyou Aragami had a complicated expression when they heard Elder Jin's words.
Other than the exiles and Luo Zheng, all the Aragami were born in this world.
Perhaps some of the Aragami were interested in the outside world. However, there were still many who were not that interested. They grew up in the bone towers and ran amok in this world. Their lives were free and easy …
"I know that some of you are against leaving." Elder Jin continued, "I am not forcing you to follow me. If you are willing to stay, you can stay in the bone towers."
As soon as he said that, the Aragami did not look any better.
The reason why the Chiyou Clan could become a powerful clan in this world was because of these powerful exiles. If these exiles were to leave, the remaining Aragami would not be enough to support the Chiyou Clan.
They would soon lose control of the Chi Spirit Bone Pagoda and be driven out by the exiles of the other races, or even slaughtered!
Therefore, no matter how unwilling they were, they were still willing to follow Elder Jin and the exiles!
"I will give you six hours to consider whether to stay or leave. Within these six hours, pack up your things and gather outside the bone pagoda." Elder Jin led the way out of the bone pagoda.
Along with him were the exiles, the elders, and the leaders of the Chi You Tribe. Of course, Luo Zheng was there as well.
"Yes! The purified aura is indeed different. It has a fresh and sweet smell! "
Mao Xue raised her head and sniffed the air.
Luo Zheng said with a faint smile, "Isn't it the same?"
Mao Xue shook her head and said seriously, "It's not the same! The light of judgment not only purifies all living beings, it also purifies all the filth in this world! "
Luo Zheng also followed Mao Xue and sniffed, but did not notice anything different.
However, Mao Xue's words made Luo Zheng think.
Why did the Primal Chaos Elder God build this prison? Why did he exile Elder Jin, Fu Er, and the others here?
Elder Jin once told Luo Zheng that the bones in this world were clones of everyone. But no matter how he speculated, why did the Primal Chaos Elder God build this prison?
More and more clansmen came out of the bone pagoda …
Most of the clansmen knew the situation they were facing, they could only follow the exiles. Only a small number of clansmen refused to leave. These clansmen thought that the Chi Spirit Bone Pagoda was their home and refused to leave with the exiles!
After all the clansmen made up their minds, huge bone ships slowly rose into the sky. Violet Spirit, Diao Yuan, and the other elders led the clansmen and moved towards the north in an orderly manner.
This world was very small for the exiles, but it was still too big for the Aragami. It would take a long time for so many people to migrate. Elder Jin and Ming Wei had already agreed to meet under the Star Spirit Bone Pagoda in the north!
At the same time, the clansmen of the Nuwa Clan also began to move towards the Star Spirit Bone Pagoda!
The Star Spirit Bone Pagoda was the territory of the Star Deer Clan.
Although Zhuang Xun of the Star Deer Clan was killed by Ming Wei because he ambushed Fu Chu, the other exiles of the Star Deer Clan did not participate. They were still willing to follow the Nuwa Clan.
As for the exiles of the Nandou Clan, the Fire Melts Clan, and other large clans, they also led their clansmen and began to migrate …
After about three months, more than ten large clans and hundreds of thousands of clansmen finally gathered under the Star Spirit Bone Pagoda.
"Luo Zheng!"
Xiao Yun saw Luo Zheng in the crowd. Her beautiful eyes squinted slightly, turning into a pair of beautiful crescent moons.
After entering the Nuwa Clan, under the nourishment of the vast amount of desolate bones, her cultivation base had also skyrocketed. She was now a middle-level Aragami.
She wore a long yellow dress, making her look elegant and refined. Her temperament seemed to have changed quite a bit.
Fu Chu, who was beside Xiao Yun, had also lost his childishness.
He did not know what method Ming Wei used to raise Fu Chu's cultivation base to the level of an upper-level True God in such a short period of time …
Even Luo Zheng could not compare to such a speed of advancement. However, it was not because Fu Chu's talent was higher than Luo Zheng's. The most important thing was Ming Wei's ability.
After a short gathering, everyone continued to migrate.
Before this, no one knew the location of the warden's clan. Now, it was natural that Fu Chu and Xiao Yun would guide them.
Hundreds of thousands of people continued to move north …
After a few months, everyone had arrived at the extreme north of this world.
This was a barren land. Apart from a large stretch of wasteland, there were no other living creatures.
As there was no Eternal Wasteland, there were no sneaky people.
In fact, before this, many exiles had already explored this place. In the end, they did not find anything.
Therefore, when Xiao Yun and Fu Chu guided them here, the exiles were rather puzzled. They could not imagine that the warden's clan's nest would be hidden here.

Chapter 2220
No one knew how big the bottom of the chaos was.
The prison only occupied a small area at the bottom of the chaos.
If they could pass through this jagged area, it would be equivalent to leaving this world …
Many exiles, such as Ming Wei and Elder Jin, had tried it themselves.
With their strength, it was not difficult to pass through. In fact, this "prison" did not have a wall to restrain them. What truly restrained them was the curse chain.
They could not break free of the curse chain. Even if they escaped from this world, they would still be pulled back.
The reason why the exiles had placed their hopes on Fu Chu was because he had the ability to sever the curse chains.
Looking at the jagged area, Ming Wei's eyes flashed with doubt. She asked, "The warden's nest is not in this world?"
Fu Chu looked ahead and nodded. He stretched out his hands and gently drew a circle in the air.
A unique scene suddenly appeared within the circle.
The curse chain behind the exiles was densely coiled together and extended into the darkness along the jagged area.
