"If we follow this path, we'll be able to pass through it?" Ming Wei raised her eyebrows after seeing the path clearly.
The other exiles also looked over, their faces full of anticipation.
Fu Chu looked at Xiao Yun.

Xiao Yun nodded. "Yes, we can pass through this path. But I think they are ready …"

As for the hundreds of thousands of clansmen, they stayed outside …
When the situation was unclear, these clansmen only needed to wait outside for orders.

About two hours later, the group had traveled over a hundred miles. Then, the road ahead suddenly opened up!
"This is …" Luo Zheng suddenly raised his eyebrows.
The eyes of the other exiles also shone brightly.
Previously, they had needed Fu Chu's ability to see the existence of the curse chains. However, after they passed through the path, they no longer needed Fu Chu's help. The curse chains appeared in front of everyone's eyes.
The Curse Chains in front of him were connected to all the living beings in this world, so there were probably tens of millions or even billions of Curse Chains floating in the air. The densely packed Curse Chains could be said to cover the sky and the sun, extending all the way into the distance.
In the distance, there was a huge platform, and the curse chains were all tied up on it!
"We finally found this damned place. Hehe …" One of the Fire Melt Race's exiles became inexplicably excited. "We just have to break the Curse Chains on our bodies, right?"
After saying that, the exiled stepped into the air. A spiral of flames randomly wrapped around him, and he actually teleported in an instant!
"Don't!"
When she saw the actions of the Fire Meld Clan's exile, Little Yun hurriedly spoke out to stop him.
However, it was already too late …
Balls of black liquid suddenly surged out of the spatial tunnel opened by the exiled. When the liquid touched the exiled, it instantly swallowed him.
It was unknown what this black liquid was, but it tightly wrapped around the exiled.
"Qin Mi!"
The exiles from the Fire Melt Race turned pale with fright.
One of them, a short old man, lightly shook his hand, and a white jade ruler appeared in his hand. Without any hesitation, the jade ruler touched Qin Mi's body. At the same time, a terrifying energy was released from the jade ruler.
This old man was clearly going to help Qin Mi get rid of the black liquid!
However, not only did the white jade ruler not get rid of the black liquid, but the black liquid actually spread along the white jade ruler, and actually wanted to swallow the white jade ruler and the old man!
The old man clenched his teeth and had no choice but to let go.
Soon, Qin Mi and the white jade ruler were cleanly swallowed by the black liquid and disappeared in front of everyone's eyes.
Just like that, an exiled died in front of everyone.
This person from the Fire Melt Race was considered average among the exiles, but he was unable to resist at all …
Everyone was speechless as they looked at the place where Qin Mi disappeared.
Xiao Yun frowned and said, "They already know that we are coming. They will definitely set up layers of restrictions. Even you can't act rashly!"
A trace of fear appeared in Ming Wei's eyes. At the same time, she said, "If I'm not wrong, this black liquid is the Dispelling Water. They actually filled the spatial tunnel with such a large amount of this …"
Elder Jin nodded. "It is indeed the Dispelling Water. This kind of water can only exist in spatial tunnels. Everyone, don't panic. It will be fine as long as you don't break the spatial tunnel!"
Although Elder Jin's words were comforting, everyone's hearts were still heavy.
They had finally found this world with great difficulty. Both sides hadn't truly fought yet, but one of them had inexplicably fallen. This trip would probably be more difficult than they had imagined.
Luo Zheng followed in the group and remained silent the whole time.
Although his strength had advanced by leaps and bounds, it was still insufficient in front of these exiles.
At this stage, he couldn't help too much. He could only wait and see.
"Elder Jin is right." Xiao Yun nodded. "Everyone, just follow me. Although they have set up restrictions, they aren't as powerful as we imagined. Everyone, don't be too rash!"
Under Xiao Yun's lead, the hundred exiles continued to advance in this gloomy world.
At the same time, on a high platform in the depths of this world, there were about thirty people standing …
These people weren't members of the warden's family. They were exiles from the bone pagoda.
The leader was Yan Lai from the Xuanyuan Family!
"Hehe, the restrictions in this place are powerful. Qin Mi couldn't resist at all and fell just like that. I was defeated by him back then!" An exiled laughed.

Chapter 2221
"Brother Yan Lai, what else are you worried about?" One of the exiles with a big beard asked, "We've all seen the tricks that the warden's family has set up. Even if there are ten of Ming Wei, what can they do to us?"

Under Xiao Yun's lead, Ming Wei, Jin Lao, and the others were gradually moving faster.
There was no clear scenery in this dark space. The grayish-white color was very depressing. Fortunately, these exiles had a strong state of mind. Each of them looked calm.

Only Ming Wei and the others who were leading the way in front could make them feel slightly relieved …
After traveling for a hundred miles, everyone heard the sound of water splashing. It was as if the tide of the sea was constantly rolling. However, they could not see any water source at all.
After Xiao Yun heard the sound, she immediately stopped.
"What's wrong?" Ming Wei walked forward and asked.
"It's the Heart of the Sea Water," Xiao Yun said with a frown.
"What's the Heart of the Sea Water?" Ming Wei asked.
As soon as Ming Wei asked, a series of thin bubbles suddenly appeared on the ground far away. These bubbles were spreading towards them at an unbelievable speed.
"Be careful, they're controlling the Heart of the Sea Water to attack!" Xiao Yun's expression changed drastically as she quickly retreated.
"Hualala!"
The bubbles on the ground quickly rose into the air. They were thin and long water arrows!
Looking from afar, these water arrows didn't seem like much. But when these water arrows approached, everyone could clearly see that each of these water arrows was 60,000 to 70,000 feet long. They were incomparably huge, and within the bodies of these water arrows, light blue water was crazily rotating, forming a sharp arrowhead. This arrowhead contained a power that made one's heart palpitate!
"Retreat!"
Ming Wei only spat out one word.
The others could retreat, but she couldn't.
Facing those huge water arrows, Ming Wei stretched out her hand and lightly patted it. A brilliant bracelet appeared on her arm.
She gently shook the bracelet, and a seven-colored light curtain opened up like a large umbrella in front of her!
"Pu!"
When the first water arrow hit the light screen, the light screen swayed slightly before the water arrow dispersed.
"Puff puff puff puff puff …"
Following which, even more water arrows hit the light screen, bursting out with a huge impact.
Ming Wei raised her right hand and stabilized the light screen. She said indifferently, "Everyone, follow behind me. Continue!"
These water arrows smashed into the seven-colored light screen, causing a series of violent explosions. Even Luo Zheng was speechless when he saw this scene. With Luo Zheng's current cultivation base, it was very difficult for him to block a single water arrow. However, Ming Wei was standing in front of him, taking care of all the attacks …
This woman's strength was truly terrifying.
Everyone gathered behind Ming Wei and continued forward.
But these huge water arrows seemed to be endless. Everyone's speed slowed down significantly. The speed of hundreds of people was like a snail …
This continued for less than an hour, but they had only traveled three to four miles. This space wasn't big, but if they continued at their current speed, it would take forever.
"We can't continue like this." Ming Wei frowned and said, "Xiao Yun, where are these water arrows coming from?"
Xiao Yun frowned as she looked into the distance and said, "The Seaheart Heavy Water is shot out by heavy water crossbows. I don't know who is controlling the heavy water crossbows, but they must be hiding in the distance to ambush us …"
"Is that so …" Ming Wei lowered her head and thought for a moment. Her gaze fell on Elder Jin and said, "Elder Jin, I'll have to trouble you."
Elder Jin understood tacitly. He nodded slightly and said, "Fu Er, Chi Yi, follow me!"
The three of them were the three strongest exiles of the Chiyou Clan. Now that they had reached this point, these large clans didn't hold back at all!
The three of them didn't hesitate and rushed out of the light screen. They turned into three streaks of light and nimbly shuttled through the huge water arrows. Soon, they disappeared from everyone's sight.
The others were still hiding behind Ming Wei. In this situation, most of them couldn't help and could only wait passively. Luo Zheng was the same.
Not long after …
From a faraway space, a few resplendent lights suddenly shot into the sky.
"Boom! Boom! Boom … "
Everyone felt the earth tremble.
A trace of worry flashed across the faces of the Chiyou Clan's elders.
Obviously, the three exiles of the Chiyou Clan were fighting with someone in the distance. Moreover, the opponent was very likely the warden's clan. Naturally, they were worried about the safety of the three exiles.
Especially Zi Yu. After all, her father was among them.
The violent rumbling continued for a period of time before it quieted down, and the water arrows that were shot from afar also disappeared ….
Not long after, three streaks of light flew over from afar. Seeing the three exiles of the Chiyou Clan return safely, everyone breathed a sigh of relief.
Elder Jin, who was in the lead, was carrying a person in his hand!
"Bang!"
Elder Jin flew in front of the crowd and casually tossed the man to the ground.
When the exiles saw this, a strange expression appeared on their faces.
"It's Lu Yu?"
"Lu Yu of the Four Symbols Clan?"
"Why is he here?!"
The Four Symbols Clan was also a small clan with a bloodline. There were only two exiles in this clan, and one of them was Lu Yu. They naturally recognized him.
Elder Jin stared at the person and said, "Apart from him, there was also Hong Kai from the Earth Brute Clan and Yi Lin from the Nine Eyes Clan who released the water arrows. However, I killed them all. It seems that these small clans entered this place one step ahead of us."
Ming Wei frowned and said, "That means Yan Lai is also here?"

Chapter 2222
At this moment, Lv Yu, who was curled up on the ground, suddenly let out a strange laugh.
Ming Wei frowned slightly and asked, "What are you laughing at?"
"Brother Yan Lai is here, of course," Lv Yu's eyes flashed with a dim light as he stared at Ming Wei.
Ming Wei sneered, "So what? Yan Lai is no match for me … But you exiles are foolish enough to follow the Xuanyuan clan! "
The Xuanyuan clan was ranked second in this world, but there was still a considerable gap between them and the Nuwa clan.
Ming Wei, in particular, was almost invincible in this world. Even Elder Jin and Yan Lai were inferior to her.
"What else? You think you're interested in small clans like us?! " Lv Yu suddenly roared when he heard this, his expression somewhat ferocious, "You big clans only know how to squeeze us exiles step by step. Even if we had a chance to escape this time, did you even consider us? To be honest, I don't think the warden clan will really let us leave if we ambush you. But if we can't leave, you guys can forget about it too! "
Ming Wei, Elder Jin, and the other exiles all had strange looks on their faces when they saw Lv Yu roaring.
Over the years, apart from the top ten clans, the other small clans and even the exiles had been greatly suppressed …
After all, the ones who supported the development of a clan were the exiles, and the strength of the exiles was obvious at a glance. Although many small clans chose to band together, they were still unable to fight against the big clans, especially giants like the Nuwa clan and the Chiyou clan.
When the small clans were being squeezed, the exiles could only ask their clans to give way and endure. Over time, the exiles of the small clans were filled with resentment towards the big clans!
In the past, everyone was in the same world, so the exiles of the small clans had no choice but to endure.
Now that the exiles like Lv Yu heard that the Nuwa clan had found a way to escape, the disparity in their hearts naturally increased, and the resentment that had been accumulated exploded.
Just as Lv Yu had said, if they could not leave, then the big clans of this world could not leave either.
It was like a group of people scrambling to climb up a dry well. The people at the top were about to climb out, but the people at the bottom were desperate to drag them down …
"Don't waste your breath on him. Let's kill him and move on!" An exile from the Fire Meld Race spoke, and a blazing flame appeared in his hand as he walked towards Lv Yu.
Ming Wei sighed softly and turned around.
Due to the lack of resources in this world, the competition between each other became even more intense. This was something that no one could avoid …
"Feng!"
The raging flames quickly enveloped the green feather.
Lü Yu's roar came from within the flames. That roar was filled with vicious curses. "You will definitely die here. All of you will die here. No one will be able to escape …"
Just as the flames were madly devouring Lü Yu, an extremely majestic energy suddenly emerged from Lü Yu's body! At this moment, he had chosen to self-destruct!
The power of the self-destruction of a Nirvana Realm powerhouse was naturally extremely terrifying. Even though Lv Yu was one of the weaker ones among the exiles, he was still not to be underestimated. Even the residual power of the explosion was enough to pose a fatal threat to the Aragami.
However, this group of people was ultimately filled with talents. Lü Yu's body turned into a huge ball of light and began to expand in all directions.
Fu Two from the Chiyou Race flashed, and a pale yellow paper talisman appeared in his hand. He slapped the paper talisman in the air, and streaks of pale yellow light formed into tentacles that enveloped the expanding ball of light.
When these tentacles completely enveloped the ball of light, the energy contained within the ball of light was completely blocked within, and not a single bit escaped …
"What a powerful technique!"
When Luo Zheng saw this scene, he secretly sighed in his heart.
Apart from Elder Jin, these exiles from the Chiyou Clan had never revealed their talents. However, the technique that they casually used was able to restrain the self-detonation of a Nirvana Realm powerhouse. This strength had indeed caught his attention …
After a few breaths, the energy produced by Lü Yu's self-destruction reached its peak and began to rapidly dissipate. In the end, it quickly disappeared under the yellow paper talisman that Fu Two had slapped out.
Probably after hearing Luo Zheng's exclamation, 9527, who had been silent all this while, suddenly spoke up, "Isn't it just the True Meaning of Soul Seal and Dao Extermination? It's not worth it, is it?"
"The True Meaning of Soul Seal and Dao Extermination?"
Luo Zheng's eyes narrowed slightly.
When Fu Two slapped out the yellow paper, Luo Zheng did indeed feel a powerful sealing power from it. He did not expect that it was also a True Meaning of Dao.
"Yes, I've said before. Although the True Meaning of Dao comprehended from the cultivation method of the great world is powerful, it can't be compared to the truth. Therefore, their so-called crossing of the Nirvana Realm is just a false crossing …"
9527 did not know how to hide his feelings. Naturally, what he expressed was extreme disdain.
"False crossing of the Nirvana Realm …" Hearing 9527's evaluation, Luo Zheng was rather speechless. He retorted, "Even if it's a false crossing of the Nirvana Realm, it's still far stronger than I am now. Otherwise, until now, I can't help at all."
Ever since they entered this space, it had been Ming Wei and Elder Jin leading the way in front. Not to mention Luo Zheng and the others, even the Aragami Elders of the major clans could only hide behind the Nirvana Realm powerhouses.
It could be said that even the weakest Nirvana Realm powerhouse was not something that the Aragami could fight against …
9527 was very unconvinced. "That's only because their cultivation is higher than yours. Just wait and see. The Warden Clan that you speak of will eventually use 'Ruin's Equality of All Living Beings. When that time comes, this group of Nirvana Realm powerhouses will definitely be dumbfounded. In the end, they will still have to rely on your Falling Star Hand. If you don't believe me, just wait and see, hehe …"
After Luo Zheng comprehended the original owner's Truth Crystal, 9527 taught him how to use it.
The method of using the Truth Crystal was extensive and profound. Some of the methods were far beyond what Luo Zheng could grasp at his current level. The 'Falling Star Hand' was the most basic magic of the original owner.
Although Luo Zheng had a small understanding of it, he had never used it in front of others.
In terms of power, it was not considered powerful in Luo Zheng's eyes. After all, the Truth Crystal itself was a type of energy. It did not have that kind of frightening feeling. It was far inferior to the energy of the Path.
"Equality of All Living Beings? What does that mean? "
Luo Zheng was slightly stunned. He had forgotten that 9527, as a part of the soul of the Primordial God of Chaos, knew more about this world than him, or even Ming Wei, Elder Jin, and the other exiles.
It was just that he did not ask before, and 9527 did not mention it …

Chapter 2223
Hearing this, Luo Zheng frowned slightly.
Luo Zheng wasn't satisfied with the power of the Falling Star Hand, but that didn't mean he looked down on the Truth God Power.
Just like what 9527 said, the Truth had to be comprehended from the fusion to the Great Unity. This level of energy might be able to touch upon the essence of the entire Chaos.
Although Luo Zheng had condensed the Truth Crystal, he hadn't truly grasped it at all. It was completely driven by his bloodline …
But the warden's clan was different.
They believed in the Ruin's orders to control this world. The Ruin must have given them some powerful means.
If so, how would Ming Wei, Elder Jin, and the others deal with it?
"Can I really use the Falling Star Hand to deal with this All Lives Equal?" Luo Zheng continued to ask. He still couldn't figure out how this God Power, which was far from meeting the requirements of actual combat, could be used to deal with the Ruin's power.
"You'll know when the time comes." 9527 seemed very confident.
Since it said so, Luo Zheng didn't ask any more questions. He followed the group and continued forward.
With Little Yun leading the way, and relying on the powerful strength of Ming Wei, Elder Jin, and the other exiles, the group became unstoppable.
Although the exiles of the small clans repeatedly attacked them, they were easily resolved. Not only that, but some of the exiles of the small clans also died at their hands.
Three days later …
The group had already traveled tens of thousands of miles in this space.
Those high platforms that were originally far away now appeared in front of everyone's eyes. Many people had a hint of excitement in their eyes.
After all, this expedition had been very smooth. After entering this space, only Qin Mi had died. After that, they had been unstoppable …
However, Ming Wei, Elder Jin, and the others weren't so optimistic.
This journey had been too smooth, so smooth that it was abnormal.
If things were abnormal, there had to be a reason. Moreover, they weren't just fighting Yan Ming or the exiles of the small clans. Their true opponent was the warden's clan.
Up until now, the warden's clan hadn't shown up, nor had they made a move.
This was the biggest hidden concern.
"Little Yun, does the Warden Clan live here?" Ming Wei asked as she looked at the platforms.
Xiao Yun nodded. This was the place where she was born, so she was naturally very familiar with it. She then said, "These high platforms are the Imprisonment Towers. There are a total of 200,000 Imprisonment Towers, and they are used to bind all the living beings in this world."
Every prison tower was connected to countless chains of curses, and all the living beings in this world were bound to the high platforms.
"Can we destroy these platforms?" Ming Wei asked again.
Xiao Yun shook her head. "These platforms were created by the Primal Chaos Ancient Gods. You can't destroy them."
Hearing this, Chi Yi from the Chiyou Race was a little unconvinced. He smiled faintly and said, "Then let me try!"
Xiao Yun didn't stop Chi Yi. There wasn't any danger in destroying these high platforms.
"Sou!"
Chi Yi abruptly lifted himself up from the ground. He clenched his fist with one hand, and a black spiral continuously revolved around his fist.
Although the energy emitted from the spiral was extremely powerful, it was so condensed that not a single trace of energy leaked out.
"Break!"
Chi Yi let out a loud roar and punched out.
The black spiral turned into a black fist shadow and smashed towards the high platform.
The moment the spiral came into contact with the platform, it immediately exploded.
"Rumble …"
It was accompanied by a violent roar.
The extremely condensed energy was released in an instant.
If it was in the outside world, the earth would have been shaken. Just the aftershock of this energy was enough to create a hurricane that could tear apart mountains.
However, in this place, other than the explosion, there was no other movement.
"My energy has been absorbed?" Chi Yi's expression changed slightly.
Ming Wei, Elder Jin, and the others' expressions didn't change at all. The power of a Primal Chaos Ancient God wasn't something that they could resist. Anything could happen here.
Hence, Ming Wei was very clear that they could only rely on Xiao Yun and Fu Chu.
"Let's go. We need to enter the center of these high platforms," Xiao Yun said.
After Chi Yi tried once, the other exiles didn't say anything else. Everyone entered from the bottom of the high platform.
In the depths of these high platforms …
Yan Hai's expression was gloomy. The black light was like the tongue of a poisonous snake that constantly flickered.
Half of the exiles standing beside him had already died at the hands of Ming Wei, Elder Jin, and the others.
It had to be said that these exiles from small clans were still too weak. Even with the warden's methods, they were still far from being able to resist Ming Wei and the others.
"Brother Yan Hai, what do we do now?" an exiled asked.
Apart from the Xuan Yuan Clan, the rest of the exiles from the small clans were in low spirits.
They wanted to rely on their own strength to hold the large clans back in this world. However, after truly facing them, they realized that their strength was nothing in front of the large clans. The gap between them was so big that it was impossible to make up for it …
"It's about time," Yan Lai said calmly.
When the other exiles heard this, they looked at each other. They didn't know what Yan Lai meant.
Yan Lai turned his head to look at the center of these high platforms. A black jade pendant appeared in his hand. He stretched out his hand and gently pinched it. With a "crack", the jade pendant shattered into pieces.
The moment the jade pendant shattered, thousands of miles away from where they were …
On a circular plate with a diameter of more than ten thousand feet, hundreds of people in black cloaks stood.
In the center of this circular plate was a stooped old man.
This old man was only left with a layer of skin. His face was like a skeleton. He was a member of the warden's clan and also the leader of the Conservative Faction.
"I didn't think that the two little bastards we left behind would still attract them …" the old man muttered.
His voice was like the buzz of a mosquito, but it was clearly transmitted to everyone's ears.
"Yan Lai's group is still too useless. They can't stop them at all," a purple youth beside the old man said with a frown.
The old man chuckled. "There was originally a gap in their strength. When Ming Wei and the others discovered the two little bastards, I knew that this day would come."
"Clan Leader, should we activate All Lives Equal now?" A clear female voice came from the black cloak.
The old man sighed deeply. His dim eyes flashed with a trace of light. After a long sigh, he finally made up his mind and nodded slightly.

Chapter 2224
As the guardians of this world, the Warden Clan wasn't very powerful.
Their duty was only to maintain the operation of this world. Most of the time, they didn't need to personally appear.
The space that Ruin opened up for them was enough to ensure their safety. They only needed to send their shadows to patrol this world. No matter how strong the exiles were, they couldn't do anything to them.
If it weren't for the conflict between the liberal faction and the conservative faction in the warden's family, neither the Nuwa Clan nor the Chi You Clan would have found this place …
As the disk slowly rotated, a light blue pattern appeared on the disk and gradually spread to the entire disk.
"I thought that as long as we persevered for three more years, the remnants of the Liberal Faction would be wiped out. I didn't expect things to end up like this. This is also the fate of our Warden Clan …" The old man sighed.
"The Liberal Faction went against Master's will. We firmly believe that they will fail," the female voice under the cloak said.
"The purpose of our Warden Clan's existence is to maintain the operation of this world. We can't fail …" Another voice said.
There were many conflicts in this world. There was no absolute right or wrong.
The Liberal Faction was only pursuing their own goals, while the Conservative Faction was protecting Ruin's orders. The exiles wanted to escape this world, while Luo Zheng wanted to return to the Divine Realm …
"Kacha!"
When the disk rotated to a certain degree, it suddenly let out a crisp sound.
At this moment, the light blue pattern completely filled the disk. If one looked down from above, they would see that the pattern formed the shape of a three-eyed monkey …
"Weng weng weng …"
As the light blue light continued to float, it floated out of the disk and turned into the shadow of a three-eyed monkey.
"Greetings, Master!"
All the members of the Warden Clan on the disk knelt on the ground.
The old man's expression was solemn and pious. Although the others were shrouded in black robes, they presumably had the same expression as the old man.
They knew that Ruin wasn't in this world. Their Master had left a long time ago, but their Master was still sacred in their hearts.
"Squeak …"
The phantom of the three-eyed monkey suddenly opened its big mouth and issued a mournful scream. This scream spread far and wide.
Along with the scream, countless light spots sprayed out from the mouth of the three-eyed monkey and began to spread in all directions.
When these light spots swept past the people on the disc, they struck into their bodies.
Even the old man standing at the center of the disc was unable to avoid it.
When the old man was hit by the blue light spot, his body suddenly trembled. Then, he stretched out his hand and clenched his fist. He smiled helplessly and said, "My strength has weakened a lot …"
The purple-haired young man also moved his body and said, "I feel a sense of powerlessness that I've never felt before. It's almost the same as those unarmed mortals …"
"I don't like this feeling," the female voice under the cloak said.
The old man smiled faintly. "Who would like that? If we were to face Ming Wei and the others head-on, I'm afraid we would lose without a doubt. Now, everyone is standing on the same starting line. Moreover, we have the weapons that Master has bestowed upon us. "
These light spots first began to spread from the disk, and then rippled outwards in circles.
It didn't take long for the dots of light to reach Yan Hai's location. The dots of light struck Yan Hai and the other exiles.
Yan Hai's body suddenly trembled, and his eyes revealed a trace of disbelief.
He felt that his soul, strength, and body were rapidly weakening. He couldn't even sense the Nirvana Realm at all, and his connection with the world in his body had been severed.
"This, this is …" Yan Hai's body was trembling.
The other exiles were the same as Yan Hai. Apart from trembling, a trace of fear appeared on their faces.
After all, at this moment, they had almost lost all of their strength.
The warden's family had once told Yan Hai that if Yan Hai led these exiles from the smaller clans and failed to ambush them, the warden's family would intervene. However, Yan Hai didn't expect the warden's family to use such a method.
"What kind of remarkable power is this? What's their motive for suppressing our strength to this extent? "An exiled from a smaller clan asked in confusion.
An exiled from the Xuanyuan Clan thought for a moment, and a trace of a smile appeared on his face. "Our strength has been suppressed. I think … Ming Wei and the others can't escape either."
Upon hearing this, Yan Hai and the other exiles' eyes lit up. They immediately understood the intentions of the warden's family.
The blue spots of light were still spreading. This energy seemed very gentle and harmless, but in reality, it was irresistible.
Below the high platform, Ming Wei was still walking in front, and the others followed closely behind.
At this moment, an ear-piercing roar suddenly sounded in everyone's ears.
"Look, what's that?"
Someone in the crowd was the first to discover the blue spots of light.
Ming Wei's gaze swept past the blue spots of light, and her eyebrows raised slightly. The tip of her foot lightly touched the ground, and her figure quickly retreated backward.
However, the speed at which these spots of light were spreading was extremely fast. In a situation where she couldn't use Space Diversion, even she couldn't avoid these spots of light.
The moment these spots of light entered her body, she let out a muffled groan. Her legs gave way, and she almost knelt on the ground.
The others were in a similar situation.
The moment they came into contact with these spots of light, they instantly felt their strength draining away. The extent of the decline was far beyond their imagination.
"What's going on?"
"I feel unprecedentedly weak!"
"…"
Elder Jin, Chi Yi, Fu Er, and the other exiles' expressions suddenly became very ugly.
"This, is what you mean by 'all living beings are equal'?"
Luo Zheng stood where he was, and after feeling the changes in his body, he asked 9527.
When the spots of light entered his body, he felt as if he had lost everything.
His once indestructible body had now become extremely weak, and was no different from a mortal.
Because 9527 had already mentioned 'all living beings are equal', he wasn't that surprised.
"Yes, after this Truth God Ability is activated, all living beings in this space will be reduced to the same level. Look at Ming Wei from before, her current strength is about the same as yours …" 9527 said.
Hearing this, Luo Zheng smiled bitterly. "This isn't good news."

Chapter 2225
Ming Wei looked at her own hands with a serious expression that had never been seen before on her face.
"How could this be?" Ming Wei said in a daze.
Fu Chu, who was beside her, was puzzled. When the Liberals and Conservatives were fighting, he was still young and did not understand many things about the Warden Clan.
Old General Jin waved his hands and gathered them in his Dantian. He took a deep breath and was about to forcefully activate the energy in his Inner World.
However, his face was red and his Dantian was like a stone ox that fell into the sea. There was no reaction at all.
Many of the Exiles tried. Whether it was using the power of the soul or pure power, or even trying to sense the other side, there was no reaction …
In the end, everyone's attention was focused on Little Yun.
Little Yun looked in the direction of the scream and said calmly, "It's the Equality of All Life. They really activated it …"
"What do you mean?" Ming Wei asked with a solemn expression. "They can directly weaken our power?"
If the warden's family had such means, there was no need for them to be afraid of anyone. In their current state, they could only be slaughtered. If Yan Hai were to attack now, he could kill everyone in the blink of an eye!
This ability was so overbearing that it could not be reasoned with …
"Not only did it weaken our power," Little Yun said. "It's the same for them. In other words, the power of all living beings in this space is on the same level. This is the so-called Equality of All Life."
Ming Wei was stunned for a moment. Her beautiful face showed a bitter smile. "Is that so …"
Little Yun nodded.
These Exiles, the Aragami Elders of the various clans, and the elites of the Aragami were all on the same level. In other words, they were not much stronger than ordinary mortals.
"It's still a big problem," an Exiled from the Star Deer Clan said.
"Now that we've come to this, we have no way out. If it's like what Little Yun said, that all life is equal, they will not be stronger than us. However, we still need to be prepared," Ming Wei said.
She quickly accepted this fact. She gently patted the jade bracelet on her wrist. She could use the Sumeru Space.
The jade bracelet flashed, and a bright and colorful ring appeared in her hand.
Clang!
The moment the ring appeared, it fell from his hand and smashed heavily onto the ground.
Ming Wei frowned and tried to pick up the ring from the ground, but it didn't budge at all …
Her current strength was not much stronger than a mortal's, and this ring was a famous magic treasure in the Mother World, the "Sky Ring Palm". This Sky Ring Palm was a relatively light magic treasure, but its actual weight was probably comparable to a mountain range.
Lifting a mountain with the strength of a mortal was simply wishful thinking …
The other exiles also tried, but those high-grade magic treasures were not light. In the past, they could naturally use them with ease, but now they had become a pile of trash.
In the end, they could only use some more practical weapons.
Ming Wei took out a black ring blade with sharp edges. This ring blade was a weapon she had used in her early years. She had not used it for many years. Back then, her cultivation was still very low. She had only kept this black ring blade as a memento. Now, it could finally be put to good use.
Elder Jin took out a strangely shaped large blade. Fu Er took out a silver-gray long spear. These were all ordinary weapons.
If some exiles did not have suitable weapons, they could only borrow them from each other.
Luo Zheng searched the Sumeru Ring for a while, but did not find any suitable weapons.
In order to enter the Realm of the Gods, he could only leave the weapons he had obtained in the universe with the Demi-Immortal Clan. After all, after entering the Realm of the Gods, everything in the universe would be restored to True Yuan.
The weapons he had obtained in the Realm of the Gods were also very important to Luo Zheng.
On the other hand, Little Yun took out a pile of weapons as if she was performing a magic trick for everyone to choose from …
Little Yun was familiar with the Warden Clan and naturally knew that they would activate All Lives Equal. In the two years she had been in the Barbarian Water Bone Pagoda, she had begun to collect some "scrap metal". Some of the Nuwa Clan members were rather puzzled by Little Yun's actions.
Now they understood that Little Yun had already expected this.
Luo Zheng chose from the pile of weapons. In the end, he took out a longsword and a flying knife that was as thin as a cicada's wing.
However, just as everyone was preparing, a roar suddenly came from the darkness in the depths of the high platform.
"Woo …"
Before this, the exiles' Divine Sense could cover a very large area. Nothing within this area could escape their detection.
Now that their souls had been greatly weakened, they were like blind people.
After hearing the roar, everyone looked toward the source of the sound. However, because they had lost their cultivation, even their eyesight had been greatly reduced. They could not see clearly at all!
"Woo woo …"
A figure slowly walked out from the shadows. His steps were swaying and very uncoordinated.
When the figure slowly walked out of the shadows, everyone could see clearly.
"It's just a sneaky person," an exiled from the Fire Melt Clan said disdainfully.
In this world, sneaky people were all too common. This kind of weak person should be the lowest living being in this world. The Aragami who were slightly more powerful would not even bother to pay attention to these people who wandered around the Eternal Wasteland. Moreover, these people were the most powerful beings in this world.
"Just a sneaky person? Have you forgotten your current strength? "another exiled retorted.
Only then did the exiled from the Fire Melt Clan remember Little Yun's words. If all lives were equal, then wouldn't this sneaky person's strength be about the same as his? Thinking of this, everyone felt very uncomfortable.
"And … it's not just one sneaky person," Ming Wei said with a frown as she lightly bit her lips.
"Woo woo woo woo …"
From the cracks below the high platforms, a few unsteady sneaky people slowly walked out. There were actually quite a lot of sneaky people here!
Those walking corpses without intelligence would tear apart any target that approached!
"Sneaky people have no intelligence and no weapons. We still have an advantage. Everyone, don't be discouraged." Even though Ming Wei understood the meaning of all lives were equal and knew that her strength was not enough to stand alone, she still stood in front of them without hesitation.

Chapter 2226
As the shadowy person charged forward, he used both his hands and legs to crawl on the ground.
This speed was like a snail in the eyes of Ming Wei and the others. They did not even consider him a threat.
But now, it seemed that his speed was not slow at all!
"Roar!"
The shadowy person howled crazily and charged towards Ming Wei.
Ming Wei's legs were straight as she gripped the ring blade in her hand tightly. When she saw the shadowy person charging in front of her, she suddenly stomped her legs and rolled her body. The ring blade in her hand was then sheathed over the shadowy person's head!
The ring blade was also a ring, but it was sharpened both inside and outside. It was very sharp.
After sheathing the ring blade, Ming Wei suddenly pulled. The shadowy person's neck was broken, and his head rolled onto the ground.
Her actions were clean and efficient. When the other exiles and Aragami saw this scene, they could not help but applaud.
Ming Wei was still Ming Wei. Even though her strength had been greatly reduced and she had become an ordinary person like the others, her previous experience and training were still present. Her strength was still not to be underestimated.
However, when she chopped off the shadowy person's head, a large amount of blood spurted out from the shadowy person's neck. It was hard for her to avoid it. The blood added a hint of killing intent to her white clothes.
Originally, when the others saw the shadowy people, they were in despair. When they heard Ming Wei's words and saw how easily she killed the shadowy person, the exiles immediately regained their confidence.
Even if their cultivation base was weakened, their strength was weakened, and even their soul energy was weakened, they still had a lot of experience. They had also learned a few powerful martial arts!
"Just shadowy people with no intelligence. What are they?! Everyone, charge! "
Elder Jin let out a muffled roar as he charged forward with his large blade.
"Flame Dragon Blade Technique!"
The large blade in Elder Jin's hand suddenly spun. The large blade was like a dragon piercing through the sun. It sliced open the chest of a shadowy person. After that, Elder Jin suddenly pulled out the large blade and twisted it with both hands. As the large blade spun, "puff puff puff", the heads of three shadowy people flew out …
The Flame Dragon Blade Technique was wide open and was extraordinarily exquisite.
"This blade technique is also inherited from the mother world, right?" Luo Zheng was also very envious when he saw this saber technique. He had not expected that these exiles not only had powerful cultivation, but also the most basic martial arts techniques were so exquisite …
In addition to Elder Jin, Fu Er's spear technique was also quite powerful. His spear was like a dragon swimming in water. Within three zhang, no one could get close to him.
The Yin people in the darkness surged out like a tide, their numbers increasing.
The exiles present all displayed their amazing martial skills. They were like a row of indestructible dams, firmly blocking the sneaky people in front of them.
But soon, Luo Zheng heard a blood-curdling scream behind him.
It turned out that other than the ones in front, there were even more sneaky people quietly charging from behind!
Everyone's attention was focused on the front. By the time they reacted, a large group of shady people had already surrounded the people behind them.
One of the Aragami from the Nuwa Clan did not manage to dodge in time and was pushed to the ground by three to four sneaky people.
"Linghe!"
When Ming Wei turned her head and saw this scene, she became even more anxious.
Linghe was an elite disciple of the Nuwa Clan. In the past few years, she had displayed amazing strength and was also a junior that Ming Wei thought highly of.
Seeing her own disciple being pushed to the ground by the sneaky people, Ming Wei was anxious. However, she was facing a large number of sneaky people. How could she spare the time to help?
At this moment, a thin stream of light shot out.
Poof!
With a muffled sound, it had already pierced into the forehead of the sneaky person on top of Linghe.
At the same time, a figure followed closely behind. A few sharp sword rays shot out from the figure's hand, and the sneaky people's chests and necks had a bloody hole. They slowly fell to the side.
"Get up." Luo Zheng extended his hand to Linghe who was on the ground.
The sneaky people pounced on Linghe and bit her. Linghe's body was also full of wounds. However, they were not fatal. It was just that she was in a mess.
After being rescued by Luo Zheng, Linghe was still in shock.
"Luo Zheng?"
Ming Wei saw this from afar. She was quite surprised.
Although the Aragami of this world practiced martial arts, most of them were still preparing for a huge body. The standard of those Aragami was still a little rough …
Ming Wei knew that Luo Zheng did not come from this world. However, she did not expect his swordsmanship to be so exquisite.
After Luo Zheng rescued Linghe, he pulled out the flying dagger that was as thin as a cicada's wing. With a light flick of his arm, he once again rushed towards the sneaky people …
"Most of these sneaky people are crawling. Although they are fast, their necks are exposed …"
With that in mind, Luo Zheng's long sword spun and swept towards the necks of these sneaky people!
Although the 'scrap metal' that Little Yun had prepared were ordinary items, this sword was still very sharp. It was easy to use.
Poof poof poof poof poof …
The tip of the sword left streaks of blood on the back of these sneaky people's necks.
These ferocious sneaky people could not crawl for more than a few steps. Their heads drooped and they were paralyzed on the ground. They could only let out a dry cry. However, their bodies were already paralyzed. They could only wait for their blood to run out.
The efficiency of Luo Zheng's method was shockingly high.
Although there were many sneaky people coming from the back, Luo Zheng had rushed into the middle of these sneaky people. With a swing of his sword, seventy to eighty sneaky people were paralyzed in the hands of Luo Zheng. He alone had blocked almost half of the sneaky people coming from the back!
Some of the exiles had also noticed the brilliance of Luo Zheng. Previously, they had used all kinds of methods to kill the sneaky people. They would either pierce their hearts or smash their heads. Naturally, their efficiency was not high.
Now that they saw that Luo Zheng was only cutting off the back of their necks, their efficiency was shocking. They immediately understood and began to imitate him.
In less than half an hour, this group of exiles had slaughtered three to four thousand sneaky people!
"Woo woo woo woo woo woo …"
However, the number of sneaky people did not seem to be decreasing. Instead, it was increasing!
"Help!"
At this moment, an Aragami elder from the Star Deer Tribe suddenly shouted for help.
This elder was directly dragged into a large group of sneaky people. In the blink of an eye, he was dragged away by those sneaky people and disappeared without a trace. Only the two words' help 'could be heard intermittently in the chaotic howls. Finally, the voice disappeared as well.
"Ah ah …"
At the same time that the elder from the Star Deer Tribe was being dragged away, another Aragami was being dragged away from another side.
The casualties that began to appear one after another made everyone's hearts heavy.
Kacha!
Ming Wei slashed the ring blade down heavily and directly broke the neck of a sneaky person.
As the blood splattered, her extremely charming face revealed a strong killing intent. "This can't go on. We can't stay here forever!"

Chapter 2227
After entering this world, Ming Wei and the others became the main force.
She had never set her eyes on Luo Zheng. After all, the gap between their cultivation bases was too big, and it was impossible to make up for it. Therefore, Luo Zheng, the other elders, and the Aragami were the targets of her protection.
In the beginning, Ming Wei even planned to only let the exiles, Fu Chu, and Xiao Yun enter this world. After all, the others were burdens in the eyes of the exiles!
However, after 'All Lives Equal', Luo Zheng's strength was no weaker than those exiles. He was even stronger than Ming Wei, Elder Jin, and the others.
Now that they were in urgent need of someone to clear the way, Ming Wei naturally called for Luo Zheng.
"Alright!"
Luo Zheng did not hesitate. His body flashed, and he moved from behind.
Facing an endless stream of sneaky people, it was only a matter of time before they died of exhaustion. Now, they could only gather their strength and fight their way out!
After that, Ming Wei assigned two more exiles from the big clans to cover the rear, and the group began to move forward!
"Skylight Spear!"
Fu Er was now at the forefront. His silver-gray spear was like a shuttle, and he stabbed more than ten times in an instant.
"Wuwuwu …"
More than ten sneaky people screamed and fell to the ground.
Then, the two exiles from the Nuwa Clan charged up as well. One of them wielded a dozen short swords, while the other wielded a strangely shaped guillotine. After a round of attacks, the two of them killed another dozens of Yin people.
Then, it was Elder Jin, Chi Yi, and the others …
These exiles took turns to attack, and a bloody path was created in front of them.
The people behind them stepped on the corpses of the sneaky people!
Luo Zheng did not hesitate at all.
Although he could not use the energy from the world in his body, his swordsmanship had been refined to the extreme in the universe.
Now, as his body floated, the longsword in his hand attacked the back of the sneaky people's necks in the simplest way.
After fighting for a while, he and Ming Wei rushed to the front.
"Wuwuwuwu …"
Ming Wei, who was at the front, was facing the sneaky people who were surrounding her. Her eyes flashed, and she lightly tapped the ground with the tip of her foot and shouted.
Swoosh!
With a sudden pull of the ring blade, the necks of the sneaky people in front of her were all cut off.
But this time, she had overlooked a sinister person beside her. That sinister person quietly approached her, opened his mouth, and bit towards Ming Wei's snow-white neck!
Under the influence of All Lives Equal, even Ming Wei would be badly mutilated if she was bitten!
Her expression changed. She knew that she couldn't avoid it. She could only take the bite.
Bang!
However, when his mouth was still two inches away from her neck, the flying dagger that was as thin as a cicada's wing once again sank into his forehead …
Seeing the person slowly fall, Ming Wei's face revealed a faint smile. She had never thought that she would actually be saved by a True God Stage junior.
Then, she used the ring blade in her hand to gently hook out the throwing knife and threw it back to Luo Zheng.
The two of them remained silent as they continued to carve out a bloody path in front of them.
Although the number of sneaky people didn't decrease, because these sneaky people didn't have any intelligence, they just charged forward mindlessly.
Meanwhile, the exiles and the Aragami, after experiencing the initial panic, had quickly calmed down!
They were also getting more and more familiar with the means to fight against these sneaky people. In the beginning, they were injured, and six of them had fallen. Later on, although some of them were injured, none of them had fallen.
Just like that, they continued to fight and move forward for dozens of miles.
The number of sneaky people gradually decreased. From the initial swarm, they were scattered at the end. Finally, there were no more sneaky people.
Meanwhile, at this time, everyone stopped moving forward …
It was not that they didn't want to seize this opportunity to continue their journey. It was just that the 'All Lives Equal' had weakened them in all aspects. Even their physical strength had been greatly weakened. Along the way, it could be said that most of them had exhausted their physical strength to the limit.
When many of them saw that the sneaky people were gone, they immediately relaxed and fell to the ground. They didn't even want to move their fingers anymore.
Even those exiles who were good at using their skills and preserving their physical strength were panting heavily.
Even Jin Lao, Ming Wei, and the others were no exception.
Ming Wei's body was covered with a layer of blood from the sneaky people. She looked like a bloody person. She lifted up a corner of her long dress and twisted it hard. With a 'splash', blood gushed out from her dress …
When she was wringing the blood away, Ming Wei's gaze quietly fell on Luo Zheng who was opposite her. She looked a little surprised.
After 'All Lives Equal', everyone's physical strength should be the same. Many of the exiles were exhausted. However, Luo Zheng was still calm and composed.
"You … Aren't you tired?" Ming Wei asked curiously.
"I'm fine." Luo Zheng smiled slightly.
The number of sneaky people that he had killed was not less than Ming Wei. It was very likely that he had killed the most of them.
However, Luo Zheng's swordsmanship was very special. The swordsmanship that he had practiced before was the most concise. He used the simplest movements to achieve the best effect. Every time he attacked with his sword, the consumption of his physical strength was almost negligible. Naturally, his complexion was much better than the others!
"Let's take a rest." Ming Wei said calmly, "We definitely can't continue our journey now."
These powerful exiles had to rest like ordinary mortals. Otherwise, they would not be able to recover their physical strength at all …
In the eyes of many, this was a very ridiculous thing. After all, the physical strength that they consumed was not even a billionth of their physical strength before. However, this' All Lives Equal 'was unreasonable. They had to understand that they were not much stronger than ordinary mortals now.
"Alright, I'll keep watch." Luo Zheng nodded.
Ming Wei nodded slightly and found a corner. She leaned against the pillar below the high platform and sat down with her legs crossed. She closed her eyes helplessly. She could not even remember how many thousands of years it had been since she closed her eyes to sleep.
Perhaps she was too tired. In just two or three breaths, she had already fallen into a deep sleep.
As for the other exiles and the Aragami, they were already snoring loudly.
Luo Zheng stabbed his long sword into the ground and glanced at these exiles. A smile appeared on his face. To be able to exhaust the energy of these exiles, it was indeed a very strange thing …
Now that everyone had fallen into a deep sleep, Luo Zheng sat cross-legged and quietly began to activate his Bloodline Divine Ability.
During the chaotic battle just now, 9527 had urged him many times to activate his Bloodline Divine Ability and use the [Falling Star Palm] to defend against the enemies …
All Lives Equal was also a Truth Divine Ability.
Under the Truth Divine Ability, all means could not be resisted.
Therefore, whether it was the soul, the body, the Life Yuan in the inner world, or the power of the Aragami, they were all 'equal'.
Even their vision and physical strength were all equal.

Chapter 2228
In the entire Chaos, the power of a higher level would suppress the power of a lower level.
And these Chaos Primeval Gods had the highest level of power.
Just like 'All Lives Equal', it could suppress everything.
No matter how strong these Exiles were, they were not much stronger than mortals now. Even the power they gained from the Paramita could not compete with 'All Lives Equal'.
But Luo Zheng's bloodline ability was also a Truth Ability, so it could naturally avoid the suppression of 'All Lives Equal'.
After 'All Lives Equal', Luo Zheng finally understood what 9527 meant.
Although the power contained in his bloodline ability was weak, the level of this power was extremely high …
"Hum …"
A very soft sound came from Luo Zheng's body.
Then there was a little green light, which gently pulsated from Luo Zheng's palm. The green light pulsated slowly and quickly turned into a small green cube.
Now Luo Zheng did not know what kind of power the bloodline ability contained in this bloodline ability was. But under the guidance of 9527, Luo Zheng could use the 'Falling Star Hand'.
He turned his palm slightly, and the small green cube slowly spread and merged into his palm. His entire palm suddenly turned green. At the same time, he looked into the distance and pinched his fingers slightly …
Before the 'Falling Star Hand' could be used, the green light in his hand had already dissipated.
Luo Zheng did not want to cause too much of a commotion.
Since the warden family had activated 'All Lives Equal', they must have corresponding methods. Before the warden family used those methods, Luo Zheng would not easily expose his methods.
"Snore, snore …"
"Snore, snore, snore …"
The Exiles and the Aragami were still sleeping. Some of them were even snoring.
These people probably had not slept so well for a long time.
Four to five hours later …
Ming Wei was the first to wake up. When she woke up, she habitually became alert and almost reflexively turned over and got up.
She never thought that she would sleep so soundly. This was simply unimaginable in the past.
When Ming Wei saw Luo Zheng who was still standing guard, she was relieved and asked at the same time, "How long has it been?"
"Almost ten hours," Luo Zheng answered.
"I'm sorry, I didn't expect to not wake up from my sleep," Ming Wei said as she glanced at the exiles who were sleeping in a disorderly manner. She smiled helplessly. "It seems that everyone is like that."
Although everyone was treated as equals, in reality, there were still some slight differences.
Ming Wei's energy recovery speed was much faster than the others, so she was the first to wake up.
"Aren't you going to rest?" Ming Wei glanced at Luo Zheng.
"I'm not sleepy." Luo Zheng shook his head.
At first, Luo Zheng didn't feel so tired because he had consumed less energy than the others. However, after he circulated his bloodline ability, he felt even more energetic.
Now that he thought about it carefully, the reason he could maintain such a state was probably because he had transplanted the bloodline of a Primal Chaos Ancient God.
Ming Wei looked at Luo Zheng in surprise.
Ever since Old Jin brought Luo Zheng to the door, Ming Wei was very curious about Luo Zheng.
However, Luo Zheng was still a True God-level junior after all. Although his talent surprised her, his strength was still not enough to catch her attention.
She led everyone into this world. Along the way, she did not notice this young man because he did not even have the chance to make a move.
She did not expect that after 'All Lives Are Equal', the strength that this kid showed would surprise her.
Although the exiles were treated as equals, they were still more powerful than the Aragami with their strong foundation. However, Luo Zheng was only a True God-level cultivator …
Under such curiosity, she was also curious about Luo Zheng.
She took two steps forward, folded her dress that was stained with blood and sat beside Luo Zheng. Then she asked, "You seem to want to leave this world too?"
Other than the exiles, many of the Aragami were not willing to leave this place. After all, this was the homeland of the Aragami. If they were to leave the vast amount of desolate bones in this place, even their cultivation would become extremely difficult!
Luo Zheng sat where he was, looking at the layers of huge platforms in the distance. He smiled faintly. "Of course."
He had left the Divine Dominion for a few years. He knew nothing about the situation in the Divine Dominion.
However, judging from the situation when he left, how could Luo Zheng not be worried?
Would his mother fall into the hands of Dongfang Chunjun? Or even fall together with the Sages?
What about Luo Yan, Xun, Ning Yudie and the others in the Divine Dominion Mansion? These were all things that Luo Zheng missed.
It was better if Ming Wei did not ask. As soon as she asked, it immediately evoked the worry and longing in his heart. There was a hint of sadness in his eyes.
"It seems that there's someone very important to you on that ship?" Ming Wei asked.
Ming Wei's initial plan was to gather the power of all the exiles of the Nuwa Clan to pull that ship down by force …
"Of course." Luo Zheng nodded. "All my family members are there, and so are my enemies."
Ming Wei had some understanding of the origin of the Divine Dominion. Of course, she also knew about the grudge between Chi You and the Dihong Clan. However, after being imprisoned in this prison, she did not know about the subsequent movements of the Mother World.
However, now Ming Wei naturally knew that the powerful Jiuli Clan had declined. It even forced Chi You to build that ship and escape from the Mother World.
"The enemies should be from the Dihong Clan, right? Or the Xuan Yuan Wei? "Ming Wei asked again.
In this world, the Xuan Yuan Clan, the Nuwa Clan, and so on were just simple names. These big clans were not called that in the Mother World.
"Yes, and no … However, all of this is because of the Xuan Yuan Wei," Luo Zheng said.
Luo Zheng had never seen the Xuan Yuan Wei, but the Divine Dominion had fallen to its current state because of the Xuan Yuan Wei. Dongfang Chunjun could be considered to have been supported by the Xuan Yuan Wei …
If he returned to the Divine Dominion, he would have to face them sooner or later.
"If we can leave this world, I'll help you kill them all." Ming Wei smiled faintly.
Although she did not know the strength of the Xuan Yuan Wei, Ming Wei was still very confident.
"You're willing to interfere?" Luo Zheng's expression showed a trace of surprise.
Luo Zheng had never been to the Mother World, but he knew that the competition between these big clans was filled with all kinds of benefits.
If he could really leave this world, he naturally hoped that Elder Jin, Fu Er, Chi Yi, and the other Chi You experts would return to the Divine Dominion with him. He did not expect Ming Wei to take the initiative to ask for help.
"Why not?" Ming Wei smiled faintly at Luo Zheng. There seemed to be flames dancing in her eyes. "As long as we can leave this world …"

Chapter 2229
Before this, Ming Wei was confident that she could crush them with her own strength.
In her eyes, the wardens were like insects hiding in the dark. She could not find them.

At that moment, the other exiles and the Aragami had also awakened one after another.
After resting for a while, they continued to move forward …
Deep in the high platform, Yan Hai and the others were quietly walking on another path.

In the hands of these exiles, there was a jet-black crossbow.
This was a crossbow used by mortals. The bowstring was already fixed, and the black arrow was already placed on the string. The arrowhead was flashing with a faint black light!
In the eyes of the exiles, these crossbows were weapons that posed no threat. In the past, even if they were shot by a thousand or ten thousand arrows, the exiles and even the Aragami would not be harmed at all.
However, now, these crossbows had a fatal lethality.
"Understood." One of the exiles showed a sinister smile on his face.
For some of them, leaving this world was not that important anymore. If they could kill Ming Wei and the others, they would be satisfied.
Yan Hai opened a cloth bag and took out pieces of black medicinal biscuits. These medicinal biscuits were emitting a pungent smell.

These methods were extremely unorthodox in the eyes of the exiles. However, they were extremely important after 'All Lives Equal' was activated!
The exiles did not say anything and after receiving the biscuits from Yan Hai, they swallowed them.
The taste of this medicinal cake was obviously very bad. Everyone frowned as they swallowed it.
After making the necessary preparations, Yan Hai said, "They are almost here. Di Jin, you lead the three of them to the Ding Chou position. Du Man, you go to the Xin Wu position …"
All the high platforms in this world were the Heavenly stems and earthly branches.
Between these high platforms, there was a large number of prisoners. Once Ming Wei and the others entered, they would release the prisoners.

Between the two high platforms at Ding and Chou were iron fences as thick as an arm. Thousands of sneaky people were trapped inside.

"Big Brother Di Jin, what do you think is going on with these sneaky people?" One of the exiles could not help but mumble.
They had seen many sneaky people, but the origin and purpose of these sneaky people had always been an unsolved mystery.
Now that they saw these seemingly living things without any signs of life, these exiles also felt that it was strange.
Di Jin glanced at the immobilized sneaky people and shook his head …
Even though they had crossed over to the other side, it was still difficult for them to understand the various actions of the Chaos Ancient Gods. The things behind these things were simply not something they could understand.
"Let's not care so much. Everyone, don't talk now and get ready." Di Jin said in a low voice, "Victory or defeat depends on this. If we succeed, we will still have a chance to leave this world …"
They hid at the side and did not make a sound.
After waiting here for more than an hour, a series of scattered footsteps came from afar.
Di Jin and the others looked towards the source of the sound and saw some figures walking over together.
"They're here." Di Jin said in a low voice.
At this moment, the fences below the high platform suddenly made a crisp "patter" sound.
The mechanisms in the fences started to move. The huge fences instantly shrank towards the ground. At the same time, all the statue-like sneaky people turned from statues into living creatures in an instant. They made "wuwu" sounds and pounced towards Di Jin and the rest.
When one of the exiles saw this scene, his expression tensed up. He raised the bow in his hand and was about to shoot these sneaky people. Seeing this, Di Jin put one hand on the bow and stopped that exiled. "Don't!"
After those sneaky people pounced over, they probably smelled the medicine on Di Jin and the rest and were unwilling to take even half a step forward. They only roared at them and immediately changed direction and rushed in another direction.
Seeing this scene, Di Jin and the rest heaved a sigh of relief. The medicine cakes that Yan Hai had given them were very effective.
On the other side …
"They're here again!"
When Luo Zheng, Ming Wei, and the rest saw the sneaky people rushing over, they didn't seem to panic.
Everyone raised the weapons in their hands and soon converged with this group of sneaky people …
After resting for several hours, everyone was full of energy. The situation was one-sided. These sneaky people were almost unable to get close and were quickly killed by the exiles!
"Puff puff puff puff puff …"
Ming Wei was like a dexterous elf, nimbly strolling among the sneaky people. The ring blade in her hand didn't seem to be a weapon, but a prop used for dancing. She could inadvertently reap the lives of sneaky people.
Not far from her, Luo Zheng's efficiency in killing sneaky people was no less than hers. These two people took the lead and took almost half of the pressure, reaping the majority of the sneaky people's lives.
But just as they were killing to their heart's content, a few crossbows were quietly raised in the shadows behind the high platform.
Di Jin, who was hiding in the darkness, aimed at Ming Wei at the front. As for the other three people, they each locked on to their own targets.
As soon as Di Jin gave the order, everyone pulled the trigger.
"Whoosh whoosh whoosh whoosh …"

Chapter 2230
Out of the four arrows that were shot out from the darkness, three of them were headed straight for Ming Wei, and one of them was headed straight for Luo Zheng.

After 'All Lives Are Equal', her perception had been greatly reduced. When she noticed the three arrows, they were only seven to eight feet away from her …

This step of his was three feet and three inches long. It was as long as a sword, but it was complicated and ever-changing.

However, they didn't know that there was no such thing as a 'True God' in the universe. Everyone entered the Dao through martial arts. Practicing martial arts was the foundation of their cultivation!

Chapter 2231
Just as Di Jin pulled out his saber, Luo Zheng had already passed by those shady people, holding his long sword, and rushed into the shadows.

Clang!

As his voice fell, the saber in his hand flipped, and he slashed at Luo Zheng's head!
Clang! Clang! Clang!

After becoming a strong person in the Nirvana Realm, Di Jin lived a peaceful life for many years. After being imprisoned in this world, he rarely fought.
Now that he was in such a situation, his ferocious nature was aroused. He slashed out with his saber without the slightest intention of defending, only attacking fiercely.

Di Jin, on the other hand, was even more insatiable. He pressed hard and did not intend to give Luo Zheng the slightest chance to relax!
"Dang dang dang dang dang dang …"
Di Jin's Saber Momentum was becoming more and more ferocious, and the strength he used was also becoming greater and greater.
Luo Zheng's steps were steady. His long sword was like a door, blocking all of Di Jin's attacks.
His purpose of rushing in was to stop these people from releasing their hidden arrows. As long as Ming Wei, Elder Jin, and the rest led the people to block the attack, there would definitely be reinforcements from the other exiles!
As long as Luo Zheng was standing here, his purpose would be achieved.
But Luo Zheng did not expect Di Jin to be so hot-tempered. When he attacked, he was ready to risk his life!
