After all, Luo Zheng's body was not as strong as iron now …
Seeing Di Jin's saber coming straight for him, Luo Zheng's eyes flashed slightly. He suddenly changed his direction and twisted his body. His whole body was almost touching the edge of the saber to avoid it. With a "swish" sound, even his clothes were cut open by the saber!
While avoiding the saber, Luo Zheng raised his arm slightly and his elbow was facing Di Jin's face.
Di Jin's momentum was originally very fierce. Now, he was using his face to hit Luo Zheng's elbow head-on.
With a "crack" sound, Di Jin's nose was broken. His nose and mouth were badly mutilated. His whole body was in so much pain that he bent over …
Luo Zheng's expression was cold. After seizing this opportunity, he swung his long sword slightly and was about to stab Di Jin's back.
But at this time, the other three exiles also attacked Luo Zheng.
These three exiles could hardly keep up with the pace of Di Jin and Luo Zheng. But now that Di Jin stopped, they naturally found a chance to intervene!
"Ding, ding, ding!"
Luo Zheng's long sword suddenly became crooked. The seemingly casual sweep of the long sword turned out to be extremely precise, down to the millimeter!
The swords, axes, and daggers that were attacking him had been swept away.
The three exiles' expressions changed greatly. They did not expect Luo Zheng's swordsmanship to be so amazing. They were already a little scared.
However, Di Jin was their backbone, and they had no choice but to save him.
The three exiles had just been forced to retreat, but they braced themselves to attack again. This time, Luo Zheng's eyes were cold. If he wanted to kill Di Jin, he was afraid that he had to kill these three exiles first!
Thinking of this, the long sword in his hand suddenly sped up. Facing the exiled exiled with an axe in the front, Luo Zheng gently raised his long sword and chopped along the handle of the axe.
Seeing that his hands were about to be cut off by the long sword, the exiled exiled was so scared that he hurriedly threw away his axe. Luo Zheng took advantage of this opportunity and the tip of his sword went straight to the exiled exiled's face.
Seeing this scene, the other two exiles tried their best to save him and block Luo Zheng's sword.
But just as Luo Zheng's sword was about to stab out, it suddenly retracted. This sword was actually a feint. His target was actually the exiled exiled with a dagger!
"Puff!"
The long sword flashed a short sword light, which accurately swept across the exiled exiled's throat and cut open his neck. Blood gushed out from the exiled exiled's neck like a fountain.
"What a cunning boy!"
Seeing this scene, the two exiles with axes and swords showed sorrow on their faces.
Although the three of them came from different races, they took care of each other in this world and had long become sworn brothers. They had a very good relationship for so many years, so naturally, they were as close as brothers.
Now that one of them had his neck chopped off by Luo Zheng, they were naturally shocked and angry.
If their cultivation was high, this injury would be nothing. But now, with their bodies not much stronger than ordinary people, this injury was enough to kill them!
"Ah ah ah!"
The remaining two exiles roared wildly and rushed toward Luo Zheng without any regard for their own safety.
At the same time, Di Jin, who had caught his breath, wiped off the blood on his face and charged forward with his saber. Three exiles risking their lives against a mere True God. It sounded unimaginable, but it was happening right before their eyes.
Facing these three guys who didn't care about their lives, Luo Zheng suddenly felt the pressure double. As he kept retreating, he was actually surrounded by danger!
But at this moment, dense footsteps came from not far away.

Chapter 2232
Elder Jin, Chi Yi, Fu Er, and the others didn't immediately rush to the back of the high platform.
They had killed their way over, attracting the attention of most of the sinister people.
Although they were worried about Luo Zheng's safety, they had no choice but to deal with the sinister people behind them first. After all, they didn't know that because Di Jin and the others had swallowed the medicinal cakes, the sinister people wouldn't follow them into the shadows behind the high platform.
Behind the huge pillar of the high platform, Di Jin's face twitched slightly.
He realized that if he didn't get rid of Luo Zheng, they wouldn't be able to escape.
It wasn't a pity for these exiles from small clans to die here, but Di Jin didn't want to be buried here!
As this thought flashed through his mind, his attacks became even fiercer!
"Die!"
Di Jin fiercely slashed with his saber with all the strength in his body.
As Luo Zheng continuously retreated, he used his sword to block in front of him as usual.
However, everyone's physical strength had been greatly weakened. Everyone's strength was almost the same. Although Luo Zheng could use his skills to the extreme, his sword couldn't block the momentum of Di Jin's saber!
"Clang!"
With a crisp sound, Luo Zheng's long sword flew out of his hand!
"Good chance!"
The other two exiles had already thrown caution to the wind, but Luo Zheng was so tenacious that they couldn't take him down even after a long time. They were extremely anxious.
Seeing that Luo Zheng's troublesome sword was sent flying by Di Jin's saber, the two exiles' hearts thumped wildly. They quickly closed in on him!
A large axe and a sharp sword greeted Luo Zheng head-on.
Di Jin also followed with his saber in hand. A trace of a sinister smile appeared on his face. He was waiting for this brat to be hit by the sword, then he would cut off his head …
Even though Luo Zheng retreated at lightning speed, he couldn't block the attack with his bare hands.
However, at this moment, Luo Zheng suddenly revealed a faint smile. His gaze pierced through the two exiles and fixed on Di Jin. He quietly raised his hand.
"You're about to die, yet you still want to put on an act?" Di Jin sneered.
All living things were equal. Even exiles like them couldn't surpass this principle. He couldn't think of any other tricks Luo Zheng could use, and it was impossible for him to use any tricks.
But just as the axe was about to chop down on Luo Zheng's head, a green light suddenly appeared in Luo Zheng's palm.
"Buzz buzz buzz …"
This energy was very weak, like a mosquito buzzing softly.
In the blink of an eye, it formed a small green cube. Then, the green cube quickly collapsed and dispersed on Luo Zheng's hand.
As a result, Luo Zheng had a green right hand!
Then, Luo Zheng suddenly stopped and stopped retreating. His hand actually went to grab the big axe!
Although the axe was made of ordinary metal, after the Equality of All Lives, anyone who was hit by the axe would be cut in half. Moreover, the Exiled Outcast had used up all his strength.
But Luo Zheng's right hand suddenly grabbed the axe's head so easily!
The Exiled Outcast holding the axe was stunned. He grabbed the axe's handle and pulled with all his strength, but the axe didn't move at all, as if it was embedded in a rock.
"I wonder who's about to die?"
Luo Zheng cracked a smile and squeezed his right hand lightly.
The axe made of fine metal was squeezed out of shape by Luo Zheng …
Di Jin and the other Exiled Outcast holding a sword were dumbfounded when they saw this scene.
Although their perception was weakened, their eyesight was still very accurate. They knew that Luo Zheng used some kind of energy.
But now, under the Equality of All Lives, even Nirvana Realm experts like them couldn't escape. How did Luo Zheng do it?
At this moment, they thought that their eyes were playing tricks on them.
Their eyes might have been playing tricks on them, but they couldn't be wrong about the power that Luo Zheng burst out with with a single hand!
While the Exiled Outcast was still in a daze, Luo Zheng had already snatched the axe and swung it fiercely.
"Bang …"
The axe's handle, which was as thick as an arm, smashed into the Exiled Outcast. How could the Exiled Outcast withstand it?
His skull instantly cracked, and his brain matter flowed out. He fell on his back on the ground …
Since Luo Zheng chose to use the supernatural power in his bloodline, he naturally wouldn't leave anyone alive!
"Bang!"
He continued to grab the axe and swing the handle.
The Exiled Outcast holding a sword came back to his senses. Just as he was about to turn around and dodge, the axe's handle smashed heavily on his head again.
Now, only Di Jin was left.
"You, how did you do it? The Equality of All Lives didn't work on you? "Di Jin remained calm on the surface, but his voice was still slightly trembling.
"Of course it worked." Luo Zheng's right hand gently kneaded the axe. The axe made of fine metal was like a lump of dough, changing its shape as he wantonly kneaded it. "But I have a way to surpass the Equality of All Lives …"
Surpassing the Equality of All Lives was equivalent to breaking the rules established by the Primal Chaos Ancient Gods!
Di Jin's face was full of disbelief, but when he saw Luo Zheng's right hand emitting a green light, he had no choice but to believe it.
At that moment, Di Jin suddenly clenched his teeth and vigorously swung the saber in his hand. Then, he immediately turned around and ran …
"Clang!"
Luo Zheng raised the axe's handle and gently pushed the saber away.
Seeing Di Jin's fleeing figure, he raised his right hand.
"Huff huff huff …"
As the axe continuously swung, it emitted a burst of sonic booms. Then, Luo Zheng threw it heavily.
Since he used the power of the Truth Crystal, he couldn't leave anyone alive.
The axe spun at high speed in the air and caught up to Di Jin's back in the blink of an eye.
Just as it was about to hit Di Jin, he suddenly lowered his body and the axe swept over his head.
Di Jin rejoiced in his heart. After all, Luo Zheng didn't chase after him. Now that he had put some distance between them, he still had a chance to escape …
But just as he stood up, he heard the sound of wind whistling in his ears.
"Not good …"
He knew something was wrong and felt a chill run down his back.
A flying dagger as thin as a cicada's wing was nailed into his back.
In the distance, Luo Zheng slowly lowered his right hand and shook it slightly. After the green light on the surface of his arm disappeared, he slowly walked towards Di Jin.
At that moment, Elder Jin and the others finally took care of the pursuers.
"Hurry up and help Luo Zheng!"
Elder Jin said in a deep voice. He quickly rushed to the back of the high platform.
But when they circled to the back of the high platform and took a closer look, they were immediately dumbfounded.
Luo Zheng was just pulling the flying dagger out of Di Jin's back. When he heard the commotion behind him, a faint smile appeared on his face.
"That's Di Jin. You took care of all four of them by yourself?" Chi Yi's face was full of disbelief.
As exiles from large clans, they clearly knew that even though All Life Is Equal, Di Jin wasn't easy to deal with.

Chapter 2233
Luo Zheng wiped the blood off the flying dagger and nodded to Chi Yi.
"How did you do it …" Chi Yi's pupils dilated slightly as he stared at Luo Zheng and continued to ask.
Everyone's strength was the same. Even if Luo Zheng relied on his extremely proficient and superb martial arts skills, it would be difficult for him to deal with these people in a short time.
Moreover, these people had hands and feet. Even if they were no match for Luo Zheng, they could always escape …
Elder Jin and the others were also full of doubts.
It was not difficult for them to kill these unintelligent people.
However, even Elder Jin could not kill these four exiles by himself. It could even be said that they had no chance of success.
"They are weaker than I imagined," Luo Zheng shrugged his shoulders.
At this stage, Luo Zheng did not want to explain too much.
Elder Jin, Chi Yi, and the others were obviously not satisfied with Luo Zheng's answer, but they could not say anything …
Moreover, the more powerful Luo Zheng was, the more advantageous it would be for them.
After Luo Zheng picked up his long sword, he also collected the crossbows used by Di Jin and the others.
These crossbows were indeed ordinary items, but they were still quite useful in this situation.
After sorting out the remaining exiles, the people behind him slowly caught up with him.
Surrounded by the crowd, Ming Wei strolled over. Her eyes swept over Di Jin's corpse, and her eyebrows raised slightly.
The exiles killed by Ming Wei, Elder Jin, and the others were all exiles from small clans. Their strength was not necessarily strong. Di Jin was a general of the Xuanyuan Clan, and his strength was far stronger than ordinary exiles. Ming Wei was naturally surprised that he had fallen here.
"You're quite efficient. Luo Zheng rushed in rashly and was not injured?" She looked at Luo Zheng, who was unscathed, and her words were full of concern. She also felt that Luo Zheng was too impulsive.
Elder Jin and the others looked at each other helplessly, laughed dryly, and then said, "We didn't do anything … When we came, Luo Zheng had already killed them all."
"What?" Ming Wei's eyes flashed.
The exiles from the Fire Melt Race, the Star Deer Race, and the other major races, as well as the Aragami, were all looking at Luo Zheng in surprise.
Luo Zheng smiled faintly at Ming Wei. He didn't explain too much. Instead, he pointed to the corpses on the ground and said, "These people must have swallowed some kind of special medicine that can emit this medicinal fragrance. Because of this medicinal fragrance, those evil people don't seem to dare to come close."
Ever since All Lives Are Equal, the shady people had indeed created quite a bit of trouble for them.
These corpses also emitted a medicinal fragrance, which could be used at a crucial moment.
It seemed that Luo Zheng still had a trump card that she didn't know about …
However, seeing that Luo Zheng was avoiding the topic, the corners of Ming Wei's mouth curled up slightly. She changed the topic to the corpses. "Since that's the case, we can use these corpses to avoid those evil people."
"Yes!" Luo Zheng nodded. He looked around at everyone and then said, "The enemy is hiding behind the high platform and sniping us like this. We can only be targets. We can't advance like this anymore."
The enemy had released a large number of evil people to rush at them. They really didn't have a good solution.
This time, if Luo Zheng didn't force his way forward, who knew how many of them would have been killed by Di Jin and the others …
It was indeed not a good idea to continue like this.
"Then what do you think we should do?" An old man from the Fire Melt Race mumbled and asked.
Ming Wei had always been the leader. Everyone naturally followed her lead. After all, she was the strongest. She could rely on her cultivation base to convince everyone.
Now, Ming Wei seemed to be following Luo Zheng's advice. Naturally, they were not used to it.
However, Luo Zheng had just taken a huge risk and killed four exiles by himself. He had already proven his strength. However, when they thought about Luo Zheng's' insignificant 'cultivation base, they still felt a little strange.
"Divide the team into two groups," Luo Zheng replied.
"Divide into two groups? How is it divided? "One of the exiles asked.
Luo Zheng smiled and said, "I'll be in one group and you guys will be in the other."
Some people were puzzled when they heard Luo Zheng's words.
Ming Wei was the first to understand. She frowned slightly and said, "Isn't it too dangerous for you to go alone? I can go with you. "
Even after 'All Lives Are Equal', Ming Wei's group was not afraid of Yan Hai's group. They had an absolute advantage in terms of numbers. In terms of strength … everyone was about the same.
However, the enemy was in the dark while they were in the open. This group of people was too big of a target. If this continued, they would probably be killed by the enemy wave after wave.
Luo Zheng's suggestion was to hide himself as well.
However, Ming Wei was naturally worried that Luo Zheng would act alone.
"Yes, I'm afraid you can't go alone. Fu Er, Ming Wei and I will go with you?" Elder Jin said from nearby.
Luo Zheng shook his head and said, "You're too conspicuous. If you're not in the team, they'll probably notice it immediately. It's safest for me to go alone."
After all, Luo Zheng had killed Di Jin and the other three. Although Elder Jin and the others did not support it, they still agreed to Luo Zheng's plan …
Under the densely packed high platforms, there were countless crisscrossing paths. It was very complicated.
As they continued forward, Luo Zheng was missing from the team …
He kept a distance of several hundred feet from these people and walked through the darkness on one side of the high platform.
After learning from their mistakes, everyone remained silent and vigilant. They were always on guard against any hidden arrows shot out from the darkness.
After Ming Wei led the team for more than ten li, there was a low murmur from behind another high platform.
"They're here. They're still led by Ming Wei …"
"Di Jin and the others didn't retreat. Did they fail?"
"I reckon Di Jin didn't escape. It looks like we can't continue fighting!"
According to Yan Hai's plan, a team of four people would kill Ming Wei and the others with crossbows. After that, they would immediately retreat and gather with the people behind them to carry out the next round of attack.
If they succeeded, they could exhaust the team led by Ming Wei to death in this world.
However, Di Jin and the others didn't escape. This caused the four people to have an ominous feeling in their hearts.
"Don't think too much. Release the traitors!" One of them ordered.
"Kacha …"
The fence between the two high platforms suddenly disappeared. The traitors imprisoned in the fence quickly rushed forward. The four people had already set up their crossbows.
Just as they aimed their crossbows in front of them, a green light suddenly flashed from the corners of their eyes.
"Where did the light come from?" One of the exiles was puzzled and turned his head.
Then he saw Luo Zheng standing not far away.

Chapter 2234
"Who are you?!"

On the other side.

Along the way, the exiles from the big clans and the Aragami were all looking more and more impressed.

Three days later...

"There's someone on the wall!" One of the Aragami shouted.
Everyone looked towards the source of the voice and saw several figures appearing one after another on the city wall.

Once upon a time in this world, even powerful exiles like Elder Jin and Yan Hai could only bow their heads in front of her.
But this time, when she was planning to leave this world, she had caused trouble for the Nuwa Clan several times.
And this time, it was also because of this person that she was injured by an arrow.
If she wasn't in a hurry to leave this world, she would have led the Nuwa Clan to destroy the Xuanyuan Clan...
Yan Hai stood on the wall and looked at the people in the distance with an indifferent expression.
His heart was filled with doubts.
None of the exiles that Yan Hai had sent out had returned.
Di Jin had failed, but could Rowling have failed as well? Even if Rowling had failed, what about Hai Hun...
The conditions created by the warden's family gave them a huge advantage. What kind of means did Ming Wei have to be able to kill all these people without a sound?
Therefore, Yan Hai, who was originally full of confidence, was no longer confident.
Ming Wei led the group to the bottom of the city wall slowly.
Both parties were a few hundred feet apart, and they looked at each other silently.
"Yan Hai, we meet again!" Elder Jin chuckled.
Yan Hai glanced at Elder Jin coldly and scoffed, "Old dog Jin? You have no right to speak here! "
Hearing this, Fu Er, Chi Yi, and the other Chiyou people raised their eyebrows, and their faces were filled with anger.
Fu Er was about to retort, but Elder Jin stopped him and smiled faintly, "You're like a trapped beast with all four of your limbs broken. Other than your mouth, you have nothing left!"
Yan Hai ignored Elder Jin's sarcasm and stared at Ming Wei, "They want to make a deal with you."
Of course, he was referring to the warden race.
In their eyes, it was Ming Wei's ability that allowed them to reach this place so smoothly...
Whether it was the warden race or Yan Hai himself, Ming Wei was a taboo woman.
Ming Wei put her hands behind her back and smiled faintly. She tilted her head and asked, "What kind of deal?"
"Ask the Nuwa Clan to leave, and you to bring your people out," Yan Hai said.
As soon as he said that, the exiles and Aragami from the other clans were shocked.
Although they had finally reached the core area of this world, they had yet to truly fight the warden race. It was still unknown who would emerge victorious...
Yan Hai's offer was still very tempting.
Hence, everyone quietly looked at Ming Wei. What should they do if she really agreed to it?

"Guilty? What do you mean? "Yan Hai sneered.

They had already guessed the strength of the warden race. The appearance of Little Yun and Fu Chu was a further confirmation.
Before Yan Hai could answer, a person in a black robe popped her head out from the city wall. A female voice came from under the black robe. "You're right. The cultivation of the warden race is indeed not as strong as yours. However, don't forget that you're not a Nirvana Realm powerhouse now. Under the Equality of All Lives, you're just a mortal. On the other hand, the warden race has been blessed by our master and can break through the Equality of All Lives limit!"
The person in the black robe standing on the city wall was no longer an illusion of the warden race. She was a real member of the race.
Hearing the black robe's words, everyone's heart skipped a beat.
If what this person said was true and the warden race could break through the Equality of All Lives limit, they would be in big trouble …
After all, the difference in strength between the Equality of All Lives and the Equality of All Lives was huge! No matter how weak the warden race was, they were still at the True God level, right? Any random True God would be able to crush all of them.

Chapter 2235
Xiao Yun looked up at the black figure on the city wall. Her plain face was still calm, but the hatred deep in her eyes could no longer be suppressed.
She and Fu Chu had suffered too much. Fu Chu was still young at that time, so most of the difficulties were shouldered by her as the elder sister.
The pain of losing her father, the pain of losing her mother …
He watched helplessly as his uncle's family was being punished and crushed by that huge millstone.
Her three brothers were shot by arrows and turned into porcupines while trying to save her.
The scenes of the past kept flashing through her mind, biting her heart like a poisonous snake.
Her motive for revenge was too strong, so she could only desperately suppress the hatred in her heart.
When she found a suitable target, "Luo Zheng," she was very excited and wanted to do everything she could to get Luo Zheng to help her.
If it wasn't for Luo Zheng's good character, she would have considered selling everything she had, including her body.
While waiting, she was worried about her gains and losses.
She didn't expect everything to go so smoothly …
Just like that, she stepped into this space step by step and finally returned to her hometown. Now, it was time for her revenge.
"Aunt Lou, you didn't expect us to meet again under such circumstances, right?" The corners of Xiao Yun's mouth curled up slightly as a smile appeared on her clean face. She was enjoying this moment.
The warden clan wasn't big, so even if the liberal faction and conservative faction were enemies, they could still climb up the hierarchy. Therefore, Xiao Yun called the black figure "Aunt Lou."
Of course, Xiao Yun didn't have any intention of treating her as an elder.
The woman with the surname Lou looked at Xiao Yun from the city wall for a while and then muttered, "I really didn't expect … I didn't expect your liberal faction to fall to such a state. In the end, you would choose this step and lead these outsiders to slaughter our clan …"
Hearing this, Xiao Yun wanted to retort, but she didn't expect Fu Chu's voice to come from behind her.

The black-robed woman with the surname Lou glanced at Fu Chu. Compared to Little Yun, Fu Chu was actually more important because he was the one who could sever those curse chains.

"Your so-called mission is merely the will of the Primal Chaos Ancient God. As for us, we will always yearn for freedom. I believe there is no need to continue this debate," Xiao Yun said indifferently.
The debate between the Liberals and the Conservatives was endless. It didn't matter who was right or wrong.
"Indeed … there is no need," the woman with the surname Lou said softly. As soon as she finished speaking, she quickly ordered, "Kill that child called Fu Chu!"
With a wave of Yan Hai's hand, several people on the city wall each took out a black crossbow and fired at Fu Chu!
The distance between the two parties was extremely close. At such a distance, the power and accuracy of the crossbow were the best.

Moreover, they had always known that Yan Hai was crafty. In such a situation, it could be said that they had to be on guard at all times.

The concentrated sound of bowstrings being released rang out.
More than 20 black sword rays turned into black raindrops and shot toward the city wall.

On the other hand, Yan Hai dodged extremely quickly and quickly hid under the city wall.

Yan Hai smiled bitterly.
Most of the exiles who had followed him had already been killed before this, and just now, he had lost two more people.
Now, his so-called men were only left with the two exiles from the Xuanyuan Clan. Including Yan Hai, there were only three of them! Letting the three of them stop the hundreds of people led by Ming Wei? Moreover, after 'All Lives Equal', everyone's strength was about the same. Even Yan Hai didn't have absolute confidence in dealing with those Aragami.
"Then go to hell. You're useless trash anyway. I only hoped that you could kill each other. I didn't expect you to be so useless. It was a one-sided slaughter." After the woman with the surname Lou finished speaking, she quickly retreated.
Yan Hai, who was standing on the spot, felt a chill run down his spine.
At this moment, he finally understood that he had been completely used by the warden clan.

Yan Hai's eyes flashed with a cold light.
He was able to lead the Xuanyuan Clan for so many years without falling. After all, he was a hero.

Chapter 2236
The wall of the warden clan's settlement was only three meters tall.
After all, their clan had never considered the possibility of an invasion. This wall was merely a formality.
Just as Yan Hai was leading the two exiles to escape, a figure had already leaped over the wall.
Yan Hai recognized the figure on the wall. It was the Chiyou clan member who had fought with Xian Ze. Back then, Di Jin had used cheating methods but had still failed to take him down.

"Whoosh!"
The woman surnamed Lou was covered in a cloak. Although she could not fly, her body was extremely light. She moved forward at high speed along the street …
On the other side, Luo Zheng was constantly leaping and dashing across the buildings. By fully utilizing the terrain, Luo Zheng's speed was almost three times faster than the woman surnamed Lou's!
Soon, the woman surnamed Lou heard footsteps coming from her right.
She looked up in confusion and saw Luo Zheng running alongside her!
Luo Zheng smiled at her and said softly, "I'm sorry, but someone wishes for you to stay."

The woman surnamed Lou sneered.
She did not know Luo Zheng's origins, but for him to be able to catch up to her in such a short period of time meant that his strength was not ordinary.
However, she did not panic …
Just as Little Yun had said, the warden clan was indeed unable to completely break free from the Equality of All Lives rule. However, the grace bestowed upon them by the Primal Chaos Ancient God allowed them to break free from the Equality of All Lives rule.
"Swish!"
As the woman surnamed Lou ran forward, she gently pulled on a belt below her ribs.

The wardens were always wrapped in this cloak, and they rarely took it off even when they were facing their own kind. They would only take it off when they were in a battle!
The moment the cloak dispersed, the agile figure of the woman surnamed Lou was revealed. Her lips moved slightly, and wisps of blue light flickered between her elbows. A unique energy began to gather.
"This energy fluctuation …"
On the roof, Luo Zheng, who was walking side by side with her, had a solemn look in his eyes.
Now that all living beings were equal, all exiles had become mortals, or rather, they were only slightly stronger than mortals.
The Warden Clan could actually unleash such power with the bodies of mortals!
"But you're only dreaming." The woman with the surname Lou waved her hands, and blue energy continued to gather, turning into two elbow-length sharp blades.
She held the two sharp blades in a reverse grip. She looked at Luo Zheng with a smile on her face, and suddenly twisted her body!
"Shua!"
After a blue halo formed, it slashed towards Luo Zheng.
"Huff … Huff …"
The halo was extremely fast. In a moment of desperation, Luo Zheng turned over and rolled down from the roof. He used his arms to support himself on the ground, and only then did he manage to stand still.
The house behind him collapsed under the slash of the halo …
"Boom … Boom … Boom …"
The halo did not stop for a long time. After slashing a house, it continued to spread in a straight line, destroying dozens of houses in a row!
Such power was naturally not worth mentioning if there was no 'All Lives Equal'. Any Aragami could easily destroy this settlement with a wave of their hand …
But now, with just one slash, it could reach such a level. It was very shocking.
"Shua, shua." The woman with the surname Lou gently waved the two blue sharp blades and stared at Luo Zheng with a sneer.
Although she did not manage to kill this kid, she was still faintly proud of herself. She said, "Are you still going to chase after me? Leave now, and I can give you a chance to spare your life. "
Luo Zheng withdrew his gaze from the destroyed house and smiled. "I don't need that chance."
Hearing this, the woman with the surname Lou's pretty face suddenly turned cold. She waved the two sharp blades and rushed towards Luo Zheng.
She wanted to kill Luo Zheng, so she naturally wanted to end the battle quickly. If she was surrounded by the group of invaders, she would be in trouble …
"Hum …"
The two blue sharp blades slashed in the air, forming two huge crescent moons that slashed towards Luo Zheng from the left and right.
Facing such a shocking attack, Luo Zheng used his sword steps and twisted his body slightly, dodging the attack by a hair's breadth.
"Very exquisite steps." The woman with the surname Lou sneered. "But how long can you dodge?"
As she spoke, the blue light blossoming from her hands once again strangled Luo Zheng. Each move was fiercer and faster than the previous one!
Meanwhile, Luo Zheng held his sword with one hand and dodged all the attacks.
He maintained a distance of three to four feet from the woman with the surname Lou, using his nimble body technique to shuttle back and forth between her blades.
If others saw this scene, they would probably be worried for Luo Zheng. After all, one wrong step and he would be cut to pieces by the woman with the surname Lou. However, Luo Zheng seemed to be enjoying this.
After attacking dozens of times in a row, the woman with the surname Lou became anxious. This kid was more difficult to deal with than she had imagined!
If this kid's reinforcements arrived, she was afraid that she would not be able to escape!
Thinking of this, a ruthless expression appeared on her face. The two blue sharp blades crisscrossed and pushed, forcing Luo Zheng to retreat.
However, this push was only half of the way.
The corners of her mouth curled up slightly. The two sharp blades suddenly changed direction and slashed towards Luo Zheng's shoulders.
If they hit, she could directly turn Luo Zheng into a stick!
But at this moment, Luo Zheng raised his head and looked at the woman with the surname Lou. He faintly smiled and placed his sword horizontally in front of his chest.
When the woman with the surname Lou saw Luo Zheng's smile, she was slightly puzzled. However, Luo Zheng actually intended to use an ordinary sword to block her two sharp blades. This was too naive. Her sharp blades could instantly cut his sword into three pieces along with his body!
However, this youth should be well aware of this because he had been unwilling to use his sword to touch her blades. Why would he use such a stupid method to block her attacks?
The two of them had only exchanged blows for an instant. At this moment, she had no time to think about these confusing thoughts.
Just as her two sharp blades were about to slash Luo Zheng, a light green Truth Crystal quietly appeared on Luo Zheng's arm. Then, a green light quickly spread along his arm and enveloped his sword.
Dang! Dang!
Dang! Dang! Two crisp sounds rang out. The scene that the woman with the surname Lou had imagined didn't happen …

Chapter 2237
"Whoosh …"

As a result, he didn't let her guess down!

The woman with the surname Lou, who was half-leaning on the ground, stared at Xiao Yun, who was in the middle of the crowd. When she saw Xiao Yun's smiling face, she softly said, "For my master … You bunch of worms will never leave this world …"

After passing these tombstones, they came to the foot of the hill behind the hill.

This young man had been under the tombstone for countless years, and his appearance had never changed. This person's personality was strange, and he loved to talk nonsense. He rarely interacted with the warden clan …

However, this young man had shown great strength. It made the warden clan understand that this young man was not someone they could provoke. This person was even stronger than the exiles of this world.

Chapter 2239

Chapter 2240

Puff!
Luo Zheng kept retreating. The burly man controlled the butterflies to close in on him.
As he kept dodging, Luo Zheng felt a sharp pain in his right leg. An azure butterfly had streaked across the outside of his thigh, leaving behind a thumb-sized wound.
Because of this injury, Luo Zheng's body slowed down. Countless butterflies had already covered his head.
"Die." The burly man stood there.

Chapter 2241
When the butterflies completely enveloped Luo Zheng, the burly man's gaze shifted away from Luo Zheng.
His name was Lou Di, and he was one of the core members of the warden's tribe.
This time, Lou Di, Lou Bing, and Bai Shehuan would be the ones leading the battle against the invaders.
In fact, according to the original plan, Bai Shehuan and Lou Bing would lead the tribe to surround the invaders and block their escape route. Then, Lou Bing would control the blue Caged Butterflies to reap the lives of the invaders.
However, as soon as the two sides started to fight, Luo Zheng rushed up alone and blocked Lou Di's path, interrupting their plan.
After confirming that Luo Zheng was dead for sure, he was more concerned about the situation in front of him. He shouldn't waste time on this young man …
However, just as he shifted his gaze away, a green light flickered from the corner of his eye!
The Truth Crystal in Luo Zheng's hand once again dyed his longsword green.
Facing the approaching butterflies, Luo Zheng narrowed his eyes and lightly flicked the longsword in his hand.
"Bang!"
The green sword tip touched the butterflies, producing a crisp sound. The blue butterflies immediately exploded and disappeared …
After piercing the first butterfly, Luo Zheng's longsword was like a snake's tongue, immediately retracting as soon as it pierced out.
"Crackle crackle crackle crackle …"
In the blink of an eye, the butterflies that were pouncing forward were all blown up by Luo Zheng's longsword.
After the butterflies formed a hole in the cage, Luo Zheng rushed out and went straight for Lou Di!
"What …"
Lou Di was a little stunned when he saw this scene.
While he was stunned, Luo Zheng's longsword was already heading straight for his face!
Lou Di's reaction was extremely fast.
His body abruptly leaned backward, and he quickly retreated.
"Swish!"
Even so, Luo Zheng's sword tip brushed past his forehead, leaving a shallow cut of blood.
How could Luo Zheng give up on such an opportunity?
With one sword in the middle, the other sword followed suit!
Lou Di looked at Luo Zheng's sword light, and her eyes were filled with fear. This was a matter of life and death. If she wasn't careful, she might lose her life under this guy's sword!
He gritted his teeth and waved his hands. Blue light flickered on his fingertips and a series of butterflies flew out!
"Ah, ah, ah, ah, ah …"
Luo Zheng had no choice but to use his sword to blow up these butterflies. Because of this, he slowed down his pursuit of Lou Di, giving Lou Di a chance to catch her breath.
Even so, the two of them were still chasing after each other.
With one retreating and the other advancing, the two quickly rushed from the front of the street to the middle of the street.
Meanwhile, the warden's family was currently engaged in a fierce battle with the exiles and the Aragami …
Swish!
A black ray whistled and nailed the leg of a warden.
The black-robed man tumbled down from the roof and fell to the ground!
"Go!"
The few Aragami immediately swarmed forward.
The leader of the Aragami was holding a curved halberd. He leaped up and nailed the black-robed man to the ground.
The other Aragami were holding all kinds of weapons in their hands as they struck the black-robed man with their heads.
In the blink of an eye, the black-robed man from the warden's family was cut into pieces!
On the right side of the roof …
Three exiles had just jumped up. Before they could even stand still, three blue lights flashed in front of their eyes.
The blue energy transformed into three flying daggers and nailed them in the chest.
Not far away, a brown-haired woman's eyes were cold. In her hand, there were blue flying daggers arranged in a fan shape. She held them in her hand neatly.
"Break."
The brown-haired woman finally spat out a word.
The flying daggers that were stabbed in the chest of the three exiles instantly shattered into countless tiny fragments and spread out from the chest of the three exiles.
These fragments could be said to be indestructible. In an instant, the organs of the three exiles were riddled with holes …
The brown-haired woman was Lou Bing from the warden's family.
Seeing her family members dying one after another, she was also heartbroken. It was difficult for her to accept such a tragic scene.
The warden's family lived in this space peacefully. They were extremely safe and did not fight with the world at all.
The only battle in their history was the civil war between the liberal party and the conservative party. However, it was far less tragic than this. After all, this battle was almost related to the survival of the warden's family.
Just when she was about to attack again, she suddenly heard a gust of wind.
Her expression changed. She quickly lowered her head and turned her body.
"Whoosh! “
A circular blade swept past her back. If she was slightly slower, she would have been caught in the circular blade and her body would have been cut in half.
As Lou Bing dodged the circular blade, the few blue flying daggers in her hand were also thrown out.
Behind her, Ming Wei's eyes were calm. She twisted her body slightly, planning to pass through the gap between the flying daggers and take Lou Bing's life!
Ming Wei was extremely experienced in battle. Even though she had lost all her cultivation, she could use all the advantages she had. She would not give Lou Bing a chance to escape.
However, just as the flying daggers flew out, Lou Bing opened her mouth and said, "Break!"
All the flying daggers instantly shattered into countless blue fragments, blocking in front of Ming Wei.
Taking advantage of this opportunity, Lou Bing quickly distanced herself.
"Ming Wei of the Nuwa Clan," Lou Bing said coldly as she stared at Ming Wei.
The warden's family often sent clones to patrol the bone pagoda. They were also very familiar with the exiles. She did not expect Ming Wei to find her silently.
"What a pity …" Ming Wei smiled faintly. With that, she gently turned the circular blade in her hand. The circular blade was covered in blood. She had already killed six black-robed people just now.
"Hmph!"
Lou Bing raised her hand. The blue light continued to condense, once again transforming into tiny flying daggers.
Even though she had the favor of the Primal Chaos Ancient God, she still felt pressured when facing Ming Wei. However, this did not mean that she did not have the confidence to kill Ming Wei.
Just as the two of them were about to fight …
Suddenly, a series of explosive sounds came from below. This strange sound immediately attracted the attention of the two people.
"Luo Zheng!"
Ming Wei saw Luo Zheng, sword in hand, chasing after Lou Di.
What was even more peculiar was that the sword in Luo Zheng's hand was enveloped in a strange energy. After this energy protected the sword, it shattered the butterflies one by one.
"Big brother!"
Seeing this scene, Lou Bing's face was filled with anxiety.
Lou Bing had thought that Lou Di would quickly finish off Luo Zheng and then quickly come to her rescue.
She had not expected that this brat would be closing in on her step by step. Seeing her brother like this, she was afraid that if he was not careful, he would be killed by that brat. Naturally, she was extremely worried.
Lou Bing did not hesitate at all. She turned around and threw several flying daggers at Ming Wei.

Chapter 2242
The flying daggers exploded in all directions. Ming Wei retreated a few steps, her gaze still fixed on Luo Zheng.
She had long suspected that Luo Zheng had some special methods.
Otherwise, he wouldn't have been able to deal with the exiles sent by Yan Hai so easily.
But Ming Wei didn't expect Luo Zheng to be able to break through the restriction of 'All Lives Equal'!
That green energy wasn't very strong, but Ming Wei felt that it was very mysterious.
This guy really couldn't be seen through …
She was stunned for a moment. Seeing Lou Bing rush down, she tapped her toes and was about to jump down from the roof. She naturally wouldn't allow Lou Bing to go up and mess things up.
But just as Ming Wei jumped up, an azure whip whizzed over!
"Whoosh!"
This whip was very fierce. It directly pulled a deep groove out of the roof's debris and headed straight for Ming Wei.
Ming Wei frowned.
She naturally couldn't use her mortal body to resist this whip. She could only twist and stay on the roof.
"Your opponent should be me, heh heh heh." Another black-robed man held an azure whip and blocked Ming Wei's path …
Luo Zheng's sword speed became faster and faster, more and more accurate.
Even though Lou Di frantically summoned countless butterflies, it was a futile effort. Those butterflies had just condensed when they were blown up by Luo Zheng's sword. Every strike was so accurate that it made one's hair stand on end.
This sword in Luo Zheng's hand was like Luo Zheng's fingers. He had already reached the point where he could do as he pleased.
"What a powerful swordsmanship. How did this guy appear …"
Lou Di was secretly complaining in his heart.
In his opinion, only a few leaders among these exiles were difficult to provoke, such as Elder Jin, Ming Wei, and the others.
Although these people were powerful, they weren't much under the restriction of 'All Lives Equal'. As long as he defeated them one by one, they were destined to be able to stop these intruders. After all, they had the grace of their master.
But now this guy had suddenly appeared. He didn't know what method he had used, but he had also broken through the restriction of 'All Lives Equal'. What the hell was this!
Lou Di had ten thousand questions in his heart, but he didn't have the chance to ask. Right now, he only hoped to save his life!
Seeing that Luo Zheng was getting closer and closer to her, Lou Di had no better way to stop him.
This brat's sword was like a black hole, firmly sucking him in. No matter what, he couldn't get rid of it …
Swish!
Luo Zheng finally arrived in front of Lou Di. He suddenly raised the long sword in his hand and chopped down. The emerald green sword light appeared spontaneously.
"Die!"
This slash was too close for Lou Di to dodge. His heart sank. He knew that he would lose his life here. Just as he was about to close his eyes and wait for death, "Xiu! Xiu! Xiu!" Three more whistling sounds reached his ears.
Three azure throwing knives flew past his head and his ears, heading straight for Luo Zheng!
How could Luo Zheng let go of the opportunity to kill Lou Di?
Even when he saw the three flying daggers, he had no intention of backing down …
"Ding!"
In the process of slashing, the long sword shattered the first flying dagger and fell heavily towards Lou Di's head.
However, the other two flying daggers actually exploded at this moment …
Countless fine blue fragments sputtered in all directions.
If Luo Zheng hadn't dodged, he would have been seriously injured by the numerous fragments!
"Troublesome!"
In desperation, Luo Zheng could only choose to withdraw his sword and retreat a few steps to avoid the fragments.
Because of this, Lou Di was finally able to escape from Luo Zheng's pursuit and keep his life.
"Huchi, huchi …"
Lou Di felt exhausted. Her heart was beating rapidly and she was gasping for air.
"Brother, are you okay?"
Lou Bing walked in front of Lou Di and stood side by side with him.
"I'm fine. If it weren't for you saving me, I would have died under that brat's sword." Lou Di was also very excited after escaping from death.
"What's the matter with him? The green energy on his sword … where did it come from?" Lou Bing looked at Luo Zheng not far away, her eyes full of doubts.
Whether it was her brother's "Caged Butterfly" or her "Cracked Blade", they were all the favor of their master. Only the favor of their master had the right to ignore the equality of all living beings.
The face of the young man in front of her was extremely unfamiliar. He was probably not a famous person in the bone tower. How could he ignore the restriction of equality of all living beings?
"I'm not sure. Maybe something went wrong with the equality of all living beings …" Although Lou Di said so, he didn't believe it himself.
The equality of all living beings was set up by their master. Who was their master? It was impossible for him to make such a mistake.
"No matter what, let's kill him together first!" Lou Bing's eyes were filled with murderous intent.
Both sides had been fighting for so long. Overall, the warden family had a certain advantage. At least, the number of Aragami and Exiles that died was more than the number of the warden family …
However, they had to deal with the elites of these invaders.
At first, Lou Bing set her target as Ming Wei. However, when her brother was in danger, she now set her target as the nameless young man in front of her.
Luo Zheng placed his long sword in front of his chest. Hearing the conversation between the siblings, his eyes revealed a faint light.
Screams and wails could be heard coming from around him.
Some of the Aragami and Exiles were being killed by the warden family …
Although Luo Zheng was not familiar with these people, they were still his comrades after all. He also understood that he had to end the battle as soon as possible!
"Go!"
Lou Bing shouted. Blue flying daggers were launched straight at Luo Zheng.
At the same time, countless butterflies flew out from Lou Di's hand. As they fluttered in the air, they followed the path of the flying daggers.
Facing the attack of the siblings, Luo Zheng moved his feet slightly. His body moved like a ghost to the side. After circling around half a circle, he found a gap and rushed towards the siblings.
Lou Di and Lou Bing cooperated with each other very well. The two of them pulled away from each other calmly. However, at the same time, they looked after each other and formed an angle …
If Luo Zheng rushed towards Lou Di, he would face Lou Bing's attack. Vice versa.
Meanwhile, as the angle was getting bigger and bigger, the siblings had unknowingly surrounded Luo Zheng!
Swoosh swoosh swoosh!
Luo Zheng swung his long sword. Streaks of green swords formed an impenetrable wall, protecting him within. He was also feeling very depressed as he was unknowingly trapped by the siblings.
Lou Bing's hands were constantly waving. Flying daggers were launched at Luo Zheng one after another. She glanced at the side and said with a smile, "Bai Shehuan, you've come at the right time! Quickly lend us a hand and kill this brat! "
The battle between Luo Zheng and the Lou siblings had attracted the attention of many people.
After all, Lou Bing and Lou Di were the core members of the warden family. And now, Bai Shehuan wanted to join the battle?
The people of the warden family felt that it was unbelievable! Even Ming Wei did not receive such treatment under the Equality of All Beings, right? Who was that young man with the sword?

Chapter 2243
On the other side of the roof stood a skinny young man. This young man was the Bai Shehuan that Lou Bing spoke of.
Bai Shehuan's left hand was holding a head. This head belonged to the Fire Meld Tribe's Exile, Cao Huan.
Cao Huan's position in the Fire Meld Tribe was like Ming Wei's position in the Nuwa Tribe and Elder Jin's position in the Chi You Tribe. Even after 'All Lives Equal', his strength was unquestionable.
Now, his head had been cut off by Bai Shehuan!
In Bai Shehuan's right hand, there was a thick heavy sword. The surface of the heavy sword was rippling with blue light, emitting a terrifying energy wave.
Behind Bai Shehuan, there were four Exiles. The Chi You Tribe's Chi Yi and Fu Er were among them.
Chi Yi and Fu Er, who were originally incomparably brave, had several wounds on their bodies. They looked at Bai Shehuan with vigilance and caution. Now, Bai Shehuan was a great enemy to them!
The strength that Bai Shehuan displayed was far more powerful than the other black-robed people.
Cao Huan of the Fire Meld Tribe was killed by him because he underestimated his enemy.
Even with Chi Yi and the others restraining him, he had killed more than ten Aragami and five to six Exiles along the way.
Originally, Bai Shehuan, Chi Yi, and Fu Er were in the midst of a fierce battle. He probably noticed the commotion on Luo Zheng's side and took the initiative to withdraw from the battle and rushed to the side of the street.
"Oh? Lou Bing, you actually have a time to beg me? "Bai Shehuan, who was standing on the roof, said condescendingly.
Among the Warden Clan's younger generation, Lou Bing and Lou Di were in the limelight.
However, in terms of strength, whether it was Lou Bing or Lou Di, there was a large gap between them and Bai Shehuan.
However, Bai Shehuan's personality was cold and eccentric. He did not have a good relationship with others in the Warden Tribe.
Most importantly, the Lou family was the largest family in the warden's family. Even the patriarch was from the Lou family.
Because of this, Bai Shehuan's relationship with this pair of siblings wasn't very good.
"What time is it now? Cut the crap! Can't you see that this guy is different from the others? "Lou Bing said sternly.
Now that their clan was facing a matter of life and death, why would they care about those personal grudges?
Bai Shehuan sized up Luo Zheng a few times. Looking at the green light that was constantly circulating on Luo Zheng's arm, his eyes flashed with a hint of light. Soon after, he said, "Interesting, this kid actually broke through the limit of All Lives Equal!"
Then, he chuckled coldly and casually threw the head in his hand away. Then, he jumped down. The surface of the heavy sword in his hand emitted an azure light. It drew a wide arc in the air and chopped down towards Luo Zheng's head!
Luo Zheng was already under a lot of pressure facing the siege of Lou Bing and Lou Di.
Now that Bai Shehuan had joined the battle, the pressure on him would increase by several folds!
Facing Bai Shehuan's chop, Luo Zheng's body suddenly shrunk. At the same time, he shattered Lou Di's butterfly and Lou Bing's throwing knife. Then, he gathered all the strength in his body and poured it into his sword.
"Whoosh!"
His long sword flicked upwards and clashed with Bai Shehuan's heavy sword.
Bai Shehuan's chop from top to bottom was naturally extremely ferocious as it carried the momentum of descending.
However, Luo Zheng's long sword flicked upwards and used his strength to the extreme. The instant his long sword and Bai Shehuan's heavy sword came into contact, the sword's body gradually increased from light to heavy. It actually managed to forcibly reduce the strength of the heavy sword's chop by more than half.
Taking advantage of the moment when Bai Shehuan landed, Luo Zheng's long sword swung crookedly. He actually borrowed the force of the heavy sword and forcibly pushed the aggressive Bai Shehuan back a few steps, buying himself some time to catch his breath!
"Luo Zheng! What an ingenious swordsmanship … "
Even Fu Er and the others gasped in amazement when they saw this scene.
If it wasn't for the principle of All Lives Equal, Luo Zheng's method wouldn't even be considered a "small trick" in the eyes of the exiles.
However, in everyone's eyes, it could be said to be a divine skill!
"Let's go help Luo Zheng!"
"How can we let Luo Zheng fight one against three!"
"Stop that pair of siblings!"
How could Chi Yi and the others let Luo Zheng face three people by himself?
However, just as they moved, several black-robed people from the warden's clan rushed forward and blocked their path …
Bai Shehuan was forced back by Luo Zheng's ingenious sword. His eyes were filled with surprise.
"I told you not to be careless. This kid isn't easy to deal with!" Lou Di said.
"Is that so?"
Bai Shehuan didn't seem to care at all. Carrying his heavy sword, he rushed towards Luo Zheng again.
Purely based on martial skills, Bai Shehuan was inferior to Luo Zheng.
However, when the siblings Lou Bing and Lou Di worked together, it was like a large net was imprisoning Luo Zheng.
Luo Zheng had to first tear apart the large net before he could deal with Bai Shehuan's multiple sword strikes. He had just forced Bai Shehuan back a few steps when the large net closed up and trapped him again!
When Chi Yi, Fu Er, and the other exiles saw this scene, their hearts were burning with anxiety.
They didn't know how Luo Zheng was able to surpass the restriction of All Lives Equal. However, Luo Zheng was probably the only one who could face the warden's clan head on.
Even people as strong as Chi Yi and Fu Er were extremely wary of the azure energy.
After all, the weapons they were using were only ordinary weapons. If they were careless and touched the azure energy, their weapons would instantly shatter. They wouldn't be able to block it at all …
Only Luo Zheng could rely on that green lustrous light to fight them head on!
It could be said that Luo Zheng's current performance was enough to make him the hope of the exiles!
However, they were being held back by the black-robed people. How could they help Luo Zheng?
It wasn't just them. Ming Wei was the same. After the warden's clan obtained the favor of the Primal Chaos Ancient God, they had a huge advantage in terms of strength.
Lou Di and Lou Bing became calmer and calmer. Especially Lou Bing's throwing knives. They turned into streaks of light and shot towards Luo Zheng's weak points. Every single one of them was fatal.
Although Lou Di's butterflies were slow, there were many of them. They flew in strange trajectories and were impossible to guard against!
As for Bai Shehuan, he passed through the gaps deliberately left by the siblings and occasionally delivered fatal blows to Luo Zheng.
Although Luo Zheng had neutralized Bai Shehuan's attacks several times, he was still surrounded by danger. In the end, he was forced into a corner.
"It's about time," Bai Shehuan said indifferently.
At this moment, Bai Shehuan looked at Luo Zheng. He no longer looked down on him.
Even against the most powerful exiles, Bai Shehuan was confident that he could defeat them. However, this brat in front of him was actually able to block the attacks of the three of them.
Bai Shehuan was very clear that if it was a one on one, he definitely wouldn't be Luo Zheng's match.
Hearing Bai Shehuan's words, Lou Bing spread out her hands. Specks of blue light condensed and turned into more than ten throwing knives. They turned into streaks of blue light and shot towards Luo Zheng!
Lou Di's eyes flashed. Those azure butterflies fluttered even faster. They continuously danced and formed a storm that closed in on Luo Zheng …
The brother and sister both used their full strength!

Chapter 2244
Bai Shehuan had used all his strength in this attack.
Now, this strange kid had nowhere to retreat and could only take this attack head-on. Bai Shehuan did not believe that he could still struggle at death's door.
"Crackle, crackle, crackle …"
Luo Zheng tried his best to deal with Lou Bing and Lou Di's attacks.
Although his expression was calm, the pressure he was facing was extraordinarily great!
Seeing Bai Shehuan approaching with the heavy sword in his hand, Luo Zheng flicked his sword up and down, cutting through countless butterflies and flying daggers. Then, he swung his sword in a crooked manner.
The tip of his sword pointed at Bai Shehuan's heavy sword.
Luo Zheng's sword technique was still as ingenious as before. However, the enemy he was facing now was not those warriors from the lower realm of Huan Yu.
Bai Shehuan ignored Luo Zheng's sword and directly smashed it.
"Clang!"
When Luo Zheng's sword and the heavy sword collided, he felt a huge force crushing him. He stretched out his hand to grab the heavy sword. The sword flew out of his hand and nailed into the ground not far away.
Facing the unarmed Luo Zheng, Bai Shehuan no longer hesitated and raised his heavy sword high up again.
At this moment, on the other side of the roof, Ming Wei was rushing over at full speed.
"Pa, pa, pa …"
Streaks of azure blue whip shadows exploded behind Ming Wei.
Seeing that Luo Zheng was forced into a corner, she clenched her teeth and went around the black-cloaked man blocking her. She was about to rescue him!
Ming Wei was like a nimble swallow. She jumped high up in the air. Her extremely elastic body was as taut as a bow. As she stretched her body, the ring blade in her hand shot toward Bai Shehuan!
At the same time, at the back of the street, Elder Jin was also running with his large blade.
According to the previous agreement, Elder Jin was in charge of protecting Ming Wei and Fu Chu. Therefore, after the battle with the warden family started, Elder Jin escorted the pair of siblings to the back.
Compared to Ming Wei, Elder Jin could not tolerate Luo Zheng dying here.
As he rushed over, he threw out the saber in his hand.
"Whoosh, whoosh, whoosh …"
The broad and thick broadsword flew over the heads of the people who were fighting and headed straight for Bai Shehuan.
"Oh?"
Bai Shehuan's dead fish eyes blinked as a sneer appeared at the corner of his mouth.
He raised his hand and waved it behind him.
The blade that Ming Wei had shot out was instantly broken into two …
After chopping down the ring blade, Bai Shehuan swung his heavy sword and heavily chopped down on the large blade that Jin Lao had thrown at him.
"Clang!"
The surface of the broadsword was directly cut into two halves and fell to the ground.
Such an ordinary weapon would not be able to withstand the energy attached to the heavy sword. This was the grace bestowed by the primal chaos ancient god.
"What a waste of effort!"
After Bai Shehuan said this, he turned around to face Luo Zheng.
He felt like he was an executioner, leisurely taking the life of the kid in front of him, and the exiles were powerless to stop him.
Speaking of which …
The ones who attacked just now seemed to be Elder Jin of the Chiyou Clan and Ming Wei of the Nuwa Clan.
These two were probably the most powerful exiles in this world. Since they were so nervous about this kid, it seemed that this kid was indeed very important.
"Die. No one can save you now," Bai Shehuan said indifferently.
"Whoosh …"
The blue light on the surface of the heavy sword in his hand formed circles of spirals. It looked like the heavy sword had turned into a spear and was attacking Luo Zheng directly.
If this spear pierced through, no matter who it was, they would die without a doubt. Their body would probably be minced to pieces by that energy …
"If you don't attack now, you won't have a chance to attack anymore …"
On the bone plate in Luo Zheng's chest, 9527 finally could not help but remind Luo Zheng.
After condensing the Truth Crystal, with the help of 9527, he had mastered the most basic use of the Truth Crystal, the Falling Star Hand.
To be honest, the Falling Star Hand was not really the most basic use.
For example, Luo Zheng had directly melted the Truth Cube Crystal and fused it into his arm and sword. This was the most basic usage of it.
Therefore, 9527 had always been somewhat dissatisfied and did not know why Luo Zheng had done this.
But Luo Zheng naturally had his own considerations.
He did not know how strong the warden clan was. If he could only rely on the energy of the Truth Crystal to deal with them, he naturally did not want to casually use a trump card like the Falling Star Hand.
What's more, just relying on the energy of the Truth Crystal and his own martial arts was enough for the journey.
But now, Luo Zheng also knew that he had no other choice.
When he raised his head, a square Truth Crystal had already formed in his hand, and it was glowing with a jade-green light.
With a thought from him, the Truth Square Crystal began to rotate slowly.
"Falling Star Hand …"
"Hum …"
In that instant, the speed of the Truth Crystal's rotation suddenly increased by countless times.
At the same time, an identical vortex appeared above everyone's head, but this vortex was ten times larger. From the vortex appeared a green dot of light!
"Whoosh!"
The dot of light was incubated in the vortex for a while, and under Luo Zheng's gentle wave of his hand, it fell down of its own accord.
After the warden clan obtained the favor of the Ruin, they basically used it in the most superficial way.
Just like Luo Zheng before, they only used this energy as a weapon …
This was probably the original intention of the Ruin, because the azure light summoned by the warden clan seemed to be unable to change other forms.
For example, the two sharp blades of the Lou woman, Lou Di's Caged Butterfly, and Lou Bing's flying daggers.
Perhaps the Ruin thought that the warden clan could rely on this "favor" to resist the Invaders.
The only one who could change the form of this blue energy was Bai Shehuan, who wielded a heavy sword.
However, Bai Shehuan's ability to change the form was very limited. He could only control this energy to a certain extent, and could not do as he pleased. He could not activate this energy and use it in his God Art either …
But Luo Zheng used a God Art.
In order to cultivate the Falling Star Hand, Luo Zheng spent a lot of time.
However, the power produced in the end disappointed Luo Zheng quite a bit … …
If what 9527 said was true, if this' Falling Star Hand 'was released by' this lord ', there would be tens of millions of stars falling. The power of each star would be incomparably great and could destroy the world in a short period of time.
However, that required a lot of Truth Crystals. For the current Luo Zheng, it was obviously impossible.
This dot of light could not even kill an ordinary Aragami, so it was not practical at all. Luo Zheng had never shown it in front of others.
However, after "All Lives Equal", everyone was equal to mortals. This Falling Star Hand was probably the only God Art that could be used.
At this moment, this impractical God Art became extremely terrifying … …

Chapter 2245
When Elder Jin saw the saber he had thrown out being cut in half, his heart began to sink.
Luo Zheng's identity was too important to the Chiyou Clan.
Elder Jin didn't want Luo Zheng to take too many risks. He was more willing to protect Luo Zheng.
However, Luo Zheng's performance after All Lives Equal was too outstanding, so he couldn't dissuade him too much.
Fortunately, Luo Zheng really did display some amazing skills and led everyone into the city.
However, after facing the warden's clan, they were still trapped in a desperate situation. If Elder Jin had known earlier, he wouldn't have brought Luo Zheng into this space even if he was beaten to death.
Just as Elder Jin was at his wit's end, a whirlpool suddenly appeared in the sky.
"This divine ability …"
The starlight shone into Elder Jin's old eyes and flickered with a green radiance.
After Ming Wei threw out the ring blade, her whole body soared into the sky and hooked onto a beam on one side of the roof. Just like that, she hung in the air with one hand.
At this moment, she was naturally very anxious.
However, even someone as powerful as Ming Wei was unable to do anything under All Lives Equal, even if she had thousands of tricks and tens of thousands of divine abilities.
"You can only rely on yourself, little guy …" Ming Wei muttered in her heart.
She believed in miracles because the process of her growth was a miracle. She believed that Luo Zheng was also a miracle.
The result didn't disappoint Ming Wei. Under the illumination of the light, a faint smile appeared on her face.
Bai Shehuan didn't expect Luo Zheng to use this move at all!

Luo Zheng stared at Bai Shehuan and smiled. He raised his right hand again.
Another Truth Crystal was formed, and it began to spin rapidly.

When Bai Shehuan realized that something was wrong, he did not hesitate and quickly retreated.
However, it was already too late. That speck of starlight was falling at an extremely fast speed.
If everyone had a cultivation base and wasn't treated as All Life Equal, there was still a chance for them to dodge. However, as mortals, there was no way for them to dodge.
Boom!
That speck of starlight smashed down, crushing half of his shoulder. When the starlight fell to the ground and exploded, it had already swallowed his entire body.
Bai Shehuan had fallen!

Although they were not on good terms with Bai Shehuan, they were still heartbroken when they saw their comrade die so tragically …

Lou Di also nodded …
Before the battle, they had already made up their minds to fight to the death.

Sou!
