He didn't expect Yan Hai to escape and appear at this time.
Yan Hai held a sharp knife in his hand, and looked down at the purple-haired black-robed man with a cold smile. "I've always been the one to scheme against others. I didn't expect you to scheme against me this time …"
"Haha, you're also prisoners of this world. How can we, the warden's clan, let you go?" The purple-haired black-robed man endured the pain and said.
"That's true. We were on different sides from the beginning. We shouldn't have cooperated." Yan Hai nodded, and pointed the knife in his hand at the purple-haired black-robed man. "Tell me how to remove All Lives Equal."
The purple-haired black-robed man sneered. "Do you think I'll tell you?"
Yan Hai shrugged, and without hesitation, stabbed the knife into the purple-haired black-robed man's abdomen, opening a wound. "If you don't remove All Lives Equal in the time it takes for a stick of incense to burn, you won't be able to recover your cultivation. You'll bleed to death. To live or to die, it's your choice."
Then, Yan Hai took two steps back and leaned against the wall, staring at the purple-haired black-robed man with an indifferent gaze.
The purple-haired black-robed man's arms and legs were already cut off. He couldn't move, and there was no way he could stop the bleeding. If this continued, he would die without a doubt …
The only way to save him was to remove All Lives Equal!
But now, the warden clan still had the strength to fight against the invaders. Once All Lives Equal was removed, Ming Wei alone would be able to crush his entire clan!
"Drip, drip, drip …"
The sound of blood dripping entered his ears, and a trace of panic appeared on the purple-haired black-robed man's face.
Sensing his panic, Yan Hai smiled faintly. "You're the only one who can save you."
The purple-haired black-robed man's heart was also in a fierce struggle. He didn't think he was a coward who was afraid of death, but he was unwilling to die without a sound.
After dozens of breaths, the purple-haired black-robed man finally spoke. "There are two ways. One is to restore the Equality Altar, and the other is to destroy it."
"Which way is faster?" Yan Hai asked again.
"Restore!" The purple-haired black-robed man said. "The Equality Altar isn't that easy to destroy. With your current strength, you won't be able to destroy it even after a few days and nights!"
"Tell me how to restore it," Yan Hai said calmly.
…
After a while, Yan Hai came out from the corner and wiped the blood off the sharp knife.
Not far away, the sound of an intense battle could be heard. He smiled coldly in that direction, then walked in another direction.
Now that the warden clan had turned out in full force, there were no guards around the Equality Altar.
Destroying the Equality Altar wasn't that easy, and only a few people in the warden clan knew how to restore it. At this moment, there was no point in guarding it.

Chapter 2250
Whoosh!
With a horizontal slash, the longsword left a thin mark on the wall!
The spreading green sword aura directly cut the entire house in half, and the house began to collapse with a rumble …
Although Luo Zheng's sword was fierce, it still couldn't injure the tomb keeper.
He held his longsword, his eyes full of solemnity.
No matter who it was, under the Equality of All Beings, they were still mortals.
Under the same conditions, Luo Zheng generally found it difficult to find a match.
But this guy displayed the toughness of a mortal body. Even with the Truth Crystal, Luo Zheng couldn't suppress him. This greatly exceeded Luo Zheng's expectations!
"Let's make a deal, okay? If I can defeat you, you'll tell me how you cultivated this Truth God Ability, "the tomb keeper said with a smile as he played with the dagger in his hand.
Seeing the tomb keeper's attitude, Luo Zheng was slightly confused.
He originally thought that this person should be in cahoots with the warden clan, but now it seemed that he didn't care about the fate of the warden clan.
"Cut the crap," Luo Zheng stretched out his hand, and the Truth Crystal in his palm spun slightly, and a streak of starlight fell rapidly towards the tomb keeper!
At the same time, Luo Zheng took a big stride forward, and the longsword surrounded by green light slashed towards the tomb keeper.
Whoosh!
The tomb keeper still looked indifferent, and took half a step back.
His body was always moving by a very small margin.
When that streak of starlight came crashing down, his body was almost stuck to it, but this streak of starlight couldn't hurt him in the slightest!
When Luo Zheng's sword aura pierced through, the tomb keeper twisted his neck slightly, and the sharp sword aura was only a few millimeters away from his neck …
This kind of dodging was like dancing on the tip of a blade. The slightest carelessness would consign him to eternal damnation.
But the tomb keeper was very leisurely, as if taking a step back was a waste of energy.
If there wasn't the Equality of All Beings, Luo Zheng could easily use the Eight Directional Flying Smoke.
This guy could do it just by relying on his experience. His perception of energy had reached a very terrifying level …
Seeing his opponent's movements, Luo Zheng was secretly shocked, and his attacks became fiercer and fiercer.
"Boom boom boom!"
Three streaks of starlight smashed down on the tomb keeper's head.
The tomb keeper twisted his body to the left and right, sticking close to the three rays of starlight. Instead of retreating, he advanced. Holding a short knife, he approached Luo Zheng.
"Huff huff …"
Luo Zheng suddenly raised his long sword and stabbed the tomb keeper seven or eight times in a row.
Every strike of his was aimed at the gravekeeper's vital points, but every strike was fruitless.
Even though Luo Zheng's sword was crooked and he couldn't see where it came from, the tomb keeper could still make an accurate judgment.
"If you don't want to say it, then I'll have to force you to!" The tomb keeper was dodging nonstop, but he still had the time to speak.
As soon as he finished speaking, he took a step forward and stood right in front of Luo Zheng. The two of them were face to face.
"Shua!"
Luo Zheng swung his long sword and slashed horizontally.
The tomb keeper's body was like a willow branch, falling backwards along with Luo Zheng's horizontal slash.
After dodging this slash, the tomb keeper once again came close to Luo Zheng. He flipped the short knife in his hand and gently knocked the back of the knife against Luo Zheng's wrist.
"Clang!"
The long sword fell to the ground.
Having lost his weapon, Luo Zheng clenched his teeth and once again summoned a ray of starlight, smashing it down on the tomb keeper.
The tomb keeper's eyes flashed faintly, and he circled behind Luo Zheng.
He grabbed Luo Zheng's back with one hand and gently pushed forward. Unexpectedly, he pushed Luo Zheng's head directly under the rapidly falling ray of starlight. At the same time, he said: "Speak."
Seeing that the ray of starlight was about to hit Luo Zheng's head, the tomb keeper suddenly pulled Luo Zheng back.
With this push and pull, Luo Zheng's back suddenly broke out in a cold sweat.
Just now, his life was completely in the hands of the tomb keeper. If the tomb keeper hadn't pulled him back, he would have died from his brain bursting!
Even so, Luo Zheng didn't intend to give up.
Under the tomb keeper's pull, Luo Zheng took advantage of the opportunity to flip over and hit the tomb keeper's face with his elbow.
The tomb keeper's mouth slightly curled. He bit the short knife in his mouth and twisted his body to avoid Luo Zheng's elbow. His hands slid to Luo Zheng's shoulders and gently squeezed.
"Crack!"
"Crack!"
Accompanied by two crisp sounds, Luo Zheng's arms hung limply. They were dislocated.
"Do you still want to struggle?" the tomb keeper asked again.
"Huff …"
Luo Zheng lifted his leg and kicked at the tomb keeper.
"Crack, crack!"
The tomb keeper avoided this kick. Luo Zheng's legs were also dislocated. His legs went limp and he directly fell to the ground.
Luo Zheng lay on the ground, gasping for breath. He was now very helpless.
With the same strength, he had never experienced such a crushing defeat.
Even if Ming Wei wanted to defeat Luo Zheng under the Equality of All Beings, it was almost impossible.
But this guy was so relaxed, almost playing with him in the palm of his hand …
"Actually, I can guess even if you don't say it." The tomb keeper looked down at Luo Zheng and said, "Your Truth God Art should come from 'Void'. When that guy just fell into this world, I went to visit him, but he ignored me …"
"Void?" Luo Zheng's eyes flashed slightly.
9527 called the chaos ancient god "Lord" and never called him by his name. It turned out that the chaos ancient god was called "Void".
"The inheritance of the Truth God Art, hehe. Countless top figures in the mother world are scrambling for it. Some even have wishful thinking of creating the Dao Fusion Grand Unification energy, but in the end, they have nothing … But why did he choose you?" The tomb keeper's face was faintly bitter.
The tomb keeper's purpose of visiting "Void" was also a glimmer of hope that he could ask for his inheritance. But in the end, his hope was still lost.
In fact, the tomb keeper stayed here because he wanted to obtain the Truth God Art. That was why he made a bet with the Ruin …
In the eyes of the tomb keeper, Luo Zheng was indeed outstanding as an upper True God.
But it was not that the tomb keeper had never seen an outstanding True God like Luo Zheng. After all, there were too many enchanting people in the mother world.
In the eyes of the tomb keeper, Luo Zheng could not even be ranked in the top ten.
He had spent countless energy and wasted countless years, but he still could not obtain something. This little guy in front of him easily obtained it, which inevitably made him feel unbalanced.
"What is the purpose of choosing you? Tell me. "The tomb keeper stared at Luo Zheng and continued to ask.
Luo Zheng did not answer and simply turned his face to the side.
"I have a way to make you talk …" The tomb keeper continued.
Just as the tomb keeper was interrogating, a "whoosh, whoosh" sound suddenly came from afar, as if a huge millstone was rotating.
When the tomb keeper heard this sound, he raised his head and looked in the direction of the Altar of Equality. His brows furrowed slightly, and he was rather puzzled.
Restoring All Lives Equal at this time? Did the warden family want to die?
Under All Lives Equal, the warden family already had difficulty resisting these invaders. If All Lives Equal was restored, it would be even more difficult for them!
As the Altar of Equality slowly rotated, orange light spots escaped from the Altar of Equality and began to spread in all directions …
When All Lives Equal came into effect, it was a process from inside to outside, from slow to fast.
It was the same for removing All Lives Equal.
And Yan Hai, who was standing in the center of the Altar of Equality, was the first to be immersed in the orange light spots.
The moment his cultivation recovered, a sinister smile appeared on his face.
At this moment, the group of people not far from the altar were just a group of insect-like mortals.
He realized that his opportunity had come …
"All of you … die!"

Chapter 2251
There was a time difference between the removal of Equality of All Lives from the Altar of Equality.
It would take about two to three breaths for the orange light to spread from the Altar of Equality to where Ming Wei and the others were.
These two to three breaths of time were more than enough for Yan Hai.
Now that Yan Hai had recovered his original strength, the shackles on his body had disappeared completely, and a terrifying aura spread out from his body.
A full-powered attack from a Nirvana Realm powerhouse could easily kill tens of millions or even hundreds of millions of mortals, let alone hundreds of people.
Yan Hai's body began to expand as he roared, and he instantly turned into a 400,000-foot-tall giant.
Crackle …
Eight dragon-shaped lightning bolts crazily coiled around his body.
Yan Hai felt extremely carefree when he thought about how these people would die by his hands …
Ming Wei, Old Monster Jin, all the exiles, and the warden's family, all of you, die!
Yan Hai did not hesitate at all. With a clap of his hand, the eight dragon-shaped lightning bolts coiled around each other and poured down on the people who were fighting …
At this moment, everyone was in a daze.
The head of the warden's family stared blankly at the thick dragon-shaped lightning bolts. He leaned on his walking stick, his body hunched, and sighed faintly.
A sense of helplessness suddenly overwhelmed him.
He was too lazy to think about how Yan Hai had removed Equality of All Lives.
The warden's family was now on the verge of defeat. Although the tomb keeper had defeated the two strongest invaders, there was nothing he could do.
However, it was useless. He couldn't force the other party to do too much.
There was no way for them to turn back now, and there was no point in turning back …
When Ming Wei, Old Monster Jin, and the other exiles felt the immense power and saw Yan Hai's incomparably huge figure clearly, their eyes were filled with unwillingness.
Previously, they had allowed Yan Hai to escape, but in the end, they had buried such a disaster. This was something that no one had expected.
The eight dragon-shaped lightning bolts approached in the blink of an eye. Just the rumbling of the lightning alone was enough to shatter everyone's eardrums and cause them to bleed from their ears …
After all, they were still mortals. Just the power emitted by the dragon-shaped lightning bolts was enough to kill them.
Luo Zheng, who was lying on the ground, saw this scene, and a bitter smile appeared on his face.
Who would have thought that after all this fighting, everyone would end up dying here?
The tomb keeper stood beside Luo Zheng and watched this scene with his mouth slightly agape.
He had never thought that something like this would happen …
"Damn it, I was too careless …"
If it wasn't for the Equality of All Lives, he wouldn't have put Yan Hai in his eyes. Not to mention Yan Hai, even if all the exiles in this world came at him together, he would not even put them in his eyes!
However, those orange dots of light were still floating slowly in the distance.
It was obvious that the dragon-shaped lightning would devour him before his cultivation recovered.
However, the tomb keeper was not an ordinary Nirvana Realm powerhouse.
He was one of the few people in the Mother World.
Equality of All Life was a truth divine art. It was indeed a great suppression on him, but it wasn't like he couldn't resist it!
At the moment of life and death, the tomb keeper finally became serious.
"Rise!"
He let out a roar.
Streaks of blood runes spread out from his cheeks, and those blood runes bloomed like auspicious clouds.
At the same time, veins popped out all over his body. He was going all out …
"Buzz buzz buzz …"
Small blue dots of light emerged from the surface of his body.
When Luo Zheng, who was lying on the ground, saw this scene, his pupils suddenly widened.
Back then, it was these blue dots of light that caused everyone's cultivation to begin to decline after they invaded their bodies.
These blue dots of light should represent the Equality of All Lives magical art.
This guy could actually resist this magical art and force it out of his body?
Equality of All Lives was set up by the Primal Chaos Ancient God!
Only now did Luo Zheng realize how powerful this tomb keeper was.
It seemed that it wasn't wrong for him to be completely defeated by him …
After forcing out those blue dots of light, the tomb keeper's aura underwent a drastic change.
At this moment, the eight dragon-shaped lightning bolts flew over.
The lightning set off waves of air, and the houses on both sides of the road kept collapsing.
Along the way, the exiles and the members of the warden's family were annihilated the moment they were submerged by the dragon-shaped lightning. They had no room to resist or avoid it.
When the lightning bolts reached the tomb keeper, he stretched out his hand and gently pointed downwards.
Luo Zheng didn't sense any magical art being used by the tomb keeper, nor did he sense any energy fluctuations.
However, the eight dragon-shaped lightning bolts were unable to take another step forward when they reached the tomb keeper.
An invisible curtain blocked the tomb keeper's path.
The dragon-shaped lightning bolts seemed to have obeyed the tomb keeper's orders, and changed directions and headed downwards.
"This guy can control other people's power?" Luo Zheng was amazed by this scene.
"Rumble rumble rumble …"
The eight dragon-shaped lightning bolts headed downwards, and instantly created a bottomless pit in the ground.
Luo Zheng lay on the edge of this huge pit, as if he were lying on the edge of a cliff.
Yan Hai's attack was originally one filled with confidence.
He didn't believe that anyone could resist even a millionth of his current power …
However, such a bizarre scene still happened.
Not only had someone blocked his attack, they had even stolen his power!
How was this possible?
Even if Ming Wei recovered her strength, she wouldn't be able to do this …
Yan Hai narrowed his eyes and stared at the tomb keeper.
"Who are you?" Yan Hai asked.
Although his voice was loud, perhaps only the tomb keeper could hear him. Everyone else's eardrums were already shattered.
"You don't have the right to know." The tomb keeper casually raised his arm and slammed it down towards Yan Hai.
The tomb keeper was extremely angry now …
He had helped the warden's family fight and fought Luo Zheng with a very relaxed attitude.
To him, this was nothing more than a change of pace after a long period of boredom.
However, this banished person acted recklessly and took the initiative to remove All Life's Equality, forcing him to forcibly break free from it.
Even for him, it wasn't easy to resist the All Life's Equality of the Chaos Ancient God, and he had to pay a considerable price.
How could the tomb keeper not be angry when he had to pay such a high price for a small fry like Yan Hai?
He only wanted to tear this guy to shreds and be happy!
As the tomb keeper slammed down in the air, an invisible palm slammed heavily towards Yan Hai.
Yan Hai immediately felt an irresistible force pressing down on him. Within his 400,000-foot-tall body, the sound of bones shattering exploded like thunder.
"Crackle crackle crackle …"
Yan Hai didn't even have time to remove the Rage God's body before his huge head was solidly flattened.
The huge body collapsed rapidly, like a mountain collapsing ….
After his soul was destroyed, the Rage God's power began to rapidly dissipate. The constantly collapsing huge body quickly shrank and disappeared from everyone's sight.

Chapter 2252
At this moment, Ming Wei had already reached her limit.
Due to the excessive blood loss, her face was as white as a sheet of paper.
Her consciousness was also in a state of confusion, and her life force was extremely weak.
She had no idea what was going on, and she was only holding on with her remaining willpower.
At this moment, the orange light entered her body.
Her suppressed cultivation base quickly recovered …
Her almost depleted life force was replenished in the blink of an eye.
Her weak aura instantly increased by tens of millions of times!
Ming Wei did not need to use any divine ability. She only needed to rely on her powerful Qi and blood to quickly recover.
The dagger that was stuck in her chest automatically withdrew from the wound under the wriggling of her muscles.
The moment the dagger withdrew, the wound had completely healed. There was not even the slightest trace of a scar, leaving only flawless skin.
Apart from Ming Wei, the other heavily injured exiles also began to recover rapidly …
Powerful auras spread out one after another, filling the entire space.
The black-robed people from the warden's clan also recovered their cultivation base.
No one in their clan had reached the Nirvana Realm. Compared to Ming Wei and the others, their auras were negligible.
However, at this moment, Ming Wei and the others did not act rashly …
They all looked toward Luo Zheng.
There was an extremely powerful monster beside Luo Zheng.
The tomb keeper could break free from All Lives Equal and kill Yan Hai from the Xuanyuan Clan with a single slap.
Regardless of whether it was Ming Wei or Elder Jin, both of them were extremely wary of such a powerful person.
If this guy stood on the warden's side, they would have no chance of survival at all.

The scene of the gravekeeper's attack had greatly shocked the warden's family.

The patriarch understood that the tomb keeper was their last hope.

If the warden's family really persuaded the tomb keeper, they might really lose everything.
"Senior, I am the Sect Master of Pear Mountain's Baiwei Sect. I am truly unwilling to be imprisoned here. If I can return to Pear Mountain, my Pear Mountain lineage will definitely remember this kindness," Ming Wei said.

This strange laughter caused Ming Wei's scalp to go numb.
Everyone else's hearts were in their throats …
There were countless experts in the Mother World.
Since the ancient times until now, countless outstanding figures have been born, and many powerful forces have been born.

As for Ming Wei, Elder Jin, and the exiles, they all turned pale with fright when they heard the tomb keeper's words.
They never imagined that they would meet such a person here.

Human Emperor Fuxi was an existence with a transcendent status in the Mother World. Who among the exiles present hadn't heard of this name?

No one would've thought that Fuxi had long left the Mother World and retreated to the depths of the chaos. He'd stayed here for so many years ….

Chapter 2253
Luo Zheng flew past a row of low houses, and what came into view was a short mountain.
On this short mountain, there were black tombstones that were six feet tall.
After flying past these tombstones, another tall tombstone appeared behind the short mountain.
Just as Luo Zheng was wondering who was buried under this tombstone, Fuxi casually threw Luo Zheng down.
When he was about one foot away from the ground, Luo Zheng lightly tapped the ground with the tip of his foot and stood firmly under this tall tombstone.
Immediately after, Fuxi's figure flashed and stood directly under the tombstone.
Luo Zheng sized up the dark tombstone. There were no inscriptions on this tombstone, nor were there any seals. He didn't know who was buried under this tombstone.
"Why did you bring me here?" Luo Zheng asked.
Fuxi's expression was very calm. At this moment, he looked like an ordinary person, and there was nothing special about him.
Who would have thought that this guy could casually kill a Nirvana Realm expert, and an expert of Yan Hai's level?
He didn't answer Luo Zheng's question, and he lightly tapped on the tombstone.
Golden lines slowly extended from the bottom of the tombstone. These golden lines climbed up in an unsatisfied manner, and soon covered the entire tombstone.
Luo Zheng scrutinized these golden lines, but he couldn't see what was so mysterious about them …
"I've been here for a very, very long time, about 30 billion years." Fuxi stared at the golden lines and said indifferently, "Even for me, 30 billion years isn't a short period of time, and I've wasted it all here …"
Hearing this, Luo Zheng was rather baffled. "You're not trapped here. Can't you leave as you please?"
Luo Zheng only knew that this guy called "Fuxi" was very powerful, and he was Nuwa's brother. This prison shouldn't be able to trap him.
This guy was willing to waste his time here, and now he was saying something like this. Luo Zheng naturally couldn't understand.
"Leave?"
A golden light flashed in Fuxi's eyes, and he placed one hand on Luo Zheng's shoulder.
"Crack!"
An irresistible force was exerted on Luo Zheng's shoulder, and Luo Zheng's shoulder blade immediately shattered into powder.
Before the pain in his body could reach Luo Zheng's mind, a light flashed in Fuxi's hand, and Luo Zheng's shattered shoulder blade was restored to its original state.
"Do you know what it means for me to leave?" Fuxi asked coldly.
Luo Zheng pursed his lips. "I don't know."
"Aren't you curious?" Fuxi asked again.
"Not at all," Luo Zheng answered.
Fuxi: "…"
Luo Zheng's only thought was to sever the curse chains and leave this prison.
Fuxi's willingness to stay here had nothing to do with Luo Zheng.
Fuxi stared at Luo Zheng for a while, then said indignantly, "It seems that you don't know anything … This is completely unreasonable! Why did Kong choose you? Why did he choose you?! "
Hearing this, Luo Zheng couldn't help but roll his eyes. After beating around the bush for so long, this guy was still stuck on this question.
Even now, Luo Zheng didn't care too much about the bloodline of the primal chaos ancient gods.
The truth god art was too complicated, and it wasn't very powerful. The only place where it could be used was after All Lives Equal descended.
The problem was that now that Luo Zheng wanted to return to the divine realm, the warden race couldn't send All Lives Equal to the divine realm, and then use the truth god art to fight the enemy, right?
All things considered, the truth god art was still of little value to Luo Zheng at this stage!
"I don't know either." Luo Zheng looked at the agitated Fuxi and shrugged. "Then can I leave?"
"Of course not!" Fuxi said as he slapped the tombstone, and the golden lines on the tombstone began to vibrate wildly.
Fuxi could easily crush Luo Zheng's body with a casual squeeze. The power of this slap made Luo Zheng's heart tremble.
However, he didn't know what material this tombstone was made of, but it didn't move after being slapped by Fuxi.
"I made a bet with Kong." Fuxi clenched his teeth and stared at the tombstone. "If I can't solve the mystery of this tombstone, I will never leave here! I want Kong to acknowledge me and pass on his bloodline to me. This is my only hope! "
Fuxi's purpose of staying here was to obtain the truth god art of the primal chaos ancient gods.
From Fuxi's point of view, the truth god art couldn't be obtained through comprehension. The only way was to integrate the primal chaos ancient gods' bloodline.
Kong gave him hope, but he couldn't solve it … As for Kong, he didn't care about him at all.
What kind of person was Fuxi? The thing he pursued with all his might was actually obtained by Luo Zheng so casually.
How unwilling was he to accept this? How jealous was he of Luo Zheng?
"The mysteries of this tombstone still come down to the comprehension of the Dao Fusion Grand Unification Energy. I'm going to use your Truth Crystal to reverse the process. Before I figure out the mysteries, don't even think about leaving. Now, take out your Truth Crystal …" Fuxi said.
With Fuxi's talent, there were very few things in the Origin World that could stump him.
The reason he couldn't solve the mystery of the tombstone was because of the limitations of the Grand Unification of Dao. He deduced that using Luo Zheng's Truth Square Crystal could solve this problem. This was because Luo Zheng's Truth Square Crystal was also comprehended by the primal chaos ancient gods from the Grand Unification of Dao.
Hearing this, Luo Zheng was slightly shocked.
This guy had been comprehending here for tens of billions of years, but he still couldn't comprehend the mystery of the tombstone.

Chapter 2262

As a Saint, Mu Haiji had the right to leave the Divine Domain and investigate at the top of the Divine Domain.

No matter who this person was, it was likely an existence he couldn't afford to provoke. He had to report this to Dongfang Chunjun …
The spatial tunnel that Ming Wei constructed once again extended downwards. From one tunnel, it split into 13 tunnels.
Although she had forcibly built a spatial tunnel here in a fit of pique, she still had to consider Luo Zheng's safety.
If someone captured Luo Zheng's location, it would be troublesome.
The thirteen tunnels were scattered throughout the Divine Domain, and Luo Zheng had entered the Divine Domain from one of them at random. This would greatly increase the difficulty of tracking him.
"This is the Annihilation Lightning Talisman! Luo Zheng, you can use it when you need to save your life. "Fu Er held three pale yellow talismans in his hand. On the surface of these talismans, streaks of lightning constantly danced.
"This is a Demon Blood Pill. As long as your soul isn't destroyed, you can instantly rebuild your physical body …" Chi Yi handed Luo Zheng a red pill.
"This is …" Elder Jin said.
When Ming Wei saw this, she couldn't help but purse her lips. She was about to say something, but in the end, she chose to shut her mouth.
After all, they had good intentions and were afraid that something would happen to Luo Zheng.
Unfortunately, this group of men didn't have the foresight of a woman like her. Whether it was now or in the future, Luo Zheng's path wouldn't be smooth sailing. Elder Jin and the others still had an advantage in strength and could help Luo Zheng.
But what about after returning to the Mother World?
In that Mother World, where experts were as common as clouds, even Ming Wei was nothing. She couldn't protect him forever.
Luo Zheng didn't refuse Elder Jin and the others' good intentions.
After everything was prepared, he cupped his hands and bid farewell to everyone. "Thank you for seeing me off, Elders. If you can break through the spatial barrier, I hope we can meet again!"
Then he turned around and stepped into the spatial tunnel. In the blink of an eye, he disappeared in front of them.
Unlike Elder Jin, Luo Zheng didn't encounter any spatial barriers in the spatial tunnel.
The spatial tunnel constructed by Ming Wei was very stable, and he could travel through it at a relatively fast speed …
Six hours later …
Luo Zheng had already crossed the entire great extreme universe and entered one of the thirteen spatial passageways at the same time.
Twenty-four hours later …
Luo Zheng felt his body suddenly become heavy, and his falling speed suddenly accelerated.
Whoosh!
A huge inertia exerted on Luo Zheng's body. He was like a falling stone, smashing into the ground at lightning speed.
"Rise!"
Luo Zheng shouted, trying to fly into the air.
But the inertia was too strong. He couldn't reverse the inertia and fly up. The inertia was like an invisible hand pressing him down to the ground.
He was getting closer and closer to the ground. If he fell at such a high speed, even if he didn't die, he would be seriously injured. Moreover, it would cause a huge commotion.
Luo Zheng's eyes flashed, and he suddenly disappeared.
He was already at the Peak Realm and was no longer restricted by the spatial laws. He could use greater teleportation.
At this time, he teleported to a higher place …
Even so, the huge inertia didn't disappear, and he continued to fall.
"Again!"
Just as he was about to smash into the ground again, Luo Zheng teleported to a higher place and continued to fall.
After repeating this over ten times, the huge inertia finally weakened and disappeared …

Chapter 2263
On the Dongfang Family's floating island, Mu Haiji walked out of the void with a spatial ripple.
In the Sea of Time, only Saints could safely use Spatial Transference.
But under normal circumstances, Saints wouldn't do so. Mu Haiji's sudden appearance on the Dongfang Family's floating island meant something important.
When the two guards of the Dongfang family saw that Mu Haiji had come in person, they hurriedly went up and bowed.
"I'm looking for the Great Sage of the East. I have something to discuss with him. Hurry and pass on the message," Mu Haiji said calmly.
The two guards looked at each other, and a trace of embarrassment appeared on their faces.
One of them said, "The Great Sage is in the back hall. He said that no one is to disturb him no matter what!"
"The back hall! He's been in the back hall all this time! He'll be destroyed by that woman sooner or later! "Mu Haiji's face was filled with anger.
In the past few years, Dongfang Chunjun's personality had become extremely strange.
Originally, under the leadership of the Wealthy Alliance, they wanted to take control of the Abyss Demonic Region. But now that matter had been put on hold.
Every time the Saints visited, Dongfang Chunjun would either be in the back hall or exploring the Sea of Time to find a body for that woman!
In the eyes of the Saints like Mu Haiji, the Wealthy Alliance had the absolute advantage and controlled the situation …
But three of the Four Great Sacred Lands were already controlled by outsiders.
Mount Buzhou was controlled by Luo Xiao's eldest disciple. Takeshima Forest was controlled by Gu Bei's second disciple.
As for Hun Huang, Shi Xiaoqiao had saved Luo Zheng's life last time. It seemed that she was also on Luo Xiao's side.
Although Luo Xiao had fallen, his schemes were far-reaching. Mu Haiji was always wary of him, afraid that he'd make a comeback one day.
When the two guards heard Mu Haiji's words, they turned pale with fright.
"Supreme Saint, don't be so agitated …"
"Our Great Sage will be here soon!"
The two guards were afraid that Mu Haiji would anger Dongfang Chunjun. If he took his anger out on them, they'd be in trouble.
Why would Mu Haiji care about them?
In his anger, he took a deep breath and said loudly, "Great Sage of the East, Mu Haiji has something to discuss with you. Please come out!"
This voice was loud and clear from afar, and it traveled all the way through the layers of palaces ….
In the exquisite and beautiful rear palace.
A plate of orange sandalwood was burning slowly. A wisp of dark blue smoke rose into the air, emitting a refreshing fragrance.
That fragrance could soothe one's soul, make one feel refreshed, and stabilize one's state of mind.
This sandalwood was the famous Builder Sandalwood of the North Sea. It was extracted from the tree branches of the Builder Tree, and it was considered an extremely valuable item in God's Domain. Even wealthy families would find it hard to obtain.
Especially in this Divine Epoch, the Builder Wood had transformed into layers of death aura, turning an area of 33 million kilometers into a region of death. This Builder Wood Sandalwood could only be even rarer …
Dongfang Chunjun sat there quietly, looking at the glass box not far away with a deep gaze.
Luo Shui had not responded to him for a long time …
He could only blame himself for being incompetent.
After all these years of cruising in the Sea of Time, he still couldn't find Luo Shui's body.
There were too many fragments in the Sea of Time, accumulated over countless divine epochs. Every moment, every breath, every instant turned into fragments of time …
It was indeed too difficult for Dongfang Chunjun.
At that moment, Mu Haiji's resonant voice sounded from afar.
Due to the fluctuations of this voice, the wisp of dark blue smoke slowly emanating from the Builder Wood Sandalwood shook violently.
Dongfang Chunjun frowned slightly. His eyes flashed, and he slowly disappeared from the palace …
After Dongfang Chunjun left, Li Luoshui's head slowly rose from the glass box and opened her eyes slightly.
A trace of thought appeared in her sad but beautiful eyes.
Although she had been in a deep sleep for the past few years, she still paid attention to any small movements.
That voice just now was Mu Haiji's …
What could have happened to make a Saint so anxious?
Did Taibai Jinxing make a move?
Or did Zheng 'er return …
A bitter smile appeared on Li Luoshui's face when she thought of her son.
At that time, throwing Luo Zheng out of God's Domain was completely a gamble that she had no choice but to make.
The chaos was also filled with traps and danger …
Although Zheng 'er did not fear the corrosion of the chaos, his cultivation was still too shallow. Whether or not he could survive was a big problem.
The hope of him returning was too slim …
Li Luoshui faintly sighed and slowly sank into the brown liquid.
… …
… …
Accompanied by a spatial fluctuation, Dongfang Chunjun appeared in front of Mu Haiji with a smile on his face. "Brother Haiji, just what is it that has made you so anxious?"
Mu Haiji's expression was indignant. "A powerhouse has visited God's Domain!"
"A powerhouse?" Dongfang Chunjun raised an eyebrow. "What kind of powerhouse? Don't tell me it's Gu Bei? "
Nowadays, Saints rarely left God's Domain. The only one who did was Gu Bei, who wandered outside of God's Domain.
And Gu Bei stood on Luo Xiao's side … …
If Gu Bei returned, Mu Haiji would indeed be on guard.
"If it's Gu Bei, then it's really fun!" Mu Haiji said gloomily. "Someone created a spatial tunnel outside God's Domain. The construction of this spatial tunnel is extremely profound. Even if I personally attacked in Doji's Domain, I couldn't destroy it!"
"What!"
This finally raised Dongfang Chunjun's guard.
Saints were said to be omnipotent in their own Domain. Thus, it was best to never attack them in another's Domain … …
If even Mu Haiji couldn't destroy a spatial tunnel in Doji's Domain, then what kind of existence was the one who created it?
However, Dongfang Chunjun's mind quickly calmed down. He narrowed his eyes and said, "It's said that there's a spatial barrier outside God's Domain. Even if a spatial tunnel is created, it's still impossible to enter. Opening that barrier isn't something that can be done in a short period of time."
"But the Xuan Yuan Wei also came in?" Mu Haiji asked.
Mu Haiji smiled faintly. "They spent twenty years to open the spatial barrier!"
"Then we only have twenty years!" Mu Haiji's eyebrows twitched.
Twenty years, to a Saint, was nothing more than the snap of a finger … …
It could be said that this crisis was imminent. Who knew where the other party came from?
Mu Haiji muttered to himself, "Moreover … the space channel was constructed all the way to the Realm of the Gods. Someone must have been sent here. In order to prevent me from finding out, the space channel was divided into thirteen parts and scattered across the thirteen great domains. It was probably to prevent me from investigating!"

Chapter 2264
The Divine Realm was so big that it would be impossible to guess with just this little clue.

Inside the Divine Luan Hall …
Dongfang Taiqing stood on the spot and looked down at the iron hand that was shining with silver light.

Hua Tianming was now heavily relied on by Dongfang Chunjun.
"Don't worry, I definitely won't die in front of you." Hua Tianming's sword-like eyebrows rose slightly. With a smile, his face was free and at ease.
In the Dongfang Clan, Dongfang Taiqing was second only to the Saints and Quasi-Saints.
The other Paragons of the Dongfang Clan were far inferior to him. They could not compete with him at all. When the next Saint Mountain descends, Dongfang Taiqing will definitely compete for the position of Saint, and the Dongfang family will most likely support him!
However, the appearance of "Heavenly Sword", also known as Hua Tianming, made Dongfang Taiqing feel threatened.
In the past few years, Dongfang Chunjun had summoned Sky Sword several times. The first time, he had given Sky Sword a pair of green and yellow swords. Later, when Sky Sword and Dongfang Yingqing got married, Dongfang Chunjun had even given Sky Sword the Fusang Token. This meant that he had recognized Sky Sword as a core member of the Dongfang Family!
Not only that, but the Dongfang Family's resources were also greatly tilted towards Sky Sword, and Sky Sword's cultivation was also advancing by leaps and bounds!
When he was an upper True God, he was already unstoppable. Now that he had stepped into the Paragons, Dongfang Taiqing was very uneasy.
Dongfang Taiqing felt that his position was wavering, so he naturally went against Sky Sword.
He had once secretly investigated Sky Sword and found out that Sky Sword's origin was very unclear. Every time this fellow returned from the Forbidden Grounds, he would quietly leave the Sea of Time and disappear for a period of time. During this period of time, one or even several Divine Cities would be destroyed, and the stone slabs protecting those Divine Cities would disappear.
Dongfang Taiqing had once reported this to Dongfang Chunjun and the other elders of the Dongfang Clan.
Perhaps because Sky Sword's performance was too perfect, Dongfang Taiqing's guesses and suspicions did not attract attention at all.
There were even elders who advised Dongfang Taiqing that the Dongfang Clan was in need of talents now, so there was no need to be jealous of talents. Although Sky Sword was an outsider, he had already married Dongfang Yingqing and should treat him as a member of the clan.
Those elders thought that Dongfang Taiqing was jealous of Sky Sword, which made Dongfang Taiqing feel very sullen.
When these two met, how could they have anything nice to say?

Someone who could enter the Divine Territory from the Chaos was at least a Saint. Asking them, who were at the Perfection stage, to find someone was not just looking for a person, but seeking death …
Dongfang Chunjun, of course, saw through Dongfang Taiqing's doubts and said, "The other party is only a True God. His cultivation is at most at the Perfection stage."
Hearing this, Hua Tianming and Dongfang Taiqing were even more puzzled …
A True God could return from the Chaos unscathed? What the hell was that?

After receiving the order, the two of them looked at each other and immediately left the Divine Luan Hall.

…
After arriving here, Luo Zheng originally planned to activate the Gen Token and leave quickly.
After all, the space tunnel passed through Mu Haiji's Ultimate Realm and could very well lead to trouble …
However, Luo Zheng was quickly attracted by an extremely rich Taoist Connotation in the distance.
"This Taoist Connotation doesn't seem to be emitted by a True God." Luo Zheng was slightly puzzled.
Out of curiosity, Luo Zheng floated up and flew forward in the air.
After passing two mountains, Luo Zheng saw a tall stone tablet at the foot of the mountain.
"That's … a Dao tablet!"
Floating in the air, Luo Zheng's eyes flashed slightly.
He knew that there were three thousand Dao tablets in the Divine Territory. Within them were three thousand Divine Daos, which were the three thousand ultimate techniques that Chiyou possessed.
Luo Zheng had seen replicas of these tablets in the Han Family's ancestral land.
Using the Immeasurable Ruler in his mind, he could quickly understand the Taoist Connotation within the tablets and copy it into the Immeasurable Ruler.
However, no matter if it was the Taoist Connotation of a True God or the Taoist Connotation of an imitation, it could be incomplete …

Chapter 2265
This was the first time Luo Zheng had seen a real Dao tablet, so he was naturally very curious.
He even gave up on the idea of leaving. Following the mountain stream between the two mountains, Luo Zheng flew downwards.
On this huge green Dao tablet, there were streaks of azure lightning flashing. These streaks of lightning spread out along the Dao tablet like little snakes, drilling into the surrounding ground.
Therefore, within a radius of ten thousand feet around this green Dao tablet, not even a blade of grass grew!
Around this Dao tablet, there were tens of thousands of people sitting cross-legged, facing it.
These people had all come because of its reputation, so they naturally hoped to attain the Divine Dao through comprehending the Dao essence …
The vast majority of people were ten thousand feet away from the Dao tablet, and those who dared to come within ten thousand feet were few and far between.
The closer one was to the Dao tablet, the greater the pressure of the Dao essence.
In the past, Luo Zheng had experienced it in Han Jia's Godly Inquiring Palace. Especially for those martial artists who had attained the Divine Dao, if they were too close to the Dao tablet, the result would be like moths to a flame, and they would be devoured by the Dao essence.
Luo Zheng flew down from the mountain stream, quietly landing on the ground. After concealing his cultivation, he slowly walked towards the Dao tablet.
"So it's the Mystic Thunder Divine Dao." As he approached, Luo Zheng's eyes flashed slightly.
The Mystic Thunder Divine Dao was an extremely common Divine Dao in the Realm of the Gods. Even Luo Zheng had seen several True Gods use this Divine Dao.
Luo Zheng's Immeasurable Ruler also recorded this Divine Dao, and it should be very complete.
Luo Zheng still wanted to verify it again. His father must have given him the Immeasurable Ruler for a deep reason. If there was a chance, Luo Zheng would definitely visit all the Dao tablets in the Realm of the Gods.
"Stop!"
When Luo Zheng was still more than ten li away from the Dao tablet, three upper True Gods flew over from afar.
These three upper True Gods suddenly landed in front of Luo Zheng, blocking his path.
The leading True God looked arrogant. He stared coldly at Luo Zheng and said, "You also want to comprehend the Dao stele?"
Luo Zheng looked at the three of them, nodded slightly, and said kindly, "Yes, I also want to comprehend the Dao stele."
"This Dao Stele belongs to the Fang Clan. If you want to comprehend it, you'll have to work for the Fang Clan for two hundred years and pay twenty Shenwu Coins!" said the True God in the lead.
Hearing this, Luo Zheng frowned slightly.
It turned out that the Dao Stele was occupied by the Fang Clan. Presumably, this large territory belonged to the Fang Clan.
Time was worthless to most True Gods in God's Domain. Two hundred years was an extremely short period of time.
In order to comprehend a deeper level of the Divine Dao, such a small price was insignificant.
Twenty Shenwu coins were nothing, but he could not afford to waste time.
Seeing Luo Zheng hesitate, the True God snorted coldly. "If you don't want to, then scram!"
Luo Zheng did not want to cause trouble here. He smiled, took two steps forward, and said softly, "I can pay a bit more, but can I be exempted from the hard labor?"
Hearing this, the eyes of the three upper True Gods brightened slightly.
The True Gods who came to comprehend the Dao Stele were all commoners. If they were True Gods with real power, they did not need to comprehend the Dao Stele at all. Their own clans could imitate the Dao Stele and teach them the complete Divine Dao.
To those people who were born with humble beginnings, two hundred years was nothing, but to ask them to fork out twenty Shenwu coins was a pain in the ass.
Now that Luo Zheng was willing, they were naturally happy to do so.
"Sure. Fifty Shenwu Coins in exchange for two hundred years of hard labor. You only need to pay seventy Shenwu Coins," said the True God in the lead with a chuckle.
They were actually asking for an exorbitant price. Generally speaking, twenty Shenwu Coins could be exchanged for two hundred years of hard labor.
Since Luo Zheng had taken the initiative to ask, they naturally wanted to extort him! The extra thirty Shenwu Coins could be divided among the three of them.
"Sure." Luo Zheng smiled. A few dozen Shenwu Coins were too easy for Luo Zheng …
With the amount of Shenwu Coins Luo Zheng had accumulated, even the wealthy families could not compare to him.
Luo Zheng waved his hand, and seventy Shenwu Coins were stacked neatly and presented to the True God.
The True God in the lead took it with a laugh and waved behind him. "You can go now!"
Luo Zheng cupped his hands and walked through the crowd, toward the Dao Stele …
The three upper True Gods quickly divided up Luo Zheng's Shenwu Coins.
"Sigh, Old Seven, this kid took out seventy Shenwu Coins so easily. You should have asked for a higher price!"
"That's right. Looking at him, he probably has a lot of Shenwu Coins on him!"
The two upper True Gods' eyes were filled with greed. Clearly, they saw Luo Zheng as a fat sheep.
The True God called Old Seven stared at Luo Zheng's back, regret showing on his face. If he had known that this guy was so generous, he would have asked for two hundred Shenwu Coins. This kind of opportunity was hard to come by!
"This guy is alone and probably has no background. Once he leaves this place, we three brothers will rob and kill him," Old Seven said with a murderous look on his face.
"That's a good idea, hehe …" The other upper True God rubbed his palms together.
Although the three of them had lowered their voices, Luo Zheng's hearing was far superior to that of ordinary True Gods. Naturally, he heard them clearly.
Luo Zheng's lips curled into a cold smile. It seemed that even if some idiots cultivated to godhood, they would still die from greed …
However, Luo Zheng did not have the time to deal with them.
The Dao Connotation of this Dao Stele was much stronger than the Dao Connotation of the Pseudo Dao Steles in the Han Family ancestral land.
"Crackle!"
When he was within ten thousand feet of the Dao Stele, dazzling electric arcs shot out from the barren land and spread up his legs.
Some of the Proof of Divinity martial artists and even the lower True Gods were horrified when they saw this scene.
It should be known that the True Gods who dared to enter ten thousand feet of the Dao Stele already had an extraordinary understanding of the Mystic Lightning Divine Way. Most of them were upper True Gods and even Perfect True Gods.
After Luo Zheng hid his cultivation, he was only a lower True God. Yet, he dared to cross the ten thousand feet lightning pool. His courage was too great!
"This lower True God is courting death by entering the lightning pool!"
"I think he can only go a few hundred feet!"
"Even three hundred to five hundred feet is extremely difficult …"
The onlookers were shocked, but Luo Zheng was as calm as if he was taking a stroll in the park.
The closer he got to the Dao Stele, the more electric arcs spread to Luo Zheng's body.
Under everyone's gazes, he had already traveled a distance of four hundred meters. Numerous lightning snakes as thick as an arm emerged from the lightning pool and wrapped around Luo Zheng's entire body in layers …
Ten thousand zhang away, the expressions of the three upper true gods turned ugly at once.
Even the three of them only dared to get close to the Dao Stele to comprehend the Mystic Lightning Divine Way.
This guy was only a lower True God, yet he could walk four thousand feet as he pleased. Moreover, it seemed like he had no intention of stopping.

Chapter 2266
Within a thousand zhang radius, there were only a few upper True Gods scattered around.
These upper rank true gods were the same as Luo Zheng. They were also coiled by lightning snakes from head to toe.
These upper True Gods had a profound understanding of the Mystic Thunder Divine Dao. They were meditating here in order to grasp that tiny bit of opportunity to comprehend the perfected divine ability of the Mystic Thunder Divine Dao and break through to the perfected True God Realm!
However, after countless divine epochs, the number of upper True Gods was not small. However, the number of True Gods who successfully broke through to the Perfection True God Realm was pitifully small.
When Luo Zheng was three hundred zhang away from the Dao Stele, an old man sitting on the ground nearby muttered, "Young man, although you are born with a special talent and are not afraid of the Dao Essence's pressure, comprehending the Divine Dao requires gradual progress. You are just a lower True God. You don't have to be so close."
This old man was advising him out of kindness. Luo Zheng smiled and cupped his hands towards the old man. "Thank you for the reminder. However, I have another way to get close to the Dao Stele."
The old man's eyes revealed a trace of doubt, but he did not say anything else.
When Luo Zheng was only ten zhang away from the Dao Stele, he finally stood still.
At this moment, the entire Dao Stele was roaring.
Huge lightning dragons circled around Luo Zheng crazily. They roared angrily and were determined to tear Luo Zheng to shreds.
However, Luo Zheng was still as steady as Mount Tai. He stood on the spot and did not move at all.
This distance should be enough …
Facing the Dao Stele of the Mystic Lightning Divine Dao, Luo Zheng activated the Immeasurable Ruler in his mind.
"Buzz!"
One end of the Immeasurable Ruler emitted a dazzling light in Luo Zheng's mind.
At the same time, two golden halos appeared at the edge of Luo Zheng's pupils!
"Absorb the Mystic Lightning Divine Dao again!"
"Buzz buzz buzz …"
The Immeasurable Ruler vibrated continuously. On the scale that contained the Mystic Lightning Divine Dao, the lightning symbol became more rounded and full!
The Divine Dao that Luo Zheng absorbed from a Perfection True God was a Perfection True God.
Back when Luo Zheng was in the Temple of the Gods of Inquiry, the Dao tablet of the Divine Dao of Purple Qi was created for Aunt Han Jiu.
However, when Aunt Han Jiu created that dao tablet, she still hadn't been able to comprehend the perfect level divine ability "Purple Sky Extinction Ground" of the Purple Clouds Divine Dao. Therefore, Luo Zheng's Purple Clouds Divine Dao was also incomplete …
If he were to absorb the Divine Dao from a lower True God, he would only be able to absorb the first two levels of Divine Dao …
Luo Zheng's Mystic Thunder Divine Dao was naturally perfected after facing the dao tablet of the Mystic Thunder Divine Dao. This was because he was facing a complete divine dao. Whether it was the first three stages of the Mystic Thunder Divine Dao, the last three stages, or even perfected divine arts, he could freely use them all!
After the scale on the Infinite Ruler was filled, the small piece of godhead in Luo Zheng's internal world that corresponded to the Mystic Thunder Divine Dao also approached perfection. At the same time, the light that was projected by that godhead also started to reach perfection …
Luo Zheng just stood there stupidly, attracting a burst of surprised looks from behind.
Whether it was an upper True God, a lower True God, or those martial artists who had proven themselves to be gods, they were all discussing in low voices.
They didn't know what Luo Zheng wanted to do, and they couldn't see through Luo Zheng's strength even more.
After a long time …
A satisfied smile appeared on Luo Zheng's face.
Just as he was about to disperse the pair of golden eyes and leave, a strange scene appeared.
From the center of this dao tablet, a unique triangular pattern appeared, and in the center of that triangular pattern, a small eye appeared.
From that eye, an arc of light suddenly shot out. This ray of light directly entered Luo Zheng's mind, passed through Luo Zheng's soul, and connected with the Infinite Ruler.
When this ray of light penetrated Luo Zheng's soul, Luo Zheng felt as if his soul was being pricked by a needle.
At the same time that he felt a slight pain, a miraculous scene suddenly appeared in his mind!
"What is this?"
Luo Zheng's face was full of disbelief.
In his mind, numerous jet-black Dao tablets began to appear …
"The positions of the 3,000 dao tablets?"
The 3,000 dao tablets were distributed in the Divine Realm according to a certain distance. The top of these dao tablets were connected with each other by arcs.
But now, the other 2,999 dao tablets were jet-black. Only the dao tablet of the Profound Thunder Divine Dao in front of Luo Zheng was lit up …
"This is a guide!" Luo Zheng's eyes lit up. "The Infinite Ruler wants to guide me to light up all the dao tablets?"
The positions of most of the dao tablets in the Divine Realm were recorded, so it wasn't too difficult to find them.
But there were still some dao tablets whose positions were very hidden …
"The dao tablet of the Thick Earth Divine Dao is actually located in the Builder Tree. It's rumored that that place has turned into a dead zone. Ordinary people can't get close at all!"
"This is the dao tablet of the Great Yan Divine Dao. Father is the first person to completely comprehend it and leave his name on it …"
"The dao tablet of the Emotion Severing Divine Dao? How could it be left in the Sea of Time? Did someone move it to the Sea of Time, or … "

At this moment, he did not choose to dodge. Instead, a small black snake appeared in his right palm …
"Pu!"
The Li Water True Arrow hit Luo Zheng and pierced through Luo Zheng's body!
"It hit?"
"This arrow will cripple him even if he doesn't die!"
Tang Qi and Tang Que's faces were filled with excitement.
This time, the entire Wealthy Clan Alliance had come out in full force. They had originally thought that this mission would be extremely difficult. They had not expected that they would succeed in one strike.
However, the excitement on their faces only lasted for two breaths of time before it froze on their faces.
In their line of sight, Luo Zheng's figure flashed strangely for a moment. The wound that had been pierced by the Li Water True Arrow actually disappeared.
In just three breaths of time, Luo Zheng had used the imperial jade seal to erase the wound …
Tang Qi's arrow had also destroyed the water net binding him, allowing Luo Zheng to regain his freedom.
"Pipa!"
The enormous lightning wings on Luo Zheng's back flapped lightly. He transformed into a thick bolt of lightning and shot down from the sky.
The Shadow Bearing Sword in his hand was already pressed against Tang Qi's abdomen. Tang Qi stared straight into Luo Zheng's emotionless eyes. His heart could not help but tremble.
At this moment, an indescribable feeling surfaced in Tang Qi's heart.
He felt that he was not facing a living being, or even an object. Instead, he felt as if he was facing a void existence …
Accompanied by this strange feeling, Luo Zheng stabbed forward with the Shadow Bearing Sword.
From bottom to top, he shattered Tang Qi's dantian, then his internal organs, and finally his soul hidden in his mind!
Whoosh!

Chapter 2271
Tang Que didn't expect Luo Zheng to dodge that arrow so easily.
He didn't expect that Luo Zheng would use that arrow to break free from his water net and even kill Tang Qi with a sword …
How could this be?
Tang Que was frightened. He kicked his legs and quickly retreated on the sea surface.
His retreating speed was extremely fast, but Luo Zheng was even faster!
Luo Zheng's sword ray hadn't reached Tang Que when Luo Zheng turned into a thunderbolt and rushed up.

Tang Que retreated and suddenly waved his arms.
Dozens of waterspouts rose from the sea surface and rolled toward Luo Zheng.
"Cha cha cha cha …"

These waterspouts rushing toward Luo Zheng couldn't withstand the Chengying Sword's slashes. They quickly fell apart.

After breaking these waterspouts, Luo Zheng got close to Tang Que and pierced his chest with his sword.
He swung his long sword and blocked Tang Que's whole body behind him.
On the other hand, the sword light that Luo Zheng had slashed out earlier whistled over at this moment …
Seeing that this sword ray was about to kill Tang Que, Tang Que suddenly gritted his teeth and a strange power escaped from his dantian.
"Hualala …"
At this moment, his body showed a transparent luster. His whole body was deformed and turned into a mass of water. It flowed along the Chengying Sword and into the sea.
This was the consummate level magical power of the Li Water Magical Power, "Formless Water Disintegration".
This magical power could dissolve the physical body in an instant and then merge with the other water bodies.
If there was enough time, this magical power could even control the entire sea area and summon the wind and rain.
Tang Que used this magical power to escape in a hurry. He was really desperate.
After all, the space in this world was also sealed. Tang Que couldn't escape.
They had blocked Luo Zheng's escape route, and at the same time, they had also blocked their own escape route. However, they had never thought that they would be facing such a powerful monster.

"Fledgling Fire Divine Dao!"

A blazing fireball spread out from under the water, enveloping the water within a radius of ten miles around Luo Zheng.

Tang Que, who had been using "Formless Hydrolysis", was also completely evaporated …
From the moment Luo Zheng broke free from the water net to killing Tang Qi and then evaporating Tang Que, this series of actions only took one or two breaths.
His speed was so fast that no one could react.
Tang Wan, who was standing on top of the mountain, was completely dumbfounded when she saw this scene.
Her two junior brothers were not weak, but they were killed by Luo Zheng in an instant. Tang Wan found it hard to accept the scene in front of her.
"Whoosh whoosh …"
After Luo Zheng evaporated all the seawater within a radius of ten miles, the surrounding seawater continued to surge in, filling up the vacuum under his feet.
"If you want to die, then feel free to follow me. I don't mind taking your lives." Luo Zheng's voice was as cold as ten thousand year old ice.

Mu Xuerong had been very cautious and had not chosen to fight Luo Zheng head-on.

The guy that they had run over before had become unstoppable!

In the end, Mu Xuerong was much stronger than Tang Que and Tang Qi. It would not be easy for Luo Zheng to deal with her …
"Luo Zheng! I will definitely kill you today! "Tang Wan roared towards the sea.
He stared in the direction that the three of them had fled. Carrying the huge sword and shield, he clenched his teeth and followed …
Luo Zheng sped along the coastline that connected the two large domains.
The length of this coastline exceeded his expectations.
As he sped along, two more black dots surrounded him from afar.
"Two more Paragons!"
These two Great Perfection True Gods were two white-haired elders. They were from the Leng Family.
Unlike Tang Que and Tang Qi, they did not choose to attack Luo Zheng immediately upon seeing him. Instead, they tried to maintain a certain distance from Luo Zheng.

The Qin family, the Zhao family, the Leng family …
When these Paragons first saw Luo Zheng, they were all surprised.
At the same time, they wondered why Fang Henshao had sent them a Quintessential Essence transmission immediately, strongly warning them not to attack.
This deepened the curiosity of these Paragons.
Although Luo Zheng had stepped into the Paragons, he did not look that scary, right?
These Paragons did not act rashly. They only surrounded Luo Zheng, surrounding him from all directions.
Luo Zheng just looked on coldly, minding his own business as he flew forward.
In the Serpent Region …
Hua Tianming used the teleportation formation and arrived at the god city at the border.
After he led the two Paragons from the Dongfang family out of the god city, he started to use greater teleportation continuously.
Now that all the Paragons were rushing towards the Serpent Region, Hua Tianming naturally could not fall behind.
When Hua Tianming teleported to the border of the Serpent Region, he could see the continuous mountain range on his left and the undulating sea on his right. This was the boundary between the Square Heaven Region and the Serpent Region.
"The space between the Serpent Region and the Square Heaven Region is sealed. After entering, we can no longer use greater teleportation. If we follow the coastline and surround them, we should be able to block the target in the Serpent Region," Hua Tianming said calmly.
"We can also ambush them in the Serpent Region, right?" a Paragons from the Dongfang family said.
Hua Tianming shook his head. "After entering the Serpent Region, the target can use greater teleportation to escape. The prerequisite is that the other party has the cultivation of a Paragons. Looking at the current situation, it should be possible …"
If it were not for the Paragons, Mu Xuerong and Fang Henshao, who had sent out the news first, would have killed the target long ago. It would not have been delayed until now.
However, Hua Tianming was slightly puzzled. The other party was only a Paragons. With Mu Xuerong and the rest's abilities, it would not be too difficult to deal with them. Why had it been delayed until now?

Chapter 2272

As a Proof God expert, he had consecutively won against those of a higher level, kidnapped several Perfection True Gods from the Time Sea forbidden area, and finally escaped with the quasi-sage from Han Jia …
All sorts of rumors about Luo Zheng made Hua Tianming secretly speechless.
However, these rumors came to an abrupt end ten years ago. From then on, Hua Tianming couldn't find any news about Luo Zheng.
This once made Hua Tianming somewhat worried. Could Luo Zheng have fallen somewhere? He never expected to encounter Luo Zheng under such circumstances. Luo Zheng was the target of their group!
At this moment, Hua Tianming was also in a state of worry. How could Luo Zheng escape under such circumstances?
Under the watchful eyes of so many people, he couldn't help Luo Zheng. After all, Hua Tianming had infiltrated the Dongfang Clan with great difficulty. After all, he had also gained Dongfang Chunjun's trust after all these years of operation. He had a more important mission to complete.
Seeing that Luo Zheng was getting closer and closer to Dongfang Taiqing, Hua Tianming's heart became more and more tangled. However, his expression was still as calm as water, and no clues could be seen.

Chapter 2273

The Divine Fate Five Spirit Body was a fusion of the Spiritual Wind, Thick Earth, Li Water, Fledgling Fire, and Green Wood Divine Dao. This wasn't a natural fusion like the Divine Origin Magnetic Mountain, but a Mystique produced by comprehending the Five Elements Divine Dao to the extreme.
Five different colors quickly appeared on the surface of Luo Zheng's body. In addition to Luo Zheng's extremely powerful body, the sturdiness of his body was beyond imagination.

The Golden Stellar Divine Dao was extremely ferocious, and Dongfang Taiqing had refined his right hand for countless years, consuming countless resources from the Dongfang Family …
The Golden Stellar Annihilation Hand contained such terrifying power that even a quasi-sage would have to be careful when dealing with it.

This attack was enough to crush Luo Zheng …

What was up with that giant hand?
Dongfang Taiqing was still immersed in his confusion when a huge force was transmitted back from the iron hand.
This force was so strong that Dongfang Taiqing couldn't resist it at all. He was sent flying backward, or rather, he was sent flying by Luo Zheng.
His iron hand was as huge as a mountain, but his body was only six feet tall. It was as if the huge iron hand had flown away with Dongfang Taiqing …
However, no one was paying attention to Dongfang Taiqing now.
All the Perfection True Gods were staring at the cloud of smoke that was slowly dissipating.

Luo Zheng had activated the power of the Rage God in his body and turned into a forty thousand feet tall giant. He stood tall in the sky and looked down at everyone.
Compared to Luo Zheng, these Perfection True Gods were as tiny as ants …

"What a powerful force! How did he do it?! "
"In terms of force, he's even stronger than a Saint …"
All the Perfection True Gods present were dumbfounded.
The force that these Perfection True Gods felt from Luo Zheng's body was actually the power of the Rage God.
However, they had never seen the power of the Rage God, so they naturally mistook it for pure force. One had to know that even a Saint couldn't create such an aura with nine stars of the Spiritual Pedestal Tier …
In the distance, Hua Tianming saw this scene and raised his eyebrows. He was also extremely shocked.
No matter how imaginative Hua Tianming was, he didn't think that Luo Zheng could turn into such a giant …
He had never heard of any Mystical Power in the divine territory that could make a person bigger than a mountain. Moreover, after Luo Zheng transformed into a giant, his power obviously increased greatly. Hua Tianming felt that such a method was even more unbelievable!

Chapter 2274

Luo Zheng was indeed only at Perfect Real God, but his current strength was probably something that even a Quasi-sage would find difficult to contend with …
Even if they attacked together now, they wouldn't be able to capture Luo Zheng and would only add to the casualties!

Even though they couldn't defeat Luo Zheng, it wouldn't be too much of a problem for them to escape.
Hence, a group of Perfect Real Gods followed behind Luo Zheng in a mighty manner as if they were escorting a king.
When Luo Zheng passed by Hua Tianming, he secretly glanced at him and used his True Core voice transmission to say, "Brother Tianming."
After Hua Tianming heard this True Core voice transmission, he floated up.
A True Core voice transmission would transmit a slight wave during the transmission process, and all the Perfect Real Gods present would be able to detect it.
In order to prevent the other Perfect Real Gods from detecting it, Hua Tianming deliberately rushed to the front of all the Perfect Real Gods. In this way, the wave of True Core voice transmission he sent to Luo Zheng wouldn't be detected by others. Moreover, Hua Tianming's performance over the years had always been a bold and skillful person.
When others saw Hua Tianming following closely behind Luo Zheng, they would also think that Hua Tianming wasn't too afraid of Luo Zheng.
"Brother Luo Zheng, I haven't seen you in these years. Your great improvement has already put me to shame," Hua Tianming's lips moved slightly as he spoke using True Core voice transmission.

"The Dongfang family," Hua Tianming said.
Hearing this answer, Luo Zheng was slightly surprised.
When he was in the Great Yan Universe, he had heard Xingwei mention that Hua Tianming had his own destiny.
He didn't expect that after coming to the Divine Plane, he would indeed take a completely different path. He actually hid in the Dongfang family …
However, thinking of the Dongfang family, Luo Zheng's heart skipped a beat.
That day, he had met his mother underground. After that, he had used the spirit crow to cultivate and was directly sent out of the Divine Plane by his mother.
His mother had told him that those Saints had found his mother. Until now, Luo Zheng still didn't know what his mother's fate was. Had she been captured by Dongfang Chunjun?
"When you were in the Dongfang family, have you ever heard of a person called Li Luoshui?" Luo Zheng suddenly asked.
"Li Luoshui?" Hua Tianming had never heard of this unfamiliar name and replied, "I've never heard of her. Who is this Li Luoshui?"
Hearing Hua Tianming's words, Luo Zheng's heart sank slightly.

"She's my mother," Luo Zheng replied.
