"Ten years ago, Mother forcibly sent me away in order to save me. She might have fallen into Dongfang Chunjun's hands," Luo Zheng continued.
Hua Tianming's eyes flashed slightly. After pondering for a moment, he said, "All these years, I've been highly regarded by Dongfang Chunjun. I can enter and leave any place on the Dongfang family's floating island at will, except for the rear palace of the floating island. I've heard a Dongfang family quasi-sage complain that Great Sage Chunjun is addicted to women and hasn't made any progress in these years …"

Chapter 2275
"What do you mean by being obsessed with women?" Luo Zheng asked anxiously.
"Legend has it that Great Sage Chunjun was obsessed with a woman. This woman was placed in the rear palace. All these years, he entered the Time Sea several times to search for her original body. I don't know if this woman … is your mother," Hua Tianming replied.
"Searching for her original body …"
Luo Zheng's heart skipped a beat as if a rock had been lifted from his chest. A sense of relief came from the bottom of his heart.
This meant that this woman was most likely his mother. She was not dead, and Dongfang Chunjun was still helping her find her original body …
No matter what, she was not dead …
Hua Tianming sensed something strange from Luo Zheng's silence and asked, "Could that woman be your mother?"
"She should be," Luo Zheng replied.
This time, it was Hua Tianming's turn to be surprised.
Luo Zheng's father, Luo Xiao, and Mu Haiji were mortal enemies. Hua Tianming had gradually learned the truth over the years. Mu Haiji, who had started the Universal War, was only Dongfang Chunjun's henchman. In other words, Luo Xiao's real enemy was the Wealthy Alliance led by Dongfang Chunjun.
What was up with Luo Zheng's mother, Li Luoshui? Why was Dongfang Chunjun so obsessed with her?
Hua Tianming was also confused …
Luo Zheng naturally did not have the chance to explain too much to Hua Tianming. Dozens of miles ahead of him was the intersection of the three large territories. Once he left the Square Sky Region and the Sea Sky Region, he would try to teleport again.
At this moment, Dongfang Taiqing spoke again to the other Perfected True Gods through Vital Energy voice transmission. "Everyone, Luo Zheng will definitely use teleportation to escape after entering the Serpent Territory! Teleportation is not restricted in the Serpent Territory. I want to try again! "
Everyone was very unwilling to let Luo Zheng leave just like that.
"How? This guy is much stronger than us. We should be thanking the heavens and earth if he doesn't come to kill us, "a Perfect True God said helplessly.
The other Great Perfection Real Gods also looked resentful. Dozens of Great Perfection Real Gods from aristocratic families had been intimidated by Luo Zheng alone. It was really embarrassing. However, looking at the giant creature that was tens of thousands of feet tall, who would dare to challenge him?
Dongfang Taiqing smiled faintly and suddenly asked, "Apart from this body, is he really that much stronger than us? If the space here wasn't sealed, would you still be afraid of him? "
Hearing that, the eyes of those Perfect Real Gods brightened …
"That's right. It's just his physical body that's strong. What he relies on is extremely powerful strength!"
"If it wasn't for the spatial seal, he wouldn't have been able to touch me!"
"In the end, he's still a Perfected True God, not a quasi-sage …"
The higher the level of one's strength, the less significant the effect of strength would be.
When one reached the Saint Stage, they would all use their great magical powers. It was rare to see a pure contest of strength. The key reason was that it was very difficult to pour all of one's strength onto the other party.
Luo Zheng's Rage God Body was indeed powerful, but once he entered the Serpentine Domain, his threat would be greatly reduced. Who would be foolish enough to stand there and let Luo Zheng smash them?
When that time came, Luo Zheng would definitely not be a match for these Great Perfection True Gods!
"But once Luo Zheng enters the Spirit Snake Domain, he can also use Greater Teleportation. It won't be that easy to keep him here," Mu Xuerong said indifferently.
Dongfang Taiqing smiled faintly. "No problem, I've brought the Floating Earth Bell."
Hearing the three words, Floating Soil Bell, all the Perfect Real Gods' eyes glowed.
This Floating Earth Bell was a spatial magic treasure, and it was the only one in the entire Divine Realm.
The bell was made from the floating soil beneath the Space Divine Dao Stele. The floating soil had accumulated under the Space Divine Dao Stele for countless years, and under the influence of the Dao, it had become extremely spiritual. Of course, spatial magic treasures made from this soil were also extraordinary.
However, after the Floating Earth Bell was excavated, the Space Divine Dao Dao Stele remained suspended in the air. This material could be considered extinct, and no one could replicate it.
If they used the Floating Earth Bell to envelop Luo Zheng, they could also cut off his Greater Teleportation ability. At that time, no matter how strong Luo Zheng was, he would be a lamb waiting to be slaughtered in front of everyone.
The morale of the Great Perfection Real Gods, which was originally low, gradually rose again.
But what they didn't know was that at the same time Dongfang Taiqing sent a true essence sound transmission, Hua Tianming had also "passed" on their plans to Luo Zheng!
"Summoning this Floating Earth Bell will also violate the laws of space and incur the punishment of the Governing Stone Tablet. Therefore, Dongfang Taiqing will use it after entering the Snake Spirit World," Hua Tianming said in a low voice.
"I understand." Luo Zheng smiled coldly. Now that the Snake Spirit World was only a step away, he naturally wouldn't foolishly wait for Dongfang Taiqing to summon this magic treasure. He then said to Hua Tianming, "If you can find other Dao Disciples, please ask them to come find me in the Immortal Abode in Takeshi Xuan Forest! Brother Tianming, we'll meet again someday! "
As soon as he finished speaking, Luo Zheng suddenly turned around.
Without any warning, Luo Zheng's gigantic hand, which was over 100 meters wide, swatted at Dongfang Taiqing!
Dongfang Taiqing was calculating the time to summon the Floating Earth Bell, and the other Great Perfection Real Gods were also waiting to act.
Once Luo Zheng's space was sealed by the Floating Earth Bell, they would attack again …
They never would've thought that Luo Zheng would suddenly attack Dongfang Taiqing at this critical moment!
"Huff huff …"
Dongfang Taiqing's expression changed drastically when he saw Luo Zheng's slap.
Just now, when he used all his strength to punch Luo Zheng, his iron hand was on the verge of shattering. If he was hit by this slap, the consequences would be unimaginable!
He wasn't like Tang Wan who cultivated the Thick Earth Divine Dao and could transmit his strength to the earth!
"Whoosh!"
Gritting his teeth, Dongfang Taiqing suddenly sank downward with all his strength.
Luo Zheng's giant hand swept past Dongfang Taiqing's head. This slap brought with it fierce winds that whipped Dongfang Taiqing's head, causing his scalp to hurt.
After dodging Dongfang Taiqing's slap, the giant lightning wings on Luo Zheng's back suddenly flashed.
"Crackle!"
Luo Zheng stepped into the Snake Spirit World first. He turned his head and smiled at everyone, then his entire body began to shrink …
At the same time, he used greater teleportation to construct a spatial tunnel and disappeared from everyone's sight.
"Chase!"
"He's only a Great Perfection Real God. He won't be able to get far with greater teleportation!"
"We still have a chance to catch him!"
The Great Perfection True Gods reacted and quickly stepped into the Snake Spirit World, each using greater teleportation.
At the same time Luo Zheng used greater teleportation, the "Gen" token was already in his hand. The instant he emerged from the spatial passageway, he activated the "Gen" token, and the "Gen" token once again constructed a spatial passageway that led to the immortal abode.

Chapter 2276
The Perfected True Gods of the Serpent Spirit Region kept teleporting and spreading their divine senses in an attempt to find Luo Zheng.
But where could he be?
The Perfected True Gods busied themselves for a while before gathering.
Dongfang Taiqing's face was ashen. He didn't expect Luo Zheng to force him away at the last moment. He didn't even have a chance to attack.
Dongfang Taiqing's eyes were filled with suspicion. This was very strange. It was as if Luo Zheng knew he was going to use the Floating Earth Bell.
Or could it be that someone among the Great Perfection True Gods had told Luo Zheng about this?
Thinking of this, Dongfang Taiqing's gaze quickly fell on Sky Sword.
Dongfang Taiqing had always been suspicious of Sky Sword's origins and had even questioned Dongfang Yingqing.
But this woman was always on her husband's side and kept her mouth shut. She refused to reveal anything!
In terms of possibilities, Sky Sword was probably the most likely one …
…
…
Inside the immortal residence …
Afo, Yu Taibai, Jing Wuhuan, and the others gathered in the plaza.
"Are you ready?" Yu Shenfeng stared at the center of the plaza and asked.
At the center of the plaza, Xun held a spear. Her slender body was straight, and her charming face was slightly nervous.
She nodded slightly.
Jing Wu Huan stretched out his hand and gently pinched. The enchantment that enveloped the immortal mansion suddenly disappeared. It was like a beautiful bubble that was instantly extinguished.
The instant the barrier disappeared, Xun truly stepped into the Deity Domain.
Although the immortal residence wasn't small, they could only move around in it all these years. It was indeed a bit depressing.
Fortunately, after constant cultivation, Xun finally achieved the Divine Dao and became a lower True God …
Attaining the divine dao wasn't that difficult, but escaping the punishment of the control slate was too difficult.
For this reason, Yu Taibai personally made a trip to Buzhou Spirit Mountain, borrowing a path through Buzhou Spirit Mountain before sneaking into the Great Yan Universe.
For Yu Taibai, this was risky. If he was intercepted by a Saint, it would be very difficult for him to escape unscathed. However, Luo Zheng had been missing for ten years. The women grew more and more worried about Luo Zheng. Yu Taibai couldn't refuse their pleas and had to make a trip himself!
After infiltrating the Great Yan Universe, Yu Taibai obtained the Golden Bloodline from Startail.
Yu Taibai wanted to invite Xingwei back to God's Domain. After all, Xingwei was originally a member of God's Domain. However, Xingwei refused. With his current state, even if he returned to the Divine Realm, he would be a cripple. He was more willing to oversee the Grand Yan Universe.
After he brought the Golden Bloodline back, Xun, Ning Yudie, and Su Lingyun all saw hope.
Previously, even if they were able to become "life forms of the same level" after they had attained the divine way, and would not be split into true essence, they had not entered the divine realm through proper means, and were still considered as "illegal immigrants". Naturally, they would be punished by the Governing Slate …
After obtaining the Golden Bloodline, the situation was naturally different. Therefore, the three women were cultivating diligently.
Originally, Smoky's strength was much higher than Ning Yudie's. However, Ning Yudie had been cultivating day and night in the Cultivation Pagoda. She had quickly caught up to Smoky and became a True God at about the same time!
Today, he removed the barrier so that the two women could officially step into the God Realm.
After the barrier was removed, the Governing Slate in the Sea of Time once again shone with light …
"Crackle!"
At the same time, a streak of azure lightning streaked across the sky above the Immortal Palace. Then, a giant black palm appeared in the sky.
This situation was exactly the same as when Luo Zheng had tried to sneak in.
When Smoky saw the giant black sphere above her head, her eyes narrowed slightly. Her long spear was already quietly raised.
"Don't move. It can't discover you," Yu Taibai reminded.
Smoky pursed her lips and nodded.
She was naturally warlike. When faced with something that she felt was a threat, she subconsciously wanted to counterattack.
However, the energy contained within this black sphere was too terrifying. She could not see how this energy was formed at all. With her own strength, it was even harder to resist …
The palm spun around in the air a few times. Because Smoky had changed her blood, she was still unable to determine Smoky's location.
"Weng!"
Not long after, the palm quickly condensed into a black sphere. With a "pop" sound, it exploded.
Instantly, it turned into countless black cotton balls. Like a giant net, it enveloped the plaza.
Previously, Luo Zheng had been entangled by this net. For a time, Luo Zheng was unable to communicate with his Inner World and use his True Essence.
Smoky watched as the net enveloped her. She could not avoid it either.
At that moment, Yu Taibai's figure flashed. After grabbing Smoky's shoulder, they moved to the side. The net naturally missed …
"Alright! I didn't expect that the omnipotent Governing Slate could be deceived like this. Firmament Saint is really smart, "Yu Shenfeng chuckled.
In the eyes of most True Gods in the Divine Realm, the most powerful thing was not a Saint like Dongfang Chunjun, but the Governing Slate.
As long as one could afford Shenwu Coins, the slate could control everything in the Divine Realm. It was much more powerful than a Saint.
Even if a Saint violated the Governing Slate's rules, they would still have to obediently accept the punishment.
Therefore, when Yu Shenfeng saw that Smoky could avoid the Governing Slate's punishment after changing her blood, he was very surprised …
"Master didn't think of this method. It should be Jiu Sheng," Yu Taibai replied.
Yu Shenfeng shrugged. He looked at the Cultivation Tower and said, "It's the next person's turn!"
Ning Yudie had just attained the Black Ice Divine Dao not long ago. That was why she had set today as the day of her "stowaway."
Then, Yu Taibai opened the door to the Cultivation Tower and let Ning Yudie out.
Luo Nian, Su Lingyun, and Mu Mingxue had already been arranged to stay on the second floor of the Cultivation Tower. Naturally, they would not be affected.
When Ning Yudie walked out of the Cultivation Tower, she had already activated the Governing Slate's rules.
Just like Smoky, after a bolt of lightning exploded in the sky, a black palm appeared. With Yu Taibai's help, she also successfully deceived the Governing Slate's punishment.
"Congratulations, you can walk freely in the Divine Realm now," Yu Taibai said with a smile.
Ning Yudie smiled and nodded lightly at Yu Taibai. "Thank you, Senior Brother."
Smoky's eyes flashed. She stared at Yu Taibai and asked, "Then can we leave the Immortal Mansion now?"
Hearing Smoky's words, Ning Yudie's face also revealed a nervous expression.
In these ten years, there had been no news of Luo Zheng again. Their hearts were naturally filled with worry.
Although they had parted ways with Luo Zheng before, the situation this time was different. Luo Zheng had suddenly disappeared when he was meeting with his mother.
And Yu Taibai had investigated through some channels. The information was very unfavorable for Luo Zheng …
"Tsk tsk, there's no need to be so anxious!" Yu Shenfeng suddenly chuckled.
Yu Taibai also said, "The Divine Realm is too big. Even if you go out now, you won't be able to find him. Moreover, you'll encounter many dangers outside."
"But we can't just stay here and do nothing," Ning Yudie said with a frown.

Chapter 2277
Xun and Ning Yudie glanced at each other silently, a hint of disappointment flashing across their faces.
The two girls weren't unreasonable people. Although they hadn't left the divine domain these past few years, they had a rough understanding of the divine domain.
With their strength that had just stepped into the lower true god realm, it was already difficult for them to move around in a large region, much less find Luo Zheng in the divine domain.
At this moment, an indifferent voice is heard: "I don't care how long you plan to wait! In any case, I'm leaving today, and I'm leaving now! "
Xi Youqin, dressed in blue, walks out of the plaza from another door.
Aunt Hanjiu follows behind Xi Youqin, her gentle face somewhat helpless.
In a few years, Xi Youqin had also smoothly stepped into the middle true god realm.
Although Aunt Hanjiu had meticulously adjusted her, this level of talent still made Aunt Hanjiu very surprised.
Aunt Hanjiu established the Purple Soul Palace and recruited so many girls with the Extreme Purple Yin Body, but she had never met such a talented disciple.
Of course, Luo Zheng had to be excluded from these people. He was an anomaly.
In Aunt Hanjiu's eyes, Xi Youqin was a piece of unpolished jade that Luo Xiao had carefully prepared for her. These few years, she had been meticulously sculpted in the immortal estate, and it was rather interesting.
She even believed that it wouldn't be long before Xi Youqin could step into the realm of perfection, and even become a quasi-sage was possible.
The only problem was that Xi Youqin was too willful, so much so that Aunt Hanjiu spent most of her time comforting her …
"Shua!"
Yu Shenfeng takes a step forward, already standing in front of Xi Youqin.
His figure is tall, and he crosses his arms, a trace of a mischievous smile on his lips. "No one's stopping you. This immortal estate's door is open, you can leave whenever you want!"
Xi Youqin looks up at Yu Shenfeng, her eyes filled with anger.
She didn't stay in the immortal estate for long before she started to regret it. She kept clamoring to leave, to find Luo Zheng …
Once, Yu Shenfeng really let her leave the immortal estate.
But in this huge Takeshi Xuan Forest, she simply could not walk out.
Those huge trees, bushes, streams, and rivers all seemed to have feet. They could change their positions at any time. From time to time, a ferocious giant ape would pass by. Xi Youqin was scared half to death. She didn't expect that after running all the way, she would unknowingly reach the entrance of the immortal mansion.
Yu Shen Feng turned it into a huge maze. He could change the path at will. How could Xi Youqin leave?
"Hua!"
Purple Taoist Connotation spread out from Xi Youqin's body.
Whoosh! Whoosh! Whoosh!
The four swords had already surrounded the Royal God Blade.
Xi Youqin was enraged, and the God Slaughtering Sword Formation was unleashed mercilessly. The Buddha Emperor's Sword, which was in the lead, smashed towards Yu Shen Feng.
If it were other middle true gods, once they were surrounded by the God Slaying Sword Formation, it would be difficult for them to escape. Even upper true gods would be in danger.
However, Yu Shen Feng was still one of the best among the Great Perfection Paragons, not to mention that he was in control of the Takeshima Forest.
The two of them were simply not on the same level …
Facing the Buddha Emperor's Sword, he reached out and grabbed it lightly. The sword was unable to move.
Xi Youqin would not let it go. The Buddha Emperor's Sword instantly turned into a ball of purple gas and escaped from his hand. At the same time, the other three swords began to move together. Each move was fatal …
When the others saw this scene, they were already used to it. They did not have much of a reaction.
Aunt Hanjiu was quite happy. This was considered a free sparring partner for Xi Youqin, who did not like to cultivate. Moreover, it was a Paragon level sparring partner.
Just as they were fighting, at the front of the plaza, there was a spatial fluctuation at the entrance of the immortal mansion.
Xun, Xi Youqin, and Ning Yudie didn't feel it.
However, Aunt Hanjiu, Afo, and Jingwuhuan all looked towards the entrance …
Especially Yu Shen Feng. He had already become one with the entire forest. He could sense every movement in the entire Takeshi Xuan Forest very clearly.
For a spatial fluctuation to occur without any warning, it could only mean that someone had activated the "Gen" token to return!
"Crack!"
Yu Shen Feng was distracted for a moment, and the Buddha Emperor's Sword landed on his head.
The Buddha Emperor's Sword formed by purple gas was powerful, but Yu Shen Feng's body was too tough. It could not cause any substantial damage. Under this attack, Yu Shen Feng was not injured at all. Instead, the Buddha Emperor's Sword turned into a ball of purple gas …
Xi Youqin saw that everyone was looking at the entrance of the immortal mansion. She was slightly stunned and asked, "What's wrong?"
Then, she turned her head and saw Luo Zheng in a green robe appear at the entrance.
Ever since Xi Youqin entered the immortal mansion, she heard that the news was extremely unfavorable to Luo Zheng. She had always been worried about him.
Fortunately, she had an almost paranoid confidence in Luo Zheng!
As long as she did not see Luo Zheng's corpse, she would not believe that he had died!
"Husband!"
Xi Youqin was the first to rush to the entrance. As she moved, a string of purple gas accompanied her.
"Bang!"
Xi Youqin fiercely crashed into Luo Zheng's embrace. Her eyeballs fell out of their sockets. It was as if she had been wronged in the immortal mansion.
When the others saw Luo Zheng return, they all had different expressions.
"Oh? In a short ten years, his cultivation is already the same as mine. Hehe … "Yu Shen Feng pursed his lips. There was a hint of dissatisfaction on his face.
Among Gu Bei's three disciples, Yu Shen Feng had always considered himself to be the most talented. Thus, the first time he met this junior brother, he wanted to teach him a lesson. He was not as good-natured as Jing Wuhuan.
In fact, Yu Shen Feng knew that he was also jealous of Luo Zheng's monstrous talent. He was afraid that this kid would surpass him one day.
Now, it seemed that this worry was unnecessary.
Becoming a Paragon True God in ten years was not just surpassing him.
In so many Divine Epochs, no one had been able to do so …
When Yu Taibai, Jing Wuhuan, and Aunt Han Jiu noticed Luo Zheng's cultivation, their eyes were filled with surprise.
According to what they thought, it was definitely not easy for Luo Zheng to become a Paragon True God. It would take him a few hundred years at the fastest, or a thousand years at the slowest. However, this amount of time was already an extremely short period of time in the divine realm.
In these ten years, where had Luo Zheng gone?
If the news from the floating island was true, Luo Zheng had no chance of surviving.
Although Yu Taibai did not say anything, he suspected that Luo Zheng might have died in the Han Family's ancestral land.
Thus, he had already secretly looked for one of Luo Zheng's replacements, the other Dao Child, Xuanyuan Chenfeng.
Although they were full of questions, Xi Youqin threw herself at Luo Zheng as if there was no one else around. They did not want to join in the fun.
Luo Zheng could feel the warm and fragrant jade in his embrace gently trembling. He could feel that Xi Youqin was truly excited …
Compared to Ning Yudie's reserve and Xun's sincerity, Xi Youqin was simply fanatical.

Chapter 2278
Ever since Luo Zheng disappeared, this kind of haze of worry had always surrounded the hearts of these girls.
Seeing Luo Zheng return unscathed, this haze naturally disappeared completely.
"I finally arrived at the Realm of the Gods, but you actually didn't wait for me in Immortal Mansion." Xi Youqin buried herself in Luo Zheng's arms and scolded.
Luo Zheng smiled faintly. "Aren't I back now?"
"Mm." Xi Youqin nodded obediently like a sheep. She suddenly raised her head and looked vigilantly at Ning Yudie and Xun, who were not far away. "I don't care. They came to the Realm of the Gods before me, so they got preferential treatment! You have to accompany me these days! "
A wry smile appeared on Luo Zheng's face. Perhaps only Xi Youqin could say such willful words.
Although Xi Youqin's voice was soft, Ning Yudie and Xun heard it clearly.
Ning Yudie frowned and was about to flare up when Xun suddenly grabbed her arm and shook her head slightly.
Xun's request was very simple. As long as Luo Zheng could return safely, she didn't need to be jealous of Xi Youqin over such trivial matters …
Yu Taibai and the others also very patiently allowed Xi Youqin to "hog" Luo Zheng, even though they also had a bellyful of things to discuss with Luo Zheng.
Thus, an incense stick's worth of time passed.
Two incense sticks' worth of time passed …
Unwittingly, an hour passed.
The entire Immortal Mansion unwittingly floated into an awkward atmosphere. Ning Yudie was obstructed by Xun and could only bitterly criticize Xi Youqin in her heart for not seeing the big picture.
The first to lose control was Yu Shenfeng.
His figure flashed with a "whoosh" and he arrived beside Luo Zheng. "Hey, hey, Xi Youqin, if you want to be intimate with my junior brother, now is not the time!"
"It's none of your business!"
Xi Youqin, who was like a little lamb just a moment ago, spoke in a cold voice. She glared at Yu Shenfeng as if she wanted to kill him.
"Little Junior Brother doesn't belong to you alone. He still has me, the Second Senior Brother, the Big Senior Brother, and the Ninth Aunt. Hehe, there's also his sister, and his son. It's not good for you to monopolize him like this, right?" Yu Shenfeng said.
Xi Youqin raised her head and said proudly, "Then I'm his wife! It's perfectly justified! "
Yu Shenfeng curled his lips and chuckled, "Then Junior Brother won't only have you as his wife!"
As he spoke, he looked mischievously at Kaoru and Ning Yudie, who were not far away.
Under this pressure, Xi Youqin felt a little embarrassed and reluctantly let go of Luo Zheng.
…
In the inner courtyard of Immortal Mansion.
After everyone sat down, A 'Fu had already served cup after cup of immortal tea, and a refreshing fragrance that refreshed the heart instantly arose within the inner court.
This immortal tea was made from wild mountain tea from the depths of Takiguan Forest. It was also made from the dew of the Dragon Saliva Tree. Both of these things were difficult to gather, but after Yu Shenfeng took control of Takiguan Forest, it was naturally easy for him to gather them.
"It's very difficult to find resources like this in Divine Dominion," Yu Taibai said as soon as he sat down.
Everyone was thinking the same thing. Divine Dominion had the most resources in Divine Dominion, and there was no way Luo Zheng would stay on a floating island. There was no way Luo Zheng could have holed himself up in some remote mountain or hidden in some second or third-rate clan for the past ten years.
After disappearing for ten years, he immediately returned to Divine Dominion. This was completely beyond their imagination.
"Divine Dominion doesn't lack resources like this, right?" Luo Zheng smiled. "Hua Tianming also reached Divine Dominion! He didn't use more time than me! "
"Hua Tianming?" Yu Taibai muttered to himself for a moment, then said, "His method of reaching Divine Dominion is completely different from yours. That doesn't count …"
Yu Taibai was very clear about the roles that Dao children should play. He naturally knew what abilities Hua Tianming possessed.
"What's the difference?" Luo Zheng revealed a suspicious expression.
"He can devour the Discipline Tablet for his own use. Naturally, his cultivation will steadily increase. There won't even be any bottlenecks. It's only natural for him to reach Divine Dominion early," Yu Taibai said seriously.
"Devour the Discipline Tablet …" Luo Zheng was shocked.
"Let's not talk about this. Where did you go after the Han family's ancestral land?" Yu Taibai asked. This was the question that everyone was most concerned about.
Luo Zheng thought for a moment, then said, "I left Divine Dominion."
"Left Divine Dominion? Didn't you escape into the primal chaos? "Yu Shenfeng's voice suddenly rose an octave, and he stood up from his chair.
Even if Yu Shenfeng controlled Takanxuan Forest and Yu Taibai controlled Buzhou Spirit Mountain, they didn't have the right to leave Divine Dominion. The primal chaos would slowly corrode their bodies until there was nothing left of them. This kind of corrosion was irresistible.
Only Nirvana Realm experts or Saints had the right to travel through the primal chaos.
"Sit down, junior brother." Jing Wuhuan glared fiercely, and Yu Shenfeng obediently sat back down.
Yu Taibai wasn't too surprised. He knew that Luo Zheng's body was special and didn't fear the primal chaos' corrosion. He continued to ask, "Did Master's wife send you out?"
"Yes." Luo Zheng nodded. "After that, I fell all the way to the bottom of the primal chaos."
"It's said that outside Divine Dominion is clouds of primal chaos. Outside of that, there's still primal chaos. It's extremely desolate. Did you eat that primal chaos to reach Divine Dominion?" Yu Shenfeng asked.
Although Yu Shenfeng's words were very idiotic, they weren't wrong …
Most true gods didn't even think about what was outside Divine Dominion. The Divine Dominion paragons here had some understanding of the primal chaos outside Divine Dominion. After all, there were records about the primal chaos in Divine Dominion's library. Those were all left behind by Gu Bei.
Who knew that Luo Zheng would shake his head. "The bottom of the primal chaos isn't desolate. There are living creatures there, and many experts!"
"There are people at the bottom of the primal chaos? They're all creatures above their level? "Even the usually calm Yu Taibai was shocked.
Jing Wu Huan, who had been silent for a long time, suddenly raised his eyebrows. "There's a world there?"
"Yes." Luo Zheng blinked. "That's a prison! There are many exiles locked up in this prison. These exiles are all from the mother world … "
"What kind of place is the mother world?" Yu Shenfeng asked curiously.
Jing Wuhuan and Yu Taibai looked at each other. Then, Yu Taibai said, "I heard that the mother world is the center of the entire primal chaos. These exiles locked up in this prison must have extremely powerful cultivations?"
"They're all Nirvana Realm experts." Luo Zheng nodded.
Hearing this, the Divine Dominion paragons and Aunt Hanjiu were all shocked.
At the same time, they felt a sense of danger.

Chapter 2279
Yu Taibai stared at Luo Zheng and asked, "Were these Nirvana Realm experts imprisoned in this prison?"
Luo Zheng nodded and said, "But later, we broke the prison's shackles and allowed them to leave."
"Leave?" Even Jing Wuhuan couldn't remain calm. "They wouldn't have entered the divine realm, right?"
Yu Taibai thought for a moment and then asked, "The key is where these Nirvana Realm experts stand."
Luo Zheng smiled faintly. "I was able to return to the Divine Dominion this time because those Nirvana Realm experts sent me back. These Nirvana Realm experts were born into different factions. Among them, the two strongest factions were the Nuwa Clan and the Chiyou Clan."
Jing Wuhuan, Yu Shenfeng, and Aunt Hanjiu didn't understand the meaning behind Luo Zheng's words.
They knew too little about their Mother World. Only Jing Wuhuan vaguely knew about its existence.
Thus, the names Nuwa and Chiyou were very unfamiliar to them.
Yu Taibai was the only one who rose to his feet and stared at Luo Zheng in disbelief. "The Nuwa Clan! Chiyou Clan? Chiyou Clan … Do they know about your bloodline? "
"I tested my bloodline in order to join the Chiyou Clan in the underground world." Luo Zheng nodded.
"Then what's their attitude?"
This man, who had always been as calm as a mountain, was now looking at Luo Zheng with excitement.
Yu Shenfeng's jaw dropped when he saw Yu Taibai's reaction. Even when he fused with the Takiguan Forest, Yu Taibai was still calm. He had never seen Yu Taibai so excited.
"I'll do my best to help," Luo Zheng said.
Yu Taibai suddenly let out a sigh of relief, a hint of uncontrollable joy appearing on his face. "Then where are they now? How many are there? "
"Including the Nuwa Clan, there should be 13 of them. They originally planned to return to the divine realm with me, but now there's a problem. They've been blocked by the divine realm and can't come in," Luo Zheng replied.
"Can't come in?" Yu Taibai was taken aback. He didn't expect this result.
Luo Zheng nodded. "When Chi You first established this God's Domain, in order to protect it, he naturally put in a lot of effort. If one wanted to forcefully shatter this God's Domain, even a peak figure of the Nirvana Realm would not be able to do so.
Aunt Han Jiu and Jing Wuhuan more or less understood what was going on now. Luo Zheng had wandered around the bottom of the Divine Region and actually brought back quite a number of Nirvana Realm experts. However, they were now blocked outside the Divine Region.
"But you and the other Saints can all return after leaving. How do you explain that?" asked Jing Wuhuan.

"It's simple. The Xuanyuan Guard was also blocked by the divine realm and spent a lot of time to enter," Luo Zheng said. "These Nirvana Realm experts aren't completely unable to come in. They need to spend a few years to break through the space channel!"
"A few years …" Yu Taibai lowered his head and fell into deep thought.
The Daoists snuck out from the Dayan Universe one by one. Shi Xiaoqiao returned and took control of the Soul Wilderness. Yu Shenfeng controlled the Takixuan Forest. Hua Tianming snuck into the Dongfang Family. Luo Zheng stepped into the Great Fulfilment …
These were all small details in Yu Taibai's eyes. Although they were carried out step by step, they wouldn't have a huge impact on the overall situation.
But Luo Zheng suddenly brought back a group of Nirvana Realm experts, which directly turned the situation around.
This was completely out of Yu Taibai's calculations …
But for their side, the situation suddenly became very favorable, so favorable that Yu Taibai felt a little unprepared.
"How many years do they need to enter the divine realm?" Yu Taibai stared at Luo Zheng and asked. He had acutely realized that this was a critical time.

…
…
"Boom!"
Inside the Dongfang Family's Divine Luan Palace.

The entire Divine Luan Palace was silent.
After these Great Fulfilment True Gods returned to the floating island, they were directly summoned to the Dongfang family.
On the platform of the Divine Luan Palace, Tang Lun, Leng Yao, Mu Haiji, and the other Saints also remained silent. Their family's Great Fulfilment True Gods were being reprimanded by Dongfang Chunjun. They also felt ashamed, but Dongfang Chunjun was right.
Below them, Dongfang Taiqing, Mu Xuerong, Tang Wan, Fang Henshao, and the recently famous "Sky Sword" were all peak Great Fulfilment experts.
Apart from them, the other Great Fulfilment True Gods were also quite strong …
Sending these people to search for that target was enough to show how important this matter was to Dongfang Chunjun. Unexpectedly, they still returned empty-handed.
As the leader of the Great Fulfilment True Gods, Dongfang Taiqing could no longer remain silent. He took a step forward, raised his head, and said, "Luo Zheng's strength has increased greatly. He is very strong now. We are indeed no match for him!"

Not to mention that Luo Zheng was very important, just this matter alone was enough to embarrass them …
The so-called rich and powerful families had become a complete joke!
"Great Sage Chunjun, forgive Mu Xuerong for speaking so boldly." Mu Xuerong cupped her hands and said, "If the space had not been sealed, we might have been able to fight. However, with the space sealed and unable to use greater teleportation, even a Quasi-Sage might not have a chance of winning!"

When the Sages heard this, their faces were filled with astonishment.

Chapter 2280
The best resources in the Realm of the Gods were all concentrated on the floating island.

Dongfang Taiqing could only give up.

The Perfect Real Gods present felt relieved and quickly left the Divine Luan Palace.

Hearing this, the Sages present all looked serious.
"People who can travel in the Chaos, one is a Sage, and the other is a Nirvana Realm expert," Leng Yao analyzed.
"Don't forget about that fellow Luo Zheng," Tang Lun added.
"Hmph, don't mention that fellow!" Mu Haiji was instantly unhappy.
The sword he had slashed into the Dayan Universe did not take Luo Zheng's life.
At the entrance of the Sea of Time, Dongfang Chunjun had personally attacked, but Luo Zheng still managed to escape …
Under the encirclement of dozens of Perfect Real Gods, he still managed to escape safely!
Now, whenever Mu Haiji heard the two words "Luo Zheng," he would have a headache.
"Let's not talk about how Luo Zheng survived in the Chaos for now. What we need to know now is who the people from outside the divine territory are and find out their stance," Dongfang Chunjun said calmly.
"Won't we know once we go and take a look?" Tang Lun said.
Qin Yuan, the Sage of the Qin family, laughed. "Who's going? Are you going? "
"Me? I'm not going! "Tang Lun quickly shook his head and said.
If the people from outside the divine territory were Sages, there was only one possibility. It was most likely that Gu Bei had returned …
However, according to Mu Haiji, the people from outside the divine territory had constructed an indestructible space tunnel in the Dayan Universe.
Regardless of whether it was Gu Bei or not, anyone who had such means would be courting death if they ran out rashly.
At that moment, a thin old man suddenly walked in from the main entrance of the Divine Luan Palace.
This old man looked amiable, and his body emitted the fragrance of medicinal herbs.
Some of the Sages sniffed lightly and knew that those herbs were definitely cheap ones …
However, when these Sages saw this old man, they did not look down on him at all. This was because they could not see through this thin old man's cultivation level!

Qin Yuan and the other Sages also cupped their hands and bowed.
The Sages of the Wealthy Alliance all knew of Elder Hu's existence, but not all of them had seen him before.
"You haven't realized the great danger we're in. Since they sent Luo Zheng back, they'll definitely find a way to enter the divine territory. However, it's only a matter of time before they forcefully open the space tunnel. Back then, we only spent twenty years.
When Elder Hu said this, the Sages' hearts immediately sank …
Even Elder Hu, who was in the Paramita Realm, was very wary of people from outside the divine territory. Sages like them were even less fortunate.
While the Sages were silent, Zhao Family's Sage Zhao Wuyi suddenly said, "Then let me, Zhao Wuyi, go!"
Zhao Family had just joined the Wealthy Alliance not long ago. With Dongfang Chunjun's support, their floating island was also thriving day by day. However, their foundation was still unstable. They needed to do something to prove themselves to Dongfang Chunjun.

Chapter 2281
Dongfang Chunjun was rather surprised that Zhao Wuyi volunteered himself.

Saints could indeed be reborn by relying on the universe, but there were always accidents.

Now that they were going to come into contact with outsiders, who knew what kind of power they had?
This was definitely a huge risk.

Protecting Zhao Wuyi's universe meant protecting Zhao Wuyi's life. Naturally, it would also protect his Zhao family's position as one of the wealthiest families on the floating island …

"This matter should not be delayed. Let's go now," Elder Hu said faintly.
Although he always looked calm in front of these Saints, he was actually very anxious.
As a Paramita powerhouse, Elder Hu had always thought that the Xuanyuan Guards were in control of the entire situation.
As long as the Origin Seal was undone, his brothers would be freed and it would only be a matter of time before they took over the divine realm.

"The space tunnel they built runs through my universe. I think they should be at the top of the universe. Brother Wuyi, you can borrow my universe. If you don't mind, I'll build a space tunnel first. Brother Wuyi, Brother Chunjun, and Elder Hu can follow me," Mu Haiji suggested.
A universe could only hold three True Gods at the same time, and this was after Dongfang Chunjun had modified the Governing Slate.
Saints were essentially Perfected True Gods, so Mu Haiji could not invite all the Saints in the Divine Luan Palace. Otherwise, he would trigger the rules of the Governing Slate and be punished.
Now, only Dongfang Chunjun, Zhao Wuyi, and Elder Hu were allowed to enter.
As for Mu Haiji, as the creator of the universe, he was naturally not restricted.
After Dongfang Chunjun and Elder Hu nodded, Mu Haiji extended his hand and used greater teleportation.
An invisible spatial fluctuation shot up into the sky, and in an instant, it was connected to the extreme universe …
Zhao Wuyi, Dongfang Chunjun, and Elder Hu followed the spatial ripples and used greater teleportation to follow Mu Haiji into the depths of the universe.
There was a light green star at the top of the universe.
For the 'pseudo-saints' of the Ultimate Universe, this tallest and brightest star had countless mysterious legends.
In the great extreme universe, there were many powerhouses who wanted to climb up this star.
However, once they reached a certain height, they would encounter an invisible 'spatial wall'. No matter how hard they tried, they were unable to cross this spatial wall.
Therefore, to the creatures of this world, this star was like a flower in the mirror, the moon in the water. They could see it, but they could never touch it.
What they didn't know was that this star was just a palace that Mu Haiji had built in his inner world.
"Buzz buzz buzz …"
Accompanied by a wave of spatial fluctuations.
Mu Haiji appeared in the palace at the top of the planet.
He tapped lightly with his finger …
In the palace, colorful flowers bloomed in an instant, and a faint floral fragrance filled the entire palace.
At this time, Dongfang Chunjun, Elder Hu, and Zhao Wuyi followed closely behind and appeared in the palace.
"That spatial tunnel is constructed from that direction." Mu Haiji pointed out the direction.
Zhao Wuyi glanced over, and nodded silently.
For a Saint, a deviation of several thousand kilometers was acceptable. With greater teleportation, this distance was only a matter of thought.
Mu Haiji, Elder Hu, and Dongfang Chunjun each separated a wisp of their soul and attached it to Zhao Wuyi's body. Afterward, Zhao Wuyi used greater teleportation again and took a step forward, entering the spatial tunnel.
This spatial tunnel was directly connected to the primal chaos outside the divine realm …
…
…
Traveling through the primal chaos was practically a compulsory course for Saints.
When Paragons and Quasi Saints became Saints in the Saint Mountain, they would develop a boundless curiosity about the world outside the divine realm. This was because they were already peak existences in the divine realm. If they wanted to climb to a higher level, they could only look outside the divine realm …
However, most Saints were very disappointed after traveling through the primal chaos.
Apart from large swaths of primal chaos, there was almost nothing there. Most Saints would obediently return to the divine realm after circling it a few times and start painstakingly managing their own clans so that they could rise to become a powerful clan on a floating island.
This was only the second time Zhao Wuyi had left the divine realm.
After standing at the top of the divine realm, his field of vision had become extremely wide.
The top of the God Domain was so vast, and as far as his eyes could see, it was completely empty. How could there be anyone from outside the God Domain?
Zhao Wuyi's eyes flashed slightly as he stood in place. His consciousness spread out in all directions with him as the center!
A thousand kilometers, ten thousand kilometers, fifty thousand kilometers …
Somewhere else at the top of the divine realm.
Ming Wei stood in the air, and below her, there was a seven-colored ring that was slowly rotating.
At the center of the seven-colored ring, the high-speed spatial ripples formed a huge spatial spiral that constantly pierced through the restrictions in space!
She'd been in this position for a long time.
Elder Jin, Fu Er, Chi Yi, and the ladies from the Nuwa Clan were all sitting cross-legged with their eyes closed.
Piercing through the restrictions in the spatial tunnel required a lot of time and energy. They were also prepared for a protracted battle.
"Clan Master, you've already persisted for more than half a month. We can take over from you," said a Nirvana Realm powerhouse from the Nuwa Clan.
Ming Wei smiled faintly. "Let's not talk about half a month. With this amount of energy, even a year and a half won't be a problem."
"But we also …" The Nirvana Realm powerhouse was halfway through his sentence when a strange expression appeared on his face.
At the same time, Elder Jin, Fu Er, Chi Yi, and the rest opened their eyes.
They sensed a weak strand of Divine Sense spreading out!
"Who's spying on us?" Chi Yi asked with a frown.
"It's just a mere Paragons … No, it's more powerful than an ordinary Paragons," said Elder Jin.
"Hmph, no matter who it is, we'll know once we catch them and interrogate them. Yuyan, you take over from me! "Ming Wei sneered and disappeared from the spot.

Chapter 2282
With Ming Wei's ability, it was a piece of cake for her to follow the divine sense to find the target.

The aura she unintentionally emitted still made Zhao Wuyi feel great pressure.
This pressure was many times stronger than "Elder Hu"!
If it was an ordinary True God, they would only think that this woman was powerful. Because Zhao Wuyi was a Saint, he could acutely sense how terrifying this woman was!

He kept warning himself in his heart that no matter what, he should not provoke this woman in front of him.
Ming Wei glanced at him with an indifferent expression. She stretched out her hand and pointed a finger. A seven-colored ring enveloped Zhao Wu Yi.

In the green star at the top of Da Ji Universe …

Such an evaluation was far beyond Dongfang Chunjun and Mu Haiji's expectations. The two Saints looked at each other and saw the shock in each other's eyes.
While they were discussing, Zhao Wuyi had already been dragged out of the spatial tunnel by Ming Wei.
Through the sub-soul attached to Zhao Wuyi, Dongfang Chunjun and the other two sucked in a cold breath again.
They actually saw more than ten Nirvana Realm experts!
These Nirvana Realm experts all had extraordinary auras and were not much weaker than the woman from before. The shock on the faces of Elder Hu, Mu Haiji, and Dongfang Chunjun became more and more intense.
Elder Jin sat on the ground and looked at Zhao Wu Yi. "Speak, why did you use your divine sense to spy on us?"

In the eyes of Ming Wei and the others, a Saint was indeed much more powerful than a Perfected True God with their universe. However, this was still a True God and did not change much.
It was a pity that Zhao Wuyi, a Saint, could not even resist …
As the rainbow light on Ming Wei's fingertip flashed slightly, she gently pulled it back, and three palm-sized rainbow rings floated in the air. Within these rainbow rings were three strands of soul shackles.
She had actually forcefully pulled out Elder Hu, Dongfang Chunjun, and Mu Haiji's sub-souls.

The three sub-souls started to change shape …

"Forgive me for not coming out to greet you? You obviously didn't dare to come out yourself and sent a scapegoat, "Ming Wei said coldly.
Existences like Ming Wei and Elder Jin could see through these tricks with a single glance.

Elder Hu still had a glimmer of hope in his heart. He hoped that these people were not standing against him!
"Why have I come?" Ming Wei smiled faintly. "Of course it's to help Luo Zheng and eliminate all his enemies! I'm sure you're on his list, right? Otherwise, you wouldn't have appeared in front of us so sneakily and asked about our stance. "
Ming Wei said it casually, but Elder Hu, Dongfang Chunjun, and Mu Haiji's hearts suddenly sank.
This was probably what they did not want to hear the most.
In the Realm of the Gods …
Mu Haiji smiled bitterly. "What did Luo Zheng do to attract so many Nirvana Realm powerhouses to help him?"
Dongfang Chunjun also took a deep breath!
Mu Xuerong originally had a chance to kill Luo Zheng in the Sea of Time forbidden zone, but he had personally ordered Dongfang Taiqing to stop her.
He never expected that a single order from him would cause such a huge hidden danger. It was unbelievable!
Elder Hu thought for a moment, then continued to control his sub-soul. He said to Ming Wei, "What Luo Zheng can give you, we can give you. We'll even offer the entire Realm of the Gods. What do you think?"
The Realm of the Gods was the result of Chiyou's efforts. Its value was extraordinary.
The exiles at the bottom of the Chaos Abyss had many ideas about the Realm of the Gods, but because of Ming Wei, they could only give up on this idea.
"Offer the Realm of the Gods with both hands?" This time, it was Ming Wei who was slightly taken aback. She then looked at Elder Jin with a meaningful smile.
Elder Jin, Fu Er, Chi Yi, and the other Chiyou Nirvana Realm powerhouses laughed loudly. Their laughter made Zhao Wuyi's hair stand on end. It made Elder Hu, Dongfang Chunjun, and Mu Haiji feel very guilty.

Chapter 2283
The Nirvana Realm was a realm that referred to living beings who had stepped foot on the Nirvana Realm.
The difference in strength between the Nirvana Realm and the Nirvana Realm was like heaven and earth.
Elder Hu could become a Xuanyuan Guard because he was considered a top figure in the Mother World.
However, in the eyes of Elder Jin and Ming Wei, he was still not worth mentioning.
Elder Hu was puzzled by Elder Jin's words. He stared at Elder Jin and asked, "Why do you say that?"
Ming Wei smiled lightly and answered on behalf of Elder Jin, "This Deity Realm was originally created by Chi You, and Elder Jin is a member of the Li Clan, a Five Star Chi You Guard. Yet, you intend to use the Deity Realm as a trade. Isn't that too naive?
Elder Hu, Dongfang Chunjun, and Mu Haiji's expressions turned ugly when they heard Ming Wei's words.
They didn't know where Luo Zheng had invited these powerful beings from …
In a situation where they couldn't fight back, they planned to sacrifice the benefits of the Realm of the Gods to bribe these people.
This was their last resort. How could they have known that the elder in front of them was a member of the Li Clan and a Chi You Guard?
Elder Jin's expression was solemn as he said in a deep voice, "This Realm of the Gods is the Realm of Inheritance of the Li Clan. If you know what's good for you, quickly retreat from the Realm of the Gods. I can spare your lives!"
Now that Elder Jin and Ming Wei had made their stance clear, there was no room for negotiation.
Mu Haiji, Elder Hu, and Dongfang Chunjun's three sub-souls fell silent …
As for Zhao Wuyi, he was also extremely depressed. It seemed that this group of people didn't come with good intentions. He was most likely going to die today.
"What if we don't leave?" Elder Hu asked calmly.
Jin Lao's eyes suddenly flashed with a bright light. His burly figure suddenly shook as he made a grasping motion towards Zhao Wuyi.
An irresistible force suddenly enveloped Zhao Wuyi. This force contained a vast might that came from the Nirvana Realm.
Zhao Wuyi was originally bound by Ming Wei and couldn't resist Elder Jin's methods.
Crack crack crack crack …
Zhao Wuyi only felt that irresistible force instantly fill every part of his body and then explode at the same time.
All the bones in his body began to break …
"Pa!"
With a loud explosion, Zhao Wu Yi's body was reduced to fine powder.
"If you don't want to leave, you'll end up like this," Elder Jin said with a cold expression.
A dignified Saint was killed by Elder Jin in a single move.
However, when Zhao Wuyi's body was crushed, a unique mark of light emerged from his dantian. This mark of light flew away along the flat space at the top of the Realm of the Gods.
"Eh?"
When Fu Er saw the light spot escape, a strange expression appeared on his face and he quickly moved behind the light spot. At the same time, he quickly formed several hand seals in an attempt to capture the light spot.
"Buzz buzz buzz …"
The handprints turned into circles of pale green runes. These runes constantly shrouded the light spots, but they were unable to obstruct them in the slightest.
Fu Er could only watch as the dot of light escaped and burrowed into the Celestial Realm …
If an ordinary True God's physical body was destroyed, their inner world would collapse and their soul would be destroyed. That would be a complete and utter death.
However, after a Saint constructed the universe, the entire universe was actually one with the Realm of the Gods. As long as the universe didn't shatter, the Saint wouldn't truly die.
There were only two ways to kill a Saint. One was to destroy the entire universe while killing the Saint. The other was to sever the connection between the Saint and the universe while killing the Saint.
Fu Er wanted to leave the light spot behind to sever the connection between Zhao Wuyi and the universe, but he failed.
His abilities weren't enough to go against Chi You's plan …
The only person in the Realm of the Gods who had this ability was Li Luoshui.

"Won't it be too dangerous to go out now?"
Ning Yudie had been sitting quietly beside Luo Zheng. Suddenly hearing that Luo Zheng wanted to leave again, she immediately felt uneasy.
She didn't know much about the God Realm. She only knew that her husband had made many enemies in the God Realm. The last time he left, there had been no news of him for ten years. She was afraid that one day Luo Zheng would really leave and never return.
Aunt Hanjiu also said with some worry, "Right now, those rich and powerful families can't enter the Soul Wilderness, can't enter Buzhou Spirit Mountain, and can't come to Takiguan Forest. Most of them will enter the Abyss Demon Realm …"
"I think Luo Zheng can make a trip," Yu Taibai said with a faint smile, "With Luo Zheng's current strength, protecting himself shouldn't be a problem. Moreover, although the Abyss Demon Realm is connected to the Evil Ghost Realm, its entrance is located in the Red Lotus Realm. The distance is very far."
Normally, a forbidden area only had one entrance.
It was impossible to enter the Abyss Demon Realm from the Evil Ghost Realm. The teleportation array on the Noble Gate Floating Island was also connected to the entrance of the Red Lotus Realm.
Moreover, Luo Zheng had the "Gen" token in his hands. Even if the other party found him, he could use the "Gen" token to return to the Immortal Mansion.
Hearing that Yu Taibai approved of Luo Zheng going to the Evil Ghost Realm, Ning Yudie could only lower her head and twist the corner of her clothes. Her eyes were filled with loneliness.
The current her was no longer the awe-inspiring Cloud Hall Hall Master. She had fallen far behind Luo Zheng. She couldn't even look at Luo Zheng's back.

Chapter 2288
North of the Divine Region......
After going around the Blue Origin Mountain and crossing the northern border was the Beichen Region.
The Beichen Region had once been an extremely fertile region. Many experts had come from it, and it could be considered a place of outstanding talents.
However, in the last few Divine Epochs, the Beichen Region had become a land of death.
The deathly aura emanating from the Creation Tree had killed all living things in the Beichen Region. The once prosperous Divine Region had fallen into disrepair and was slowly collapsing.
Elder Hu's figure silently descended from the deathly aura.
The deathly aura was like a pack of hyenas that had discovered food. It gathered toward him from all directions.
"Hmph!"
Elder Hu's body trembled slightly. A formless power spread out from his body, and the deathly aura that had gathered around him dissipated......
He then gazed into the distance. In the distance, the 330,000 kilometer tall Creation Tree was faintly discernible.
After locking onto the Creation Tree's position, Elder Hu once again used greater teleportation to teleport toward it.
Not long after......
Elder Hu appeared on the thickest branch in the center of the Creation Tree.
On this branch, there were countless strands of dark red spider silk. These strands of spider silk were entwined around four huge crimson spheres.
Within the spheres, one could vaguely make out the shape of a person.
"Hu Yan, did something big happen in the Divine Region recently?" A cold voice came from one of the spheres.
Hu Yan cupped his hands toward the four spheres and replied, "There's indeed trouble this time."
"Trouble? That Yuan guy has been cursed. He can't come out of the Creation Tree. That woman called Luo Shui is pretty much done as well. All she has left is her head. What trouble in the Divine Region can't you deal with? "An imposing voice floated out from another sphere.
"That's right. You're the only Nirvana Realm cultivator left in such a huge Divine Territory. The entire Divine Territory is managed by you alone. Even so, you still caused trouble for us. What a piece of trash!" Mocking words floated out from another sphere.
These scarlet spheres were the Origin Seal that Elder Yuan had used all those years ago.
The entire Origin Seal relied on the Creation Tree. It used the tree's life force to constantly maintain the seal.
That was why the Chaos Origin Seal was so difficult to break, and it had sealed these Xuan Yuan Guards until now.
The three people who spoke were called Pu Zhe, Hou Bo, and Cha Yao. These three were all two star Xuan Yuan Guards. In terms of strength, they were a level higher than Huyan.
Back then, because Hu-yan was slightly weaker, the Chaos Origin Seal he received was also slightly weaker. That was why he had a chance to break the seal.
As for these three Xuan Yuan Guards, they had been bound for so many divine epochs, so they were naturally in an extremely miserable state.

Huyan ignored the three of them and turned to the largest red sphere and said, "Big brother."

If other dangers occurred in the Divine Territory, it wouldn't be a big problem.
In the face of absolute strength, they could suppress any danger. However, if an expert from the outside world interfered, it would be troublesome.

The three Xuan Yuan Guards instantly fell silent. The surroundings were as silent as ghosts.

Huyan furrowed his brows, looking distressed. "According to Big Brother's plan, the simplest way was to let the Divine Realm collapse. The Chaos Origin Seal would have broken by itself, and it would have been done once and for all. But in the end, Luo Shui's son stopped it. That kid disappeared for ten years, and for some reason, he brought back a group of Nirvana Realm experts from the outer realm!"

"Indeed, it's too coincidental." Huyan shook his head. "And these people are trying to open a space tunnel."
Pang Miao laughed bitterly. "We did this before. It looks like they'll be able to enter the Divine Territory in a few years."
"Then what do we do now?" Hou Bo couldn't help but ask.
As Paramita Realm experts, the four of them were not weak. However, a five star Chi You Guard was not to be underestimated even in the Divine Territory.
The Divine Territory belonged to Chi You. When they came in, the fate of the Xuan Yuan Guards was obvious.
"I've already told them to start preparing the second plan," Huyan replied flatly.
The first plan was to let the Divine Territory collapse. The Origin Seal would not be able to rely on the Jianmu Wood to continuously inject energy into it and would naturally collapse.
The second plan was to find a way to control the Abyss Demonic Territory and use its power to forcefully tear open the Origin Seal.
This plan was more difficult. After all, a forbidden land like the Abyss Demonic Territory was not so easy to control.
"Control the Abyss Demonic Territory... Are the Saints confident?" asked Pang Miao.
Elder Hu thought for a moment and replied, "Now that Dongfang Chunjun is almost in control of the Sea of Time, so many Saints have come out. The possibility is very high this time."
Pu Zhe sighed faintly. "Even if the seal is really broken, I'm afraid it's useless. We're still no match for a five star Chi You Guard..."
When he said this, the five of them, including Pang Miao, fell silent.
Indeed, even if the Chaotic Origin Seal was undone now, these five Xuanyuan Guards seemed to be unable to do anything besides flee.
Even the strongest Pang Miao was only a three star Xuan Yuan Guard. The difference in power was too great.
After a long while, Pang Miao said flatly, "There's still a chance."

"Use my Xuanyuan Gouyu to ask for help from the Primal Chaos and summon the nearest Xuan Yuan Guard," said Pang Miao.
Elder Hu's expression turned strange when he heard Pang Miao's suggestion.
The Xuanyuan Gouyu was a token that all Xuan Yuan Guards needed. Through it, the Xuan Yuan Guards could summon each other.
However, that was usually in the mother world!
Using the Xuanyuan Gouyu to summon other Xuan Yuan Guards in the boundless Primal Chaos?

Chapter 2289

Starting from upper True God, wanting to take another step forward in cultivation was as difficult as crossing a heavenly chasm.
Becoming a Secondary Saint or a Saint was an opportunity that could only be encountered by chance.

"Great Sage Chunjun, if you have something important to discuss, why not invite me, Emperor Hanqing? Haha … "Emperor Hanqing did not dare to step onto the Dongfang family's floating island. He could only float outside the island and send his voice in.

Even among the rich and powerful, they were the most talented.
However, it was not easy to become a Great Perfection deity from a True God.

Emperor Hanqing said, "My two daughters and one son have done well. As a father, I am naturally very happy. Now that the Saints are going to conquer the Abyss Demon Region, I, Emperor Hanqing, am shameless enough to ask for this chance. I wonder if Great Saint Chunjun is willing to give me this chance?"
Before Dongfang Chunjun could say anything, Leng Yao shook his head lazily. "You, Han Jia? Not enough for us to trust you! "

Chapter 2290
This time, Dongfang Chunjun was determined to take down the Abyss Demon Zone. Gathering so many Saints and Perfect Sovereigns was already a very cautious move.

Chapter 2291
All these years, Dongfang Yingqing had listened to Hua Tianming's every word.

In the outermost area of Takeshi Xuan Forest …
A black python that was hundreds of meters long moved quickly.

Luo Zheng stood where he was and didn't intend to make a move.
Seeing the black python getting closer and closer to Luo Zheng, a thunderous sound suddenly came from not far away.
"Crackle!"
A two-meter-tall puppet rushed over.
The puppet's hands were inlaid with two extraordinary long swords. Arcs of electricity were constantly flowing from the puppet's arms to the swords.
"Pu pu!"

The puppet rolled with the help of the two swords, and the swords cut a circle around the black python's body.
Boom!
Just like that, the snake's body, which was more than ten feet wide, was severed.
The snake's head and broken body rolled crazily, creating deep trenches in the ground.
"Crack, crack!"
The puppet sheathed the two long swords on its back and retracted its arms. Its height rapidly decreased and soon turned into a ferocious-looking old man.

Old Man Extreme Evil was just an ambitious and ruthless person in the Vast Sky Region. A puppet forged by Imperial God Blade was not to be underestimated even among the rich and powerful. Old Man Extreme Evil even felt that it would be difficult for him to dig out all the potential of this puppet with his own abilities.

Old Man Extreme Evil thought for a while and shook his head firmly.

These things were definitely not something that Old Man Extreme Evil had the right to touch. Even if Luo Zheng was kind enough to share some of the harvest with him, Old Man Extreme Evil probably wouldn't be able to enjoy it.

Old Man Extreme Evil and the big-mouthed monster were fellow sufferers in the dungeon and had a pretty good relationship.

Most importantly, the Azure Demon Region was so close to the Takeshi Swirl Forest. If something big really happened in the divine realm, he might be able to escape back to the Takeshi Swirl Forest and use it as a shelter. This was Old Man Extreme Evil's plan.

The Dao disciples who'd sneaked into the divine realm were scattered all over the place.
Yu Taibai's goal was to find the Dao disciples in the vast divine realm …
As for Jing Wuhuan, he represented the Gu Clan and went to Xunmu to meet with the Xuanyue Clan.
