Chapter 2292
In the Realm of the Gods, Dao tablets had always been treated as a cultivation resource.
Thus, apart from a very small number of Dao tablets in special locations, almost all of the 3,000 Dao tablets were controlled by various factions.
The Profound Thunder Divine Dao tablet was controlled by the Fang Family. As one of the Realm of the Gods, outsiders naturally did not dare to have any objections.
However, some of the Dao tablets were controlled by first-tier and second-tier clans.
The other clans were envious of the benefits brought by the Dao tablets. Naturally, there would be disputes and even war.
Several hours later …
The endless forest in front of Luo Zheng suddenly disappeared, replaced by an extremely mystical grassland.
The lush green grass sprouted and grew at an inconceivable speed. Then, it quickly withered and turned yellow, turning into withered grass. From this withered grass, green grass sprouted and grew again.
From high up in the sky, one could clearly see that this grassland was constantly changing from green to yellow, from yellow to green …
"Ordinary grasslands wither with each passing year. However, the grasslands here wither in the span of a breath." Luo Zheng gazed at this grassland.
This scene might seem mystical, but after careful consideration, one could understand.
Due to the dao stele of the Life Divine Dao, this place was filled with powerful life force. Under the stimulation of the life force, the grass grew rapidly. It was also because of this that they reached the end of their lives in advance and withered rapidly.
Not long after following this grassland, an enormous green stone tablet appeared in the distance.
Even from such a distance, that powerful life force could affect Luo Zheng …
And on the other side of this stone tablet, there was another enormous stone tablet. This was the Dao tablet of the Azure Wood Divine Dao. And on the other side of the Azure Wood Divine Dao, there was a row of neatly arranged trees, dense and dense.
The distance between the two Dao tablets was less than three hundred meters.
This scene did not surprise Luo Zheng. The Immeasurable Ruler had created an image in his mind that he could not forget. In this image, the two Dao tablets were originally right next to each other.
According to Luo Zheng's previous plan, if there were people guarding these two Dao tablets, he would also throw out some Shenwu coins.
After fusing the two Divine Daos, he would quickly leave.
But when Luo Zheng flew in front of the Dao tablets, a trace of surprise appeared on his face.
Around these two Dao steles, there wasn't a single True God …
Previously, there were quite a number of Real Gods in the surroundings of the Mystic Thunder Divine Path. Moreover, the Fang Family had specially sent people to guard it.
There were many cultivators of the Life Divine Dao and the Green Wood Divine Dao in the Realm of the Gods. It was impossible for no one to come because of their reputation. This was not logical.
However, just as Luo Zheng approached, a voice suddenly sounded.
"Outsiders, you are not allowed to approach the Dao Stele. If you don't want to die, hurry up and leave!"
Luo Zheng frowned slightly.
The voice came from the ground, but there was no True God hiding there.
Given the strength of Luo Zheng's soul, even a Paragon would not be able to hide. The source of this warning was somewhat strange.
Luo Zheng did not listen to the warning of the voice. Instead, he continued to approach the Dao tablet.
"If you take another step forward, you'll definitely die!"
The warning voice rang out again.
This time, Luo Zheng's gaze was fixed on the green grass below. His eyes flashed slightly. "So it's Plant Sound Transmission …"
The true speaker was not hiding here. He was just using the Green Wood Divine Dao's Plant Sound Transmission to borrow the surrounding sounds to make a sound. It was just a small trick.
"I'm just borrowing the Dao to observe the Dao Stele. I mean no harm," Luo Zheng replied with cupped hands.
Then, he calmly approached the Dao Stele.
Unexpectedly, just as he took a few steps forward, there was a sudden fluctuation from the ground.
"Rumble …"
Long thorns broke out of the ground and pierced toward Luo Zheng.
The thorns on these long thorns faintly flickered with a pale green light. Obviously, they contained a strong poison.
"Whoosh!"
Luo Zheng's figure flashed lightly, easily avoiding these long thorns.
Although he was not afraid of these long thorns or the strong poison on them, Luo Zheng was still rather angry that someone had attacked him without saying anything.
"I said, I just want to observe the Dao Stele. If Your Excellency wants to attack again, don't blame me for being impolite …"
However, before Luo Zheng's voice died away, the long thorns continued to break out of the ground and came straight for him.
Luo Zheng's eyes flashed fiercely. He twisted his body and displayed the Thousand Pound Drop.
He suddenly fell from the sky!
"Crack!"
When the long thorns suddenly came into contact with Luo Zheng, they broke one after another, unable to cause any harm to Luo Zheng.
"Boom!"
The moment Luo Zheng fell to the ground, a huge force poured into the ground.
The soil here was loose and soft. The moment the force poured in, it immediately pressed down a large pit hundreds of feet wide. The surrounding ground seemed to ripple and fluctuate in all directions.
Then, the surroundings fell into silence.
Luo Zheng glanced indifferently at the Dao Stele and walked forward again. But at this moment, a sky full of green arrows suddenly flew from the distant forest.
"Swish swish swish …"
"You're courting death!"
Luo Zheng was finally a little angry.
His figure suddenly flashed and moved to the front of the forest. At the same time, he suddenly pulled out his long sword and slashed out a sword light that was thousands of feet wide.
"Rumble …"
This sword light flew out, and tens of thousands of towering trees were cut in half by Luo Zheng.
This sword light swept across dozens of miles, and countless towering trees collapsed. It was a mess …
And among these collapsed trees, there were constantly figures flashing by, and from time to time, there were screams.
Although these people's movements were extremely fast, and they used the Green Wood Divine Dao's "Hidden Wood" to merge themselves with the trees, how could they escape from Luo Zheng's divine sense?
Luo Zheng's divine sense swept over and was slightly surprised. There were thousands of True Gods hidden in this forest. This should be a large group.
Luo Zheng didn't want to kill too much and incur some sins for no reason. He just wanted to teach these guys a little lesson.
But just as he turned and was about to approach the Dao Stele, he heard a voice in the forest. "The experts invited by the Mo Clan are too strong! Let's fight! Form the Green Wood Formation to suppress him! "
As soon as the voice fell, bits of green light shot up into the sky. The True Gods hiding in the forest released their Taoist Connotation. These Taoist Connotations connected with each other to form a mysterious formation.
"Swish …"
The forest, under the connection of the Taoist Connotation, blended into a whole and turned into a giant beast.
In the face of such a turbulent Taoist Connotation, Luo Zheng's face finally showed a trace of caution. It seemed that the other party had mistaken him for a helper invited by the Mo Clan.

Chapter 2293
In the middle of this forest, nine upper-level True Gods stood in the air.
Waves of deep and shallow Dao Accumulations gathered from all directions and connected tightly with these nine upper-level True Gods.
Their clan had been residing in the Fenglin Region for generations.
In ancient times, they were once a big clan, and two Secondary Sages were born in the clan.
It was a pity that these two Secondary Sages died in Saint Mountain when they were fighting for the right to be conferred as a Sage, causing their clan to be unable to recover from the setback.
Since then, their clan had been in decline until now …
In the last Divine Epoch, their clan's only Paragon died, and they were even more on the wane.
After losing the protection of a Paragon True God, they would even lose these two Dao tablets that they relied on for survival.
Another second-tier clan in Fenglin Region, the Mo Clan, coveted the control of these two Dao tablets and attacked repeatedly.
However, although their clan had declined, the Green Wood Formation left behind by the two former Secondary Sages was also extremely powerful. With thousands of people joining forces, it was enough to fight against a Paragon True God!
This time, when they heard that the Mo Clan had gone to Soft Light Region to invite an expert to help, their clan was on high alert and hid here in advance.
Luo Zheng had unintentionally barged into this place, and they naturally mistook him as an "expert" invited by the Mo Clan.
However, just as the nine upper-level True Gods set up the formation, a wave of space fluctuation suddenly appeared in front of them.
The expression of the leader of the nine True Gods changed. "Not good!"
Just as the old man's voice fell, Luo Zheng's figure had already appeared in front of them.
"Shifting Wood!"
"Fantasy Wood!"
"Knot …"
Although the nine upper-level True Gods' movements were extremely fast, Luo Zheng was even faster.
Before the Green Wood Formation completely closed, Luo Zheng was like a ghost, charging in front of the old man.
Bang!
With a light shoulder bump, the old man instantly felt his throat heat up. His head went black, and his whole person crashed onto the ground.
This old man was the leader of the Green Wood Formation. After he left the formation, how could this formation still be set up?
The other upper-level True Gods were flustered, as if trying to save this formation that was on the verge of collapse.
How would Luo Zheng give them the chance?
"Bang bang bang bang bang …"
Since Luo Zheng knew that they had mistaken him for someone else, he wouldn't kill them.
With a flash of his figure, he smashed these upper true gods into the ground.
By the time the last upper True Deity landed, the entire grand array had finally collapsed.
The nine True Gods lying on the ground coughed up blood and looked at Luo Zheng in despair.
The old man in the lead knew that the situation was hopeless. His face was ashen as he panted and said, "The Mo Clan … a mere second-tier clan was actually able to invite such an expert. The heavens want to destroy my Green Wood Clan!"
Their clan prospered because of the two Dao tablets. In the past, the two secondary saints also majored in the Green Wood Divine Dao. Following the development of history, they called themselves the Green Wood Clan. In reality, they were an amalgamation of many great clans.
The other True Gods were also silent as they stared at Luo Zheng.
In the face of such an expert, even shouting "fight to the death" was meaningless.
"I don't know any Mo family," Luo Zheng stared at the old man and said calmly.
The old man was dumbfounded.
Seeing that the old man didn't respond, Luo Zheng added, "I'm just a passerby who wanted to comprehend the Dao tablets. You guys attacked me first."
When the nine upper True Gods heard Luo Zheng's words, their expressions were naturally very interesting.
There were really too few Perfect True Gods in the Deity Plane. In addition, the Mo Clan had spoken first and Luo Zheng just happened to appear here. Naturally, they naturally assumed that Luo Zheng was an expert invited by the Mo Clan.
"You, you … really aren't someone invited by the Mo Clan?" The old man looked at Luo Zheng and looked like he was about to cry.
"Indeed, I'm not," Luo Zheng replied calmly.
Luo Zheng's reply made the True Gods of the Green Wood Clan heave a sigh of relief. Their faces were still full of bitterness.
Even if they tried their best, it would still be difficult to deal with the Mo Clan. Now that the nine of them had been injured by Luo Zheng, when the expert invited by the Mo Clan really came, how would they deal with him?
The old man thought for a while and his eyes lit up. He said to Luo Zheng, "Friend, no … expert, our Green Wood Clan is being invaded by an enemy. I can give you all the Green Wood Clan's savings over the years. I only ask that you help us resist the enemy!"
Luo Zheng frowned slightly.
He only wanted to absorb the complete Taoist Connotation and light up the Dao tablets. He didn't plan to stay for long.
If he agreed to this old man's request, he would be entangled in the conflict between the two clans and would waste a lot of time.
"These two Dao tablets are controlled by your clan?" Luo Zheng asked.
"Yes, our Green Wood Clan has controlled these two Dao tablets for many Divine Epochs …"
Before the old man could finish, Luo Zheng casually took out a hundred Shenwu coins. "Sorry, I only want to borrow these two Dao tablets. I don't care about anything else."
After handing over the Shenwu Coins to the old man, Luo Zheng's figure flashed and once again teleported between the two steles.
The old man held the coins with a face full of bitterness and disappointment. What their Green Wood Clan needed wasn't Shenwu coins, but the protection of an expert …
However, the old man gritted his teeth and took out a healing pill. He then said to the others, "Everyone, heal quickly. Since this person isn't an expert invited by the Mo Clan, we might still be able to fight!"
Thus, the other upper True Gods followed the old man's lead and sat down to heal their wounds.
Fortunately, Luo Zheng had been merciful and had only used brute force. Moreover, he had controlled his strength to a minimum, so their injuries weren't too serious.
"Huff …"
On the other side, Luo Zheng sat down facing the Life Divine Dao tablet.
The Infinity Ruler in his mind flickered, and the pair of golden eyes in his eyes began to absorb the complete Dao of the Life Divine Dao.
At this moment, over a thousand True Gods suddenly charged out from the other side of the grassland. These True Gods aggressively charged toward the Dao tablets.
Luo Zheng's gaze swept over them. He already knew that this group of people should be the "Mo Clan" that the old man had mentioned.
It was hard to say who was right and who was wrong when the two clans fought. Luo Zheng didn't want to get involved, so he naturally couldn't be bothered. He focused on absorbing his own Divine Dao.
"Old Man Di Mu! It's for your own good to have you obediently hand over these two Dao tablets. You don't have the strength, yet you want to take the tablets for yourself? That's called overestimating yourself, hahaha … "The leader of the Mo Clan was dressed in golden armor and wielding a black spear.
This person was called Mo Kuan, the current patriarch of the Mo Clan. He was also an extremely ambitious person. In his eyes, the most important step for the Mo Clan's rise was to take the two Dao tablets for himself.
This time, Mo Kuan had come prepared!
However, after he finished speaking, his gaze landed on Luo Zheng, and his pupils suddenly contracted.
A Perfect True God!
The thousand True Gods of the Mo Clan originally had high morale and looked aggressive. However, after noticing Luo Zheng, their momentum instantly weakened.
"Mo Kuan! You said that the other party only has nine upper-level True Gods. That's why I, He Yidao, helped you. I don't want to be enemies with a Perfect True God. "Behind Mo Kuan, there was a black-clothed man with a malicious expression.
Although He Yidao was also a Perfect True God, he didn't come from Luxurious Floating Island.
In terms of strength, he was far inferior to a Perfect True God from a wealthy family.
Even so, He Yidao could do whatever he wanted in the Deity Plane. After all, there were too few Perfect True Gods in the Deity Plane.
This time, the Mo Clan had paid a terrible price to invite He Yidao to help. However, He Yidao didn't want to be enemies with another Perfect True God.
Mo Kuan looked at Luo Zheng with uncertainty. He noticed that Luo Zheng was focused on comprehending the Dao tablet. His eyes lit up slightly, and he said softly, "Brother He, don't be anxious. This person shouldn't be a helper invited by the Green Wood Clan. We just have to deal with the Green Wood Clan!"
"We'll do as you say. However, I'll say this first. If this young man interferes, I'll leave without saying anything!" He Yidao said.
"That's right, that's right …" Mo Kuan said as he looked at Luo Zheng nervously.
"That's good!"
He Yidao sneered and clapped his hands lightly. A formless Taoist Connotation spread out.
Behind him, thirteen upper-level True Gods soared into the sky.
These upper-level True Gods had cold expressions and their eyes were emotionless. They were like puppets, but they were real flesh and blood.
At that moment, Luo Zheng had finished absorbing the Taoist Connotation of the Life Divine Path and was ready to absorb the Green Wood Divine Path.
At that moment, he was attracted by He Yidao's Taoist Connotation.
"Great Ruling Skill?" Luo Zheng raised his eyebrows slightly.

Chapter 2294
Luo Zheng discovered an azure-robed woman among the thirteen upper True Gods.
Although it had been several years, Luo Zheng could still clearly see this woman's appearance. She was exactly the same as Ji Luoxue in the Great Evolution Universe!
However, there were so many True Gods in the Divine Territory, and there were still many people who looked similar. He did not act rashly, but suppressed his thoughts and quietly observed.
Whoosh whoosh whoosh whoosh …
Under He Yidao's control, the thirteen upper True Gods rapidly changed their formation.
The thirteen seemed scattered, but in reality, they contained a profound meaning. It seemed that He Yidao was not only proficient in the Great Domination Art, but also in formations.
In the forest, the elder of the Green Wood Race summoned the other eight upper True Gods and formed the Great Green Wood Formation again.
The elder's eyes were filled with bitterness.
After seeing this Paragon He Yidao, he knew that the Green Wood Race's momentum was gone.
Although the Great Green Wood Formation was said to be able to resist Paragons, the Green Wood Race had declined to this day and was no longer as powerful as before. Moreover, Luo Zheng had easily broken the formation earlier, greatly reducing the Great Green Wood Formation's power.
Even so, he still chose to resist with all his might.
He Yidao's eyes were filled with disdain when he saw the circulation of Dao in the forest.
He Yidao sneered and said, "This petty trick is nothing more than a praying mantis blocking the arm. Thirteen Poison Attack Formation, break their formation for me!"
Swish!
In an instant, wisps of black poisonous miasma appeared on the thirteen upper True Gods under He Yidao's control!
A sweet and fishy smell spread from these upper True Gods. Clearly, the black poisonous miasma was extremely toxic.
He Yidao was not from a wealthy family and was also a person who was both good and evil. Without the support of a wealthy family's resources, it was inevitable that he would use some cruel methods to become stronger.
The Thirteen Poison Attack Formation was to capture thirteen upper True Gods and soak them in the Divine Territory's Phoenix Crown Blood Droplets to create thirteen "poison men". When facing an enemy, they would release billowing poisonous miasma that others could not deal with.
Such poison men would wither on the ground and die if they touched trees. Even He Yidao did not dare to touch them in the slightest.
What was even more amazing was that although the "Phoenix Crown Blood Drop" itself was a strange poison, after combining with the poisonous blood, it could greatly increase one's strength.
However, this kind of strengthening could not be maintained for long. The phoenix crown blood droplets would constantly corrode the bloodline of a true god. At the same time, it would invade the inner world of the true god through the bloodline. In less than half a year, the true god's inner world would be destroyed, and the true god would become a cripple.
Therefore, every once in a while, He Dao would capture some upper true gods to make new puppets.
Luo Zheng stared at the woman who seemed to be Ji Luoxue.
Although the poisonous miasma on the surface of her body was surging and almost completely covering her, she still relied on her own Taoist Connotation to expel the poisonous miasma from her body.
"Five Elements Divine Dao!"
After discovering that this woman cultivated the Five Elements Divine Dao, Luo Zheng was able to confirm her identity.
In the Grand Evolution Universe, Ji Luoxue had stepped into the Dao via the Five Element Divine Dao!
Originally, he didn't want to get involved in the enmity between the Mo Clan and the Green Wood Clan. However, it seemed that he had no choice but to act now.
With a thought, Luo Zheng slowly stood up.
He Dao was naturally extremely arrogant in front of the Green Wood Clan. In reality, most of his attention was focused on Luo Zheng. After all, the only person who was worthy of his vigilance was Luo Zheng.
Now that he saw Luo Zheng stand up, He Dao's malicious expression changed slightly. He then said, "Friend, I've always had enmity with the Green Wood Clan. I hope that you won't interfere. After I take down the Green Wood Clan, I'll share any benefits with you. What do you think?"
Luo Zheng looked at He Dao expressionlessly and said indifferently, "Whatever enmity you have with the Green Wood Clan has nothing to do with me."
Hearing this, He Dao was slightly delighted.
However, Luo Zheng then said, "However, I might have a bit of enmity with you!"
"What?" He Dao's expression froze slightly. He didn't know what Luo Zheng meant.
However, as soon as Luo Zheng finished speaking, his figure had already mysteriously disappeared from where he stood.
As a grassroots True God, He Dao had stepped into the Great Perfection stage. He Dao didn't know how many great dangers and life-or-death situations he had experienced in his life!
The moment he saw Luo Zheng disappear, he knew that Luo Zheng was going to attack him.
Swish!
The moment Luo Zheng appeared beside He Dao, he stretched out his hand and grabbed at He Dao's head.
With Luo Zheng's current strength, as long as he grabbed He Dao, he would be able to crush him with his strength alone.
However, He Dao's reaction was extremely fast. He promptly used greater teleportation, so naturally, Luo Zheng's grab missed.
In the next instant, He Dao had already escaped into the middle of the 13 puppets.
The poisonous miasma emitted by the 13 puppets curled up and protected him. Then, He Dao's shocked and angry voice sounded, "I've never met you before. What enmity do you have with me?"
Luo Zheng said indifferently, "If I say there is, then there is!"
The Great Domination Technique could dictate the life and death of the puppets, and the puppets couldn't resist. Naturally, Luo Zheng wouldn't point it out.
Crackle!
After he finished speaking, a pair of thunder wings suddenly spread out behind Luo Zheng's back!
Then, he transformed into a bolt of lightning and charged toward He Dao.
The Green Wood Clan originally looked as if they were facing a great enemy. Because of Luo Zheng's previous attitude, they didn't dare to count on Luo Zheng to help them.
After all, Luo Zheng was neither related to them in any way. It would be good if he didn't take advantage of their misfortune.
They didn't expect that two Perfect-stage True Gods would fight because of a disagreement. Their faces were filled with inexplicable expressions.
However, inexplicably, a glimmer of hope lit up in the hearts of these people who were originally in despair!
Seeing Luo Zheng charging toward him, He Dao's expression turned cold.
Swish! Swish! Swish!
Swish! The poisonous miasma on the surface of the 13 puppets was like living creatures. It transformed into countless tentacles and swept toward Luo Zheng.
Even a Perfect-stage True God would have to retreat in the face of the poisonous miasma. He Dao wanted to make Luo Zheng retreat in the face of difficulties!
Facing the poisonous miasma that had transformed into tentacles, Luo Zheng didn't retreat in the slightest. He submerged himself into the miasma …
The poisonous miasma that even a Perfect-stage True God would have to retreat in the face of had no effect on Luo Zheng.
Seeing this scene, He Dao's expression turned gloomy again.
If the poisonous miasma couldn't affect Luo Zheng, he was afraid that he would lose without a doubt. However, his greatest reliance was the Thirteen Poison Attack Formation. At this moment, he wasn't willing to teleport away and planned to take a risk.
Clenching his teeth, He Dao suddenly clapped his hands together and slapped toward Luo Zheng.
The poisonous miasma of the 13 puppets gathered in his palm. This poisonous miasma had fused with the Taoist Connotation of the 13 puppets. Coupled with He Dao's cultivation, the poisonous palm formed was naturally powerful!
Whoosh!
Seeing that He Dao didn't retreat but faced the enemy head-on, Luo Zheng raised his eyebrows slightly. This was exactly what he wanted.
Luo Zheng didn't hesitate at all and slapped toward He Dao …

Chapter 2295
"Bang!"
The moment the two palms collided, the poisonous miasma on He Dao's palm was smashed to pieces.
A tremendous force spread up He Dao's arm, and his arm was completely pulverized into powder ….
Although He Dao knew that he couldn't defeat Luo Zheng, he didn't expect the difference to be so great.
He had once seized an opportunity to spar with a Paragon from the Noble Floating Island. Although he had lost, he hadn't been completely crushed.
Thus, this palm was completely out of his expectations!
While He Yidao was still in shock, Luo Zheng took advantage of the situation and slapped He Yidao's dantian with his other hand.
Sometimes, it was extremely difficult for a Paragon to kill a Paragon.
This was because both sides could teleport away in the blink of an eye. If He Yidao really escaped, it would be troublesome for him to find a spatial tunnel to chase after him. At the very least, it would take him a lot of time.
The force of this slap seemed gentle, but it was enough to destroy He Yidao's inner world!
"Crack!"
A crisp sound rang out.
He Dao's inner world had already shattered into pieces …
He cultivated the Grand Domination Technique. This Divine Dao wasn't as strong as other Divine Daos, but the structure of his inner world was extremely perfect. When it shattered into pieces, the trillions of secondary lifeforms inside were all exterminated.
"Boom!"
After taking two consecutive hits from Luo Zheng, He Dao fell backwards and smashed into the Life Divine Dao's dao stele.
The dao steles of the Deity Realm were extremely sturdy. In theory, they couldn't be destroyed.
His physical body smashed into the stele, and all the bones in his body shattered. He sat on the ground, paralyzed.
However, a miraculous scene also appeared.
The Life Divine Dao's dao stele released a powerful life force that soaked into He Dao's body.
The injuries all over his body healed in the blink of an eye. Even the arm that had been smashed to pieces by Luo Zheng recovered in an instant …
However, the shattered inner world could no longer be restored.
Not far away, the patriarch of the Mo Family saw this scene and couldn't help but feel his heart beat wildly.
They were both Paragon True Gods, but how could the difference be so great? He Dao couldn't resist this person at all …
As for the members of the Green Wood Clan in the forest, they all widened their eyes.
The fact that Luo Zhengwen was willing to attack He Yidao had already caused them to fall into a state of ecstasy. They had never imagined that with just two palms, He Yidao would be easily dealt with. This was as simple as a perfected True God killing an upper True God.
Where did this young man come from?
Losing his inner world meant losing his cultivation. Even though He Dao's soul was still powerful, without the support of his Taoist Connotation, he couldn't use the Great Ruling Technique.
"Plop, plop …"
The thirteen "Poison Men" under He Dao's control fell from the sky.
He Daoist leaned against the stele and did not choose to run away.
Now that he was a cripple, it was impossible for him to escape from Luo Zheng. He could only stare at Luo Zheng with his malicious eyes.
Even now, He Daoist still couldn't figure out why Luo Zheng would attack him.
After some of the Poison Men were released from their shackles, they slowly regained their consciousness and slowly crawled up from the ground, their eyes filled with confusion.
"Where is this?!"
"What about me? Why are you here? "
"It's that person!"
Although these upper True Gods didn't know what was going on, when they saw He Dao, they couldn't help but tremble.
The painful experience of being captured by He Dao and being refined into a Poison Man was still vivid in their minds. However, their memories after that had completely disappeared. Naturally, they remembered He Yidao.
Ji Luoxue was the last to wake up.
She didn't know what the other Daoists were like, but she was sure that her experience was the most unlucky.
Just as she successfully snuck in, she was almost caught by a giant hand that appeared out of nowhere in the sky. Although she was lucky enough to escape, she was embroiled in an internal dispute of a family for no reason and was pursued all the way.
After much difficulty, she finally escaped the pursuit and her luck finally turned. She entered the "Heaven Radish Family" of the Thousand Cloud Region. This was a quasi-first-tier family and was considered pretty good in the entire Divine Region.
Relying on her outstanding talent, Ji Luoxue quickly gained a foothold in the Heaven Radish Family and attracted the attention of a Perfect True God in the "Heaven Radish Family."
In a short time, she lived up to expectations and became an upper True God …
However, the first time she went out, she encountered He Yidao and watched helplessly as she was refined into a Poison Man. After that, her memories were blank.
When she slowly woke up and saw He Yidao, fear instantly appeared on her face.
However, her eyes flashed and she saw a familiar figure standing beside her …
"Luo … Luo Zheng?"
After all, they hadn't met for so many years and Luo Zheng's aura had changed greatly after reaching Perfect True God. She actually didn't dare to recognize him!
Luo Zheng smiled slightly at Ji Luoxue.
"You're really Luo Zheng?" Ji Luoxue still couldn't believe it.
Luo Zheng smiled and said, "The real deal."
After confirming Luo Zheng's identity, she was still stunned for a while before hot tears filled her eyes. She was practically biting her lips to stop herself from crying.
Ji Luoxue was a prodigy in the Divine Region and had never suffered like this. Walking in the Divine Region was difficult and she had suffered all kinds of hardships.
Before she was refined into a Poison Man, she only knew that she was dead for sure …
She never thought that she would be able to see Luo Zheng again the moment she woke up.
In her agitation, she did not hesitate in the slightest as she pounced towards Luo Zheng.
But when she reached him, she suddenly stopped in her tracks.
Ji Luoxue still remembered that when He Yidao was refining Poison Men, he had also been very careful. Her entire body was now filled with deadly poison and she was afraid that she would poison Luo Zheng.
Luo Zheng did not regard her as an outsider as he gently pulled her into his arms.
This little girl's journey in the Divine Region had also been rather bumpy. Although she was an extremely strong person, she could not help but explode at this moment, bawling like a little child.
When He Yidao, who was lying beneath the Dao Tablet, saw this scene, the corners of his eyes could not help but twitch.
"You said that the enmity between you and me is because of this little girl?" He Yidao asked unwillingly.
In He Yidao's eyes, these upper level True Gods were just puppets and they had to be replaced every year or so.
There were very few Perfect True Gods in the Divine Region but there were many upper level True Gods. If he wanted to capture these puppets and create Poison Men, it was all too easy and he had never thought much of these Poison Men.
"That's right, are you very surprised?" Luo Zheng asked back.
He Yidao laughed weakly, "I am very surprised indeed …"
"How should we deal with him?" Luo Zheng no longer cared about He Yidao and instead asked for Ji Luoxue's opinion.
"Kill him!"
After Ji Luoxue's agitation, she had regained her composure and she only said in a cold voice.
"That's right, kill this vicious bastard!"
"It would be best if we could extract his soul and refine it!"
Hearing these words, the other thirteen upper level True Gods who were also Poison Men looked at He Yidao with eyes full of hatred …
"Alright!"

Chapter 2296
Hearing Luo Zheng mention the antidote method, the Poison Humans were shocked.
Their hatred for He Yidao temporarily overshadowed their worry for themselves, forgetting that they had been refined into Poison Humans by He Yidao.
They weren't afraid of the poison in their bodies.
Although their bodies had been corroded to varying degrees by the phoenix coronet blood droplets, the poison in their bodies was easy to deal with.
However, the phoenix coronet blood droplets had flowed into their dantian through their meridians, beginning to invade their inner worlds. This was the most troublesome part.
"Right! Antidote! "
"My inner world is showing faint signs of collapse!"
"We can't kill him. Make him take out the antidote …"
These Upper True Gods' desire to live was just as strong.
If they really couldn't cultivate, their only option was to abandon their dantian.
However, if they did that, their cultivation would be lost, and they would be nothing more than cripples in the Realm of the Gods. No one was willing to accept such an outcome.
He Yidao's face was ashen.
After losing his dantian, he couldn't even commit suicide by exploding his inner world.
He Yidao was going to die either way. He Yidao wasn't willing to let his soul be tortured to death, not to mention that his opponent might use a soul-searching technique on him.
Completely disheartened, He Yidao lifted his head and untied his spatial ring, tossing it at Luo Zheng. He then said, "The phoenix coronet blood droplets can be used to force the poison out of the body by cultivating the 《Poison Fusion Technique》. The technique is in this spatial ring."
As He Yidao tossed the ring, he erased the soul imprint on it.
The ring flashed with red light, and an exquisite jade page appeared in Luo Zheng's hand.
He opened the jade page and flipped through it a few times, then nodded slightly. This 《Poison Fusion Technique》 was a reverse poison cultivation technique. It was used to detoxify the poison of True Gods who originally cultivated poison techniques.
But at this moment, He Yidao's mind stirred slightly, and an extremely powerful soul pressure suddenly came from the spatial ring in Luo Zheng's hand.
This soul pressure erupted and rushed straight into Luo Zheng's mind!
Immediately, He Yidao's eyes became hazy, as if he had become demented …
At this moment, he abandoned his physical body and flew away in his soul form!
He Daoist hadn't played any tricks on the 'Poison Fusion Art', but while Luo Zheng was verifying its authenticity, he had activated an attack-type soul magic tool in his spatial ring.
After all, he was a Consummate True God who practiced the "Great Ruling Technique." In terms of strength, he might not be as strong as a considerable number of Consummate True Gods, but his soul was extremely powerful. He Dao was able to seize the opportunity to escape by shaking Luo Zheng's mind with his soul magic treasure!
Although he was only left with his soul, he could still find an opportunity to possess someone else's body. In the Divine Realm, as long as one didn't die, there was always a chance.
If an ordinary Perfect Real God were to be attacked by a soul magic treasure, they might not be able to come back to their senses for a while.
However, the current Luo Zheng's soul was so sturdy that it even surpassed that of many secondary saints.
When that powerful soul fluctuation hit Luo Zheng's soul, it only made Luo Zheng slightly absent-minded.
In less than half a breath's time, Luo Zheng's eyes regained their clarity.
He raised his head to look at the path that He Dao's soul had taken. His eyes focused slightly as the surface of the soul in his mind began to glow with a faint golden light.
An extremely cold soul suppression burst forth from his eyes.
"Swish swish swish swish swish swish …"
Tens of thousands of little soul arrows pierced through the sky in an instant, completely enveloping He Dao's soul. In the blink of an eye, He Dao's soul was torn to shreds by countless little soul arrows.
The Poison Men, the Mo Clan, and the Green Wood Clan were unable to see this scene with their naked eyes.
However, they could sense the soul suppression that permeated the sky through their divine senses.
That soul suppression exuded an extremely sharp concept. Even if they used their divine senses to probe, it still caused their scalps to go numb.
This was enough to explain He Dao's tragic end.
The Green Wood Clan and the Poison Men weren't too afraid.
However, Mo Kuan and the Mo Clan's True Gods only felt a chill run down their spines.
Faced with such an unstoppable powerful cultivator, the Mo Clan was at a loss as to whether they should leave or stay. They were afraid that they would do something that would upset this young man and cause him to kill them. The Mo Clan Patriarch was also mulling over how to explain himself in his heart, so that he could draw a clear line between himself and He Dao.
After Luo Zheng had killed He Dao, he returned to Ji Luoxue's side and tossed the jade page handwritten book to her.
The other Poison Men also stared fixedly at the small booklet …..
Noticing their gazes, Luo Zheng smiled faintly and said to Ji Luoxue: "Make a copy of this book and give it to them."
"Mm." Ji Luoxue nodded and did as she was told.
It was at this moment that the old man from the Green Wood Clan led his clansmen out of the forest, heading straight for Luo Zheng.
"My friend, thank you for your help," the old man said with a face full of gratitude.
Luo Zheng's expression was calm as he replied: "I had no intention of doing anything. It was only because that fellow was unlucky that he attacked my friend."
He looked at Ji Luoxue with some worry in his heart.
After all, the Divine Region was not the Great Yan Universe. Those Dao Children could not all be so lucky and there was a possibility of them dying. He wondered how the other Dao Children were faring right now.
"Even so, it is still a blessing for our clan. You are also our clan's great benefactor." The old man's face was full of smiles as he took out a small jade box. He carefully opened the jade box and a jade-colored pill appeared in the box. "I see that your friend is also deeply poisoned. This pill is condensed from the Dao of the Divine Dao of Life and can only be produced once every Divine Era. It is the most precious treasure of our Green Wood Clan. After consuming it, one's physical body will immediately be reconstructed and all poisons will be expelled. Please kindly accept it, benefactor."
Luo Zheng glanced at the jade-colored pill. Indeed, extremely pure Dao of the Law of Life flowed within it.
After receiving the pill, Luo Zheng tossed it to Ji Luoxue.
Then, he turned his head to look at Mo Kuan, who was on the mountain slope, and said in a cold voice: "Originally, I did not want to participate in the conflict between your Mo Clan and the Green Wood Clan. However, the helper you invited harmed my friend. Consider it your Mo Clan's bad luck. From now on, your Mo Clan will migrate out of the Fenglin Region!"
A second-tier clan usually occupied several Divine Cities and had deep roots in a certain region.
However, Luo Zheng's one sentence was asking them to migrate …
Hearing Luo Zheng's words, Mo Kuan could only feel bitterness in his mouth. Fortunately, the young man wasn't a bloodthirsty person. If it was some Perfect Real God who was more ruthless, they would have already taken action and killed all of them.
Although he was unwilling, Mo Kuan still hurriedly nodded his head. "That He Dao refining poison humans is indeed despicable. Sir, you can be considered to have gotten rid of a huge scourge. My Mo Clan is blind and should be punished. We will naturally move out of the Fenglin Region!"

Chapter 2297
When the Green Wood Tribe saw that Luo Zheng had expelled the Mo Family, they were even more grateful and immediately invited Luo Zheng to their tribe for a gathering.
However, Luo Zheng refused.
His original plan was to absorb all of the Dao from the tablet before leaving.
Now that he had run into Ji Luoxue, he naturally had to ensure her safety.
The elder of the Green Wood Tribe didn't dare force the issue and led his tribesmen back into the forest after thanking him profusely.
"I still need to study the tablet. You can practice the Tandem Poison Dao on the spot and wait for me to absorb the Dao before sending you back to the immortal residence," Luo Zheng said to Ji Luoxue.
Ji Luoxue nodded and sat down cross-legged on the spot.
In truth, the other twelve poison humans had immediately started practicing the Tandem Poison Dao after obtaining the Tandem Poison Dao.
The tablet of the Life Divine Dao was very peaceful. Not only did it not use Dao to attack, it healed all life that came close.
In comparison, the tablet of the Green Wood Divine Dao was much more violent and violent, on par with the tablet of the Black Thunder Divine Dao. Luo Zheng had to expend some effort to get close to the tablet.
After the time it takes to burn a stick of incense, Luo Zheng finished absorbing the Dao and returned to Ji Luoxue's side.
Ji Luoxue had completely entered a meditative state.
It was obvious that she had forced out much of the poisonous miasma.
After the dark brown miasma was forced out of her body, it burrowed into the soil around her and formed an eye-catching black circle.
Of course, the same was true for the other poison humans.
"I'll send her back directly …" Luo Zheng thought to himself.
He had just left the immortal residence not long ago. If he returned now, it would be troublesome to leave again.
Ji Luoxue had been to the immortal residence once before. If she returned, Yu Shenfeng and Aunt Hanjiu would take care of her as well.
When he thought of this, the Gen token appeared in his hand. He activated it and tapped it on Ji Luoxue's body.
Hum …
The spatial ripples emanating from the Gen token startled Ji Luoxue awake.
"Luo Zheng, you …" Ji Luoxue exclaimed.
"I'll send you back to the immortal residence. Don't resist," Luo Zheng said softly.
"But I …" Ji Luoxue hesitated.
In that moment of hesitation, Ji Luoxue had completely sunk into the spatial tunnel and quickly escaped into the immortal residence.
"It's been so many years since I last saw that fellow, but his personality is still the same …"
Throughout the entire process, Ji Luoxue had an angry expression.
Although Ji Luoxue was well aware that this immortal estate was safe, she was still willing to wander around the divine realm with Luo Zheng. However, he had sent her back without any explanation. Naturally, she felt even more depressed.
After sending Ji Luoxue back to the Immortal Abode, Luo Zheng looked at the Gen Command Token and smiled. He stuffed it into his spatial ring and teleported away.
…
…
After passing through the Fenglin Region was the Zixia Region, the Mingzhou Region, and other small regions.
Luo Zheng used Greater Teleportation all the way, not stopping at all.
Occasionally, there would be people who would see Luo Zheng's figure teleporting, but in the blink of an eye, he would disappear under the astonished gazes of those people. Some of the more experienced Gods would point to the place where Luo Zheng disappeared and say that this was a divine ability that could only be used by Paragon True Gods and above.
After passing through a series of small regions, Luo Zheng went around a few large regions. These large regions all had Dao tablets. Since it was on the way, Luo Zheng naturally had no reason to miss them.
Just like that, he went south while collecting Dao tablets.
In twenty days, he found a total of thirteen Dao tablets. It could be considered a small harvest.
It was not an easy task to collect all of the Dao tablets.
After all, with the cultivation of a Paragon True God, the distance of Greater Teleportation in the divine realm was still too short. If he became a Quasi-Saint or even a Saint, it would not take much time to set foot on the three thousand Dao tablets scattered throughout the divine realm …
After a month, Luo Zheng finally arrived at the Evil Ghost Region.
The Evil Ghost Region was not called the Evil Ghost Region back then.
60 Divine Epochs ago, the Abyss Demonic Region was not as large as it was now. The entire Abyss Demonic Region had only swallowed the Red Lotus Region.
As time passed, the Abyss Demonic Region grew larger and larger. In the end, it expanded into the Evil Ghost Region.
Although the inside and outside of the forbidden area were two completely different worlds.
However, the forbidden area still had a considerable influence on the outside world. After the Abyss Demonic Region expanded into this region, all kinds of ferocious creatures continuously appeared in this region. Even some originally docile creatures became incomparably ferocious. Thus, this region was named the Evil Ghost Region.
Whoosh!
Luo Zheng directly teleported from a neighboring large region to the Evil Ghost Region.
As soon as he teleported over …
"Hahaha …"
Not far above Luo Zheng's head, a monster with a human head and an eagle's body laughed bitterly. It opened its bloody mouth and bit at Luo Zheng's head.
"Pa!"
Luo Zheng's eyes flashed. A bolt of lightning came out of nowhere and smashed this human-headed eagle-bodied monster into pieces.
After killing this monster, Luo Zheng realized that something was wrong.
After casually teleporting, he was actually in a large canyon. Moreover, there were countless human-headed eagle-bodied monsters squatting on both sides of the canyon.
The roar caused by the bolt of lightning reverberated in the canyon, immediately startling these monsters.
The silence in the canyon lasted for three breaths of time. Then, all the human-headed eagle-bodied monsters spread their wings and pounced at Luo Zheng.
At the same time, there was a strange laughter.
"Hahahaha …"
"Kakaka …"
Seeing this scene, Luo Zheng shook his head slightly. Next, he used greater teleportation again and his figure quietly disappeared from the canyon.
However, the Evil Ghost Region really lived up to its reputation.
Luo Zheng teleported again and unexpectedly encountered a type of butterfly with a ghost face painted on its wings.
After using the Mystic Thunder Divine Dao to kill some of the ghost-faced butterflies, he could only teleport again.
After such bumps and bumps, Luo Zheng finally arrived at the east-central part of the Evil Ghost Region. This was the place where the Evil Ghost Region and the Abyss Demonic Region bordered.
"This is the Abyss Demonic Region …" Floating in the air, Luo Zheng stared at the scene in front of him in a daze.
Directly in front of him was a huge pit.
The bottom of this pit couldn't be seen at all. After all, the entire Abyss Demonic Region had swallowed several large regions!
Although Luo Zheng had seen this scene through the Stone Barf Rat, it was far less shocking than seeing it in person.
The pale green smoke was endless, constantly drifting up from the bottom of the abyss.
Although the forbidden area had sealed off the space, the smell of sulfur contained in the smoke still assailed his nostrils …
"Rumble …"
The edges of the pit were constantly collapsing.
The Abyss Demonic Region was still relentlessly expanding, eroding the territory of the Evil Ghost Region. At this rate, the Evil Ghost Region would be completely swallowed by the Abyss Demonic Region in a few divine eras.
Some people had even predicted that if no one controlled the Abyss Demonic Region, then the entire Divine Realm would be swallowed by the Abyss Demonic Region.
Luo Zheng sized up the surrounding environment, a trace of embarrassment appearing on his face.

Chapter 2298
After circling around the edge of the pit for a while, Luo Zheng didn't find anything out of the ordinary.
Then, his eyes narrowed slightly and he released his Divine Sense, covering an area of several dozen kilometers.
Apart from a part of his Divine Sense being reflected by an invisible wall when it entered the forbidden area, he still didn't find anything out of the ordinary.
After fumbling around for a while, Luo Zheng could only take out the rat corpse from his Sumeru Ring.
After activating the light emitted by the Stone Forest Rat's corpse, the scene once again appeared in Luo Zheng's mind.
Luo Zheng followed the scene and moved to the center.
In this position, the scene in Luo Zheng's mind completely merged with the scene he saw with his eyes.
"It really is a place …" Luo Zheng's face was a little confused.
It was reasonable to say that the birth of such a creature would cause quite a commotion, so why couldn't he see anything out of the ordinary?
Could it be that he was too late and the reincarnation of the Stone Forest Rat had already been dug out?
Just as Luo Zheng was thinking, more than a dozen streaks of light suddenly appeared in the Divine Sense he released.
"Someone's coming?"
Luo Zheng immediately withdrew his Divine Sense and looked down.
Directly below him, there was a crack extending from the edge of the pit. Then, he took a step and escaped into the crack.
"Dao of Nature! Hide! "
After softly spitting out a word, his aura disappeared and he completely merged with the surrounding scenery.
In other people's perception, Luo Zheng was no different from a stone.
Not long after Luo Zheng hid, more than a dozen people flew above Luo Zheng. Among them, a big man sniffed and said, "The smell coming from the Abyss Demon Domain is really unpleasant!"
"It's just the smell of sulfur, what's wrong with that? Did any of you notice a strand of Divine Sense passing by just now? "A calm voice said.
"No!"
"I didn't feel it either."
"How could there be other people in this damned place? Patriarch Qianlong is too sensitive, hehe. "
Several other voices said.
"Was it my imagination? I clearly sensed it, but that Divine Sense was withdrawn too quickly. It was only for an instant … "That calm voice murmured.
Hearing this, Luo Zheng, who was hiding in the crack, felt his heart skip a beat.
This person is quite sharp. I wonder what his cultivation is.
If they were just some upper true gods, Luo Zheng wouldn't care.
Luo Zheng wasn't even afraid of some Great Perfection experts, unless it was a Great Perfection expert from a wealthy family who could attract the Saints.
However, the probability of this happening wasn't high …
"Don't overthink it. Getting down to business is the most important thing. Lu Tianyi, where is the location you mentioned?" the calm voice asked calmly.
Then, a muffled voice sounded, "It's right under our feet. I set up an illusion array last time and hid the cave!"
Dozens of days ago, Lu Tianyi had gone to the Evil Ghost Realm to capture a kind of ghost rhinoceros.
The value of this Ghost Rhinoceros Horn was very high. It was necessary to refine many top quality pills. This kind of item was very popular among the rich and powerful families, first and second tier families.
Now, one Ghost Rhinoceros Horn could be sold for more than twenty Shenwu coins.
For True Gods, other than entering forbidden areas, it wasn't easy to earn Shenwu coins. Hunting Ghost Rhinoceros Horns was naturally a good way to make money.
As more and more True Gods hunted Ghost Rhinoceros Horns, the number of Ghost Rhinoceros Horns in the Evil Ghost Realm naturally decreased.
Last time, Lu Tianyi didn't find any prey along the way and could only go deep into the Evil Ghost Realm. He didn't expect to see a strange phenomenon at the edge of the Abyss Devil Realm.
When he flew here, he found that the area had collapsed and a cave had appeared.
Lu Tianyi couldn't figure out what was at the bottom of the cave, but that strange phenomenon definitely meant that something had been born.
It could be a rare treasure, but it could also be some peerless monster …
Lu Tianyi was only an upper true god. How could he dare to explore without his strength?
Thus, he set up an illusory array to tightly cover up the cave.
Lu Tianyi's strength might not be the best among upper true gods, but he was extremely proficient in the art of setting up formations. He actually managed to deceive Luo Zheng.
"It's just an illusion array?"
Hearing this, Luo Zheng secretly pursed his lips. He was actually deceived by an illusion array due to his carelessness.
Unable to hold himself back, he carefully poked his head out from a corner of the crack and glanced at the True Gods. His eyebrows suddenly raised.
"A quasi-sage and two Perfect True Gods?" Luo Zheng's eyes revealed surprise.
The number of quasi-sages in the God Realm was even rarer. Only a few first-rate families had quasi-sages overseeing them.
He didn't expect to run into one today by Luo Zheng …
Just as Luo Zheng was sizing them up, the purple-robed quasi-sage suddenly sensed something and abruptly turned his head.
That pair of eyes were as sharp as swords as they swept across a crack not far away.
But that crack was clearly empty!
The quasi-sage's expression became even stranger. He even released his divine sense, but he still didn't find anything!
"What's wrong with Patriarch Qianlong today? Why do you look so suspicious? "a white-robed middle-aged man beside him said with a smile.
This quasi-sage was Patriarch Qianlong, the master of one of the Qianlong Regions.
Some first-rate families couldn't enter the Sea of Time due to their own lack of strength. They could only expand in their own regions and eventually occupy the entire large region for themselves.
However, the wealthy families didn't care about such things.
For example, after Han Jia became a wealthy family on the floating island, the large regions under Han Jia's control were still allowed to expand. Han Jia didn't completely control all the large regions. Instead, they gave a lot of resources to the subordinate families.
Patriarch Qianlong knew that he had no hope of becoming a True God and couldn't bring his family into the wealthy families. Thus, he could only confer himself the title of "region master."
"I keep feeling that someone is spying on me. Looks like I'm overthinking," Patriarch Qianlong said faintly. He didn't expect that a Perfect True God would be able to hide under his nose.
At that moment, the True God called Lu Tianyi walked around an empty space three times. Then, he slammed his hands heavily on the ground.
A purple-red line spread rapidly along the ground. This line drew a large circle on the ground. The scene in this circle was formed by an illusion array. Now that Lu Tianyi had removed the illusion array, a sizable hole was revealed.
Patriarch Qianlong stood at the hole's entrance and looked down. Then, he was the first to jump in.
The other two Perfect True Gods and more than ten upper-level True Gods didn't hesitate and followed Patriarch Qianlong into the hole.
After a while, Luo Zheng slowly climbed out of the crack. His brows were slightly furrowed. "This hole was probably created for the Stone Forest Rats. Unexpectedly, someone found it in advance … What's more troublesome is that there's a quasi-sage inside!"

Chapter 2299
This cave had just been created, so the rocks and soil on the cave walls were still very soft.
If it wasn't touched properly, it would probably cause quite a commotion, and it might even collapse.
Although Luo Zheng used the True Meaning of Nature to hide himself, if he really alarmed the quasi-sage, such a concealment method would be meaningless.
Therefore, in the process of descending, Luo Zheng also carefully slowed down.
"Patriarch Qianlong, there are two paths below!"
The entire cave was straight up and down, but here it diverged into a fork.
The conversation below the cave naturally drilled into Luo Zheng's ears clearly.
"There are not two caves. I have already used my divine sense to investigate clearly. The one on the left is a dead end. Just take the right one," Patriarch Qianlong said indifferently.
"Then take the right one!"
The group chose the cave on the right and continued descending.
After a while, Luo Zheng also came to the fork. Fortunately, he had already heard these people's conversation just now.
Now, Luo Zheng did not dare to use his divine sense to identify the path, which was also a big problem. He could only follow behind this group of people.
Without much hesitation, Luo Zheng also chose the cave on the right …
As he continued to go deeper, the bottom of the cave became darker and darker, and the surrounding environment became more and more oppressive.
At the same time, there was also an increasingly pungent smell of sulfur!
"This sulfur smell is too smelly!" An upper True God complained.
"Do you think this cave leads all the way to the Abyss Demon Territory?" Another person muttered.
"Indeed, this cave is abnormally deep …"
The Abyss Demon Territory was like a nightmare to people in the God Realm.
Even the experts of the wealthy families fell in the Abyss Demon Territory. Therefore, this time, Dongfang Chunjun almost turned out in full force to be on the safe side.
If this cave really led to the Abyss Demon Territory, they would be in trouble.
"Haha, if this place really leads to the Abyss Demon Territory, I'm afraid we'll be rich!" Patriarch Qianlong said indifferently.
"Patriarch Qianlong is right. This place will become the first entrance to the Abyss Demon Territory!" Another person said.
The only entrance to the Abyss Demon Territory was located in the depths of the Red Lotus Region, and that entrance had already been occupied by the wealthy families.
Even a powerful quasi-sage like Patriarch Qianlong was not qualified to directly enter the forbidden area.
The only way was to ask for help from the Wealthy Clans and borrow their spatial passageways.
Even a Secondary Saint had to use such a method to enter the forbidden grounds. From this, one could imagine the importance of clans...
Luo Zheng didn't expect the cave to be so deep.
But he maintained a certain amount of patience …
After all, he was different from the people below. These people were attracted by the Heavenly Anomaly after the reincarnation of the Stone Forest Rat. They did not know what was here.
Luo Zheng's purpose was very clear.
After the time it took for three incense sticks to burn, the path became wider and wider.
As Luo Zheng was descending, his body accidentally brushed against the wall of the cave. His eyelids twitched slightly. He was afraid that the stone on the wall would loosen and roll down, alarming those people.
Unexpectedly, when he touched the wall of the cave, he found that it was rather soft.
"This is … the Muscle Stone Layer?" Luo Zheng was slightly surprised.
Speaking of which, he had followed this group of people all the way down and didn't know how deep they had gone. He was afraid that they had already passed through the Gangue Stone Layer and the Flint Stone Layer.
"Below the Muscle Stone Layer is the Black Fire Torrent. That thing can even burn a Sage to death." Thinking of this, Luo Zheng secretly became vigilant.
Just as he was thinking this, a discussion came from below.
"We've actually reached the Muscle Stone Layer. I think … we should go back?"
"This Black Fire Torrent is not something we can resist!"
Some people already had the thought of turning back. After all, it was really too dangerous to continue going down.
"We've already reached such a deep place! Kun Ao, to think that you're a Perfect True God! "someone said softly.
"Yes, we've already reached the Muscle Stone Layer. I reckon we're almost at the end. We're just one step away. If we go back now, all our previous efforts will be wasted," Patriarch Qianlong said.
The Perfect True God called Kun Ao frowned and didn't say anything in the end.
But at this moment, a gust of hot wind blew out from the depths of the cave.
"Wu —"
The hot wind spewed along the cave, making a sound like a flute.
"Where did this wind come from!"
"Let's go down and take a look!"
After these people felt the hot wind, they felt it was strange and sped up their footsteps.
"Wu —"
When the hot wind rolled in front of Luo Zheng, Luo Zheng's nose sniffed slightly.
"This smell …"
In addition to the heavy smell of sulfur, there was a faint sweet smell in the hot wind. He couldn't tell where the sweet smell came from.
But at this moment, there was a cry of surprise from below.
"Be careful!"
"There's something coming out from the Muscle Stone Layer!"
"It's the legendary Earth Ghost!"
"What the hell? Why would we bump into such a thing here?"
Suddenly, there was a cry of panic from below.
Hearing this, Luo Zheng's figure swept past lightly and found a corner in a cave to hide. Then, he stuck his head out to take a look.
He saw that in the Muscle Stone Layer, which had turned into a cave wall, there were four-horned black snakes that were as thick as an arm.
One had to know that the Muscle Stone Layer was the toughest soil layer in the Divine Territory. Even an upper True God couldn't open the Muscle Stone Layer, and even a Paragon would have to expend a lot of effort to tear it open.
A creature that could survive in the Muscle Stone Layer was definitely not an ordinary creature!
There were records of Earth Ghosts in the Divine Territory's history …
Earth Ghosts were the only creatures that could crawl freely in the Muscle Stone Layer. Because they were located very deep underground, there were very few records of Earth Ghosts.
It was said that most True Gods who had seen Earth Ghosts were already hibernating underground, adding a hint of mystery to this creature.
However, Luo Zheng didn't act rashly. After all, the target of these Earth Ghosts wasn't him.
Unless there was no other choice, he wouldn't show himself.
"Hiss hiss hiss …"
These Earth Ghosts coiled their tails on the wall, arched their snake bodies, and kept flicking their tongues. They weren't in a hurry to attack.
"What are these Earth Ghosts doing?"
"They don't seem to have any evil intentions?"
"I, I … Let's go back!" A skinny upper True God said with a sobbing tone.
After this upper True God suggested this, Kun Ao said firmly, "It's still not too late to turn back now. These Earth Ghosts should be guarding something, but no matter what it is, it's not something we can touch with our abilities. We can sell this information to the wealthy families and let them explore it!"
However, just as Kun Ao finished speaking, the entire cave began to tremble. The cave above their heads, which was the path they came from, was constantly squirming, and it was completely sealed!
Luo Zheng was originally hiding in the corner of the cave. Seeing that his hiding place was constantly narrowing, he had no choice but to jump out of his hiding place.
If he didn't jump out, he might be completely trapped in the Muscle Stone Layer!

Chapter 2300
Patriarch Qianlong's group was originally on guard against the surrounding four-legged earth ghosts.
However, the cave above their heads suddenly closed. Before it closed, a person jumped out, giving them a fright.
After taking a closer look, he realized that this person was actually a Perfected True God. This was simply too rare …
"Who are you?! How dare you tail us! "Patriarch Qianlong stared at Luo Zheng and shouted.
As a quasi-sage, Patriarch Qianlong's soul had already cultivated to the Yang God level. His voice was full of soul pressure, and it went straight for Luo Zheng.
Patriarch Qianlong didn't expect this shout to seriously injure Luo Zheng, but at least it would make him dizzy!
However, Luo Zheng stood in place and didn't react.
Seeing Luo Zheng like this, Patriarch Qianlong's eyes showed a hint of fear.
If they were on the ground, Patriarch Qianlong might have attacked without saying a word.
However, everyone was in danger now. In addition, there were many four-legged earth ghosts clinging to the cave walls. In addition, Luo Zheng didn't look simple, so it wasn't convenient for Patriarch Qianlong to attack.
Earlier, Patriarch Qianlong had sensed that someone was following them outside the cave. At that time, he thought it was just an illusion.
"This doesn't count as tailing you, right? It can only be said that you were one step ahead of me, "Luo Zheng replied calmly.
"You're lying! I discovered this cave long ago and used an illusion formation to cover it. You clearly followed us here! "said the upper-level True God called Lu Tianyi coldly.
After Lu Tianyi said that, Patriarch Qianlong and the other True Gods' expressions became more and more unfriendly.
Luo Zheng shrugged and didn't argue.
He naturally knew that this cave had something to do with the Stone Forest Rats.
The first ones to discover this place's abnormality were him and Yu Taibai.
However, he naturally wouldn't mention this to these people.
"Don't bother with this kid. This cave is too strange. It actually sealed us inside. It's better for us to leave first!" Kun Ao said as he stared at the earth ghosts.
After the cave was sealed, the earth ghosts began to stir …
Patriarch Qianlong took a deep look at Luo Zheng, and then looked at the deepest part of the cave reluctantly.
The situation in front of him was indeed too complicated. It was impossible for him to fight with this kid who had appeared out of nowhere. For the sake of safety, Patriarch Qian Long nodded. "Let's leave first before we discuss further …"
In the Deity Domain, when Paragons and Sages used greater teleportation, they would rarely teleport underground because it was too dangerous.
The entire depths of the Divine Realm were filled with the Black Flame Torrent. If one didn't control the distance to move underground, they would fall into the Black Flame Torrent and be swallowed up.
However, Greater Teleportation underground was extremely simple. One Greater Teleportation would allow one to move to the surface.
One Secondary Saint and two Great Perfection Saints were enough to bring the other ten people away.
Now that Patriarch Qianlong was finally willing to leave, Kun Ao breathed a sigh of relief. With a thought, he began to construct a spatial tunnel.
But at this moment, a strange scene appeared.
In front of Kun Ao, there were no spatial fluctuations!
"Greater Teleportation can't be used!" Kun Ao's expression turned abnormally ugly.
The other upper true gods were even more nervous as they looked at Patriarch Qianlong. After all, Patriarch Qianlong was a Secondary Saint. He might be their last hope.
Patriarch Qianlong waved his hand lightly, but there were still no spatial fluctuations …
"This is impossible …" Patriarch Qianlong's expression turned solemn.
In some places in the Deity Domain, spatial laws could indeed be banned.
For example, in the Sea of Time, Ruo Mu, where the Sword Race resided, and some forbidden areas couldn't use greater teleportation.
However, spatial laws were banned in the Sea of Time because of the rules of the Governing Slate. Furthermore, it only restricted Paragons and Sages, not Secondary Saints and Sages.
As for the Sword Race, greater teleportation was banned when Elder Yuan borrowed Ruo Mu to transform the entire Deity Domain into a "non-offensive area."
Banning spatial laws didn't mean they were ineffective …
Patriarch Qianlong and Kun Ao couldn't use spatial laws at all. It was as if spatial laws didn't exist in this cave!
This subverted their understanding. As far as they knew, such a place shouldn't exist in the Deity Domain.
At this moment, the densely packed Four-legged Earth Ghosts that were clinging to the cave walls suddenly rushed toward them.
Hiss hiss hiss hiss hiss …
Patriarch Qianlong's eyes flashed coldly as he faced the Four-legged Earth Ghosts. Five-Colored Dao Accumulations spread out from his hands.
Seeing the Five-Colored Dao Accumulations, Luo Zheng raised his eyebrows slightly. "Five Elements Divine Dao. What a stable Dao Accumulations. As expected of a Secondary Saint!"
The two Five-Colored Dao Accumulations converged and formed a huge strangling force!
Crack crack crack crack …
The hundreds of Four-legged Earth Ghosts at the front were kneaded together by this strangling force as if they were a huge black dough.
If a group of True Gods were kneaded by this force, they would probably be kneaded into a pile of flesh and bones in an instant.
However, after these Four-legged Earth Ghouls were rubbed for a while, they crashed heavily into the cave wall and scattered. The remaining Four-legged Earth Ghouls were unscathed. After rolling a few times on the ground, they pounced at them again!
Hiss hiss hiss …
Seeing this scene, everyone's expression changed.
Even a Secondary Saint was unable to kill these Four-legged Earth Ghosts, let alone others!
"Let's go! We can only run deeper! "Patriarch Qianlong waved his hand and said.
Now, they had no way out. They indeed had no other choice but to move forward.
Whoosh whoosh whoosh whoosh …
Kun Ao, Lu Tianyi, and the others took the lead and fled downward.
Seeing that the group of True Gods leading the way had run away, Luo Zheng also stepped forward and followed closely behind.
However, just as he rushed over, dozens of Four-legged Earth Ghosts pounced on him …
"Eight Winding Smoke!"
With a slight thought, Luo Zheng's body became light as a feather, and he drilled through the gaps between these Four-legged Earth Ghosts in an extremely natural manner.
"Ah!"
"These damn things!"
At this moment, two upper True Gods in front of Luo Zheng had already been surrounded by Four-legged Earth Ghosts. One of them was Lu Tianyi, who had set up the illusion formation.
Under the frenzied biting of the Four-legged Earth Ghosts, the two upper True Gods were instantly reduced to a bloody mess. It was a tragic sight!
"Save me!" Lu Tianyi roared crazily.
The other upper True God had long since stopped breathing. Half of his head had been eaten clean by the Four-legged Earth Ghosts. Clearly, there was no hope for him.
Although Lu Tianyi was shouting for help, the upper True Gods in front of him were all fleeing for their lives. Who would dare to turn back?
As for Patriarch Qianlong, Kun Ao, and the others, they had no intention of lending a helping hand.
Seeing Lu Tianyi's mournful screams, Luo Zheng frowned slightly. His figure had already fluttered in front of Lu Tianyi. At the same time, he gathered energy in his palm and slammed it heavily on Lu Tianyi's chest!
Pfff!
This skillful energy poured into Lu Tianyi's body, but it did not harm him.
As Lu Tianyi's body suddenly shook, the skillful energy spread out along the surface of his body, shaking off the Four-legged Earth Ghosts on his body.
